prayacchetām snātum icchāmi pārthiva brāhmaṇāḥ eva jāyeran na anyaḥ varṇaḥ katham cana īdṛśam ratham āruhya kaḥ anyaḥ jīvitum utsahet rathena vāhitaḥ ca api śramāt krodha udbhavāya te sa ca api bhṛgu śārdūlaḥ tam vedam dhārayiṣyati nārāyaṇam upāsya asya varāt tam putram icchati pustakam ca tathā gāvaḥ kanyā dānam tathā eva ca ye na dāsyanti puruṣāḥ teṣām saukhyam pareṇa kim a pūjyamānāḥ kauravya pūjā arhāḥ te tathāvidhāḥ yat kim cit pāṇḍaram puṣpam yat kim cit anulepanam alakṣmī parihārāya nityam deyam yudhiṣṭhira nitya anuṣṭhāna yuktāḥ ced pratigṛhṇanti sādhavaḥ yājakāḥ dvijasattamāḥ dhane satya pradātṝṇām vidvadbhyaḥ saṃpradānena tatra api aṃśaḥ asya pūjayā pradadyāt jñāna dātṝṇām jñāna dāna aṃśa bhāj bhavet śriyā hi abhīkṣṇam saṃvāsaḥ darpayet saṃpramohayet brāhmaṇeṣu pramūḍheṣu dharmaḥ vipraṇaśet dhruvam dharma praṇāśe bhūtānām abhāvaḥ syāt na saṃśayaḥ yaḥ rakṣibhyaḥ saṃpradāya rājā rāṣṭram vilumpati yakṣye rāṣṭrāt dhanam tasmāt ānayadhvam iti bruvan yat ca ādāya tat ājñaptam bhītam dattam su dāruṇam yajet rājā na tam yajñam praśaṃsanti asya sādhavaḥ apīḍitāḥ su saṃvṛddhāḥ ye dadati anukūlataḥ tādṛśena abhyupāyena yaṣṭavyam na udyama āhṛtaiḥ tadā rājā mahā yajñaiḥ yajeta bahu dakṣiṇaiḥ na khāta pūrvam kurvīta na rudantī dhanam haret hṛtam kṛpaṇa vittam hi rāṣṭram hanti nṛpa śriyam yeṣām svādūni bhojyāni samavekṣyanti bālakāḥ na aśnanti vidhivat tāni kim nu pāpataram tatas yadi te tādṛśaḥ rāṣṭre vidvān sīdet kṣudhā dvijaḥ bhrūṇahatyām ca gacchethāḥ kṛtvā pāpam iva uttamam dhik tasya jīvitam rājñaḥ rāṣṭre yasya avasīdati yasya sma viṣaye rājñaḥ snātakaḥ sīdati kṣudhā a vṛddhim eti tat rāṣṭram vindate saha rājakam krośantyaḥ yasya vai rāṣṭrāt dhriyante tarasā striyaḥ krośatām pati putrāṇām mṛtaḥ asau na sa jīvati yaḥ etām viduṣe dadyāt pṛthivīm pṛthivīpatiḥ pṛthivyām etat iṣṭam sa rājyāt rājyam iti vrajet punar ca jananam prāpya rājā eva syāt na saṃśayaḥ eka āhāra karīm dattvā ṣaṣṭi sāhasram ūrdhva gaḥ tāvati āharaṇe hṛtvā narakam dviguṇa uttaram daśa hastena vaṃśena daśa vaṃśān samantataḥ sauvarṇāḥ yatra prāsādāḥ vasoḥ dhārāḥ ca kāma dāḥ vidyā dānam ca kanyānām dānam pāpa haram param brāhmaṇa arthe gavā arthe vā rāṣṭra ghāte atha svāminaḥ kulastrīṇām paribhave mṛtāḥ te bhūmi daiḥ saha sva nirjitām svayam krītām bhṛtām ca vā arpayet nṛpa su nirmitām su vikrītām subhṛtām vā api yat nṛpaḥ hṛte kṣetre daridrāṇām aśru pātaḥ su dāruṇaḥ iṣṭāpūrtam narasya asya hanyāt tri puruṣam kulam upatiṣṭhanti bhūtāni āśaya abhyāgatam vipram bhojana artham upasthitam tāmrāṇi tila pātrāṇi triṃśat pala mitāni tu tām prāpnuvanti gām dattvā vimuktāḥ sarva kilbiṣaiḥ vaiśākhyām paurṇamāsyām tu tilān dadyāt dvijātiṣu teṣām māghyām pradānena sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt pānīya abhyarthinam dṛṣṭvā prītyā dadyāt tvarā anvitaḥ gavām loma pramāṇena svarga bhogam upāśnute saṃtāḍyā na tu pādena gavām madhye ca na vrajet arthasya daśa bhāj dātā tat nivṛtta pravṛttaye yudhiṣṭhiraḥ uvāca brāhmaṇa sva apahāreṇa kiyān doṣaḥ bhavati uta paritrāyanti śaktāḥ tu namaḥ tebhyaḥ mṛtāḥ tu vā tathā gavām paritrāṇe pīḍane ca śubha aśubhe vipra goṣu viśeṣeṇa rakṣiteṣu gṛheṣu vā kṣīra sravāḥ vai saritaḥ girīn ca sarpiṣām tathā gāḥ vai dadyāt nyāya bhūtāḥ dvijebhyaḥ yaḥ yātum ākāṅkṣati deva lokam rājasūya aśvamedhābhyām phalam prāpnoti mānavaḥ kāntāram yaḥ prapannaḥ tu gām dvijāya prayacchati kṣemeṇa pratimucyeta tam adhvānam na saṃśayaḥ yānapātram samārohan yaḥ ca gām saṃprayacchati tīrtvā samudram kṣemeṇa pratyāyāti gṛham gṛhī mṛtyu kāle sahasrākṣa yaḥ ca gām saṃprayacchati mṛtyum sukham sa labhate lokān ca āpnoti anuttamān gobhiḥ ca samanudhyātaḥ jitvā śatrūn mahīyate yam yam kāmam samuddiśya gām dvijāya prayacchati dānāt damaḥ viśiṣṭaḥ hi dānam kim cit dvijātaye vidyā dānāt param nā asti veda vidyā mahā phalā śūrāṇām ca api nirdiṣṭam phalam ca niyatam śṛṇu tatas prajāsu sṛṣṭāsu dakṣa ādyaiḥ kṣudhitāḥ prajāḥ mukha jā sā asṛjat dhātuḥ surabhim lokamātaram atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam vasiṣṭhasya ca saṃvādam saudāsasya mahātmanaḥ gāvaḥ bhaviṣyam bhūtam ca goṣu yajñāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ prātar namasyāḥ tāḥ gāvaḥ tatas puṣṭim avāpnuyāt lokān anantān samavāpya dhīraḥ saurabhyaḥ puṇya karmāṇaḥ pāvakāḥ śubha darśanāḥ tāḥ kila śṛṅgam ākāṅkṣan varṣa ātapa nivāraṇam mānuṣīm ca tvacam gurvīm śīta vāta ātapa asahām tatas brahmā dadau tāsām manasā dhāritām parām āhūya manujam prāha goṣu svām tanum ādadhaḥ ācchādanam ca āvaraṇam yūyam gṛhṇīta mānavāḥ śīta vāta ātapam yena dhārayanti prajā arthinaḥ sukumāra tvacam yūyam vastraiḥ ācchādayiṣyatha evam astu iti tāḥ prādāt tvacam svām carma eva ca phalāni ca rasa āḍhyāni mṛdūni rucirāṇi ca yudhiṣṭhiraḥ surāṇām asurāṇām ca bhūtānām ca pitāmaha prabhuḥ sraṣṭā ca bhagavān munibhiḥ stūyate vibhuḥ tasya upari katham hi eṣa golokaḥ sthāna tām gataḥ saṃśayaḥ me mahān eṣa tat me vyākhyātum arhasi bhīṣmaḥ tatas me śṛṇu kārtsnyena go mahābhāgyam uttamam puṇyam yaśasyam āyuṣyam tathā svastyayanam mahat kīrtiḥ viharatām loke gavām yogeṣu bhaktimān śrūyate hi purāṇeṣu mahā ṛṣīṇām mahātmanām saṃsthāne sarva lokānām devānām ca api saṃbhave surabhiḥ nāma vikhyātā rohiṇī kāmarūpiṇī saṃkalpya manasā pūrvam rohiṇī hi amṛta ānanā ghoram tapaḥ samāsthāya nirmitāḥ viśvakarmaṇā puruṣam ca asṛjat bhūyas tejasā tapasā ca ha dedīpyamānam vapuṣā samiddham iva pāvakam saḥ apaśyat divya rūpām tām surabhim rohiṇīm tadā dṛṣṭvā eva ca ati vimanāḥ saḥ abhavat kāma mohitaḥ tam kāma ārtam atho jñātvā svayaṃbhūḥ loka bhāvanaḥ mārtaḥ bhava tathā ca eṣa bhagavān abhyabhāṣata tatas sa bhagavān tatra mārtāṇḍaḥ iti viśrutaḥ cakāra nāma tam dṛṣṭvā tasya ārtībhāvam uttamam saḥ adadāt bhagavān tasmai mārtāṇḍāya mahātmane surūpām surabhim kanyām tapaḥ tejaḥ mayīm śubhām yathā mayā eṣa ca udbhūtaḥ tvam ca eva īṣā ca rohiṇī prajā bhaviṣyate puṇyā pavitram paramam ca vām tvad prajā saṃbhavam kṣīram bhaviṣyati param haviḥ yajñeṣu ca ājyabhāgānām tvad prajā mūla jaḥ vidhiḥ prajā śuśrūṣavaḥ ca eva ye bhaviṣyanti rohiṇi tava tena eva puṇyena go lokam yāntu mānavāḥ idam pavitram paramam ṛṣabham nāma karhi cit yat vai jñātvā dvijāḥ loke mokṣyante yoni saṃkarāt etad kriyāḥ prapatsyante mantra brāhmaṇa saṃskṛtāḥ devatānām pitṝṇām ca havya kavya purogamāḥ yugaṃdharāḥ ca te putrāḥ santu lokasya dhāraṇe yān yān kāmayase lokān tān lokān anuyāsyasi evam etān varān sarvān agṛhṇāt surabhiḥ tadā bruvataḥ sarva loka īśān nirvṛtim ca agamat parām sṛṣṭvā prajāḥ ca vipulāḥ loka saṃdhāraṇāya vai brahmaṇā samanujñātā surabhiḥ lokam āviśat upariṣṭāt gavām lokam proktam te sarvam āditas yudhiṣṭhiraḥ surabheḥ kāḥ prajāḥ pūrvam mārtāṇḍāt abhavan purā bhīṣmaḥ śṛṇu nāmāni divyāni gomātṝṇām viśeṣataḥ yābhiḥ vyāptāḥ trayaḥ lokāḥ kalyāṇābhiḥ janādhipa surabhyaḥ prathama udbhūtāḥ yāḥ ca syuḥ prathamāḥ prajāḥ mayā ucyamānāḥ śṛṇu tāḥ prāpsyase vipulam yaśaḥ suṣāva ekādaśa sutān rudrāḥ ye chandasi stutāḥ vṛṣākapiḥ ca śaṃbhuḥ ca kapālī raivataḥ tathā tasya ca eva ātmajaḥ śrīmān viśvarūpaḥ mahā yaśāḥ ekādaśā ete kathitāḥ rudrāḥ te nāma nāmataḥ mahātmānaḥ mahā yogāḥ tejaḥ yuktāḥ mahā balāḥ ete variṣṭha janmānaḥ devānām brahma vādinām viprāṇām prakṛtiḥ loke ete eva hi viśrutāḥ ete ekādaśa proktāḥ rudrāḥ tribhuvana īśvarāḥ śatam tu etat samākhyātam śatarudram mahātmanām suṣuve prathamām kanyām surabhiḥ pṛthivīm tadā viśva kāmadughā dhenuḥ yā dhārayati dehinaḥ sutam go brāhmaṇam rājan ekam iti abhidhīyate sṛṣṭvā tu prathamam rudrān vara dān brahma saṃbhavān oṣadhīnām rasānām ca devānām jīvitasya ca tatas śriyam ca medhām ca kīrtim devīm sarasvatīm catasraḥ suṣuve kanyāḥ yogeṣu niyatāḥ sthitāḥ etāḥ sṛṣṭvā prajāḥ eṣā surabhiḥ kāmarūpiṇī suṣuve caramam bhūyas divyāḥ gomātaraḥ śubhāḥ puṇyām māyām madhuścyotām śivām śīghrām sarit varām hiraṇya varṇām subhagām gavyām pṛśnīm kuthāvatīm aṅgāvatīm ghṛtavatīm dadhi kṣīra payovatīm amoghām su rasām satyām revatīm mārutīm rasām ajām ca sikatām ca eva śuddha dhūmām a dhāriṇīm jīvām prāṇavatīm dhanyām śuddhām dhenum dhana āvahām indrām ṛddhim ca śāntim ca śānta pāpām sarit varām catvāriṃśatam ekām ca dhanyāḥ tāḥ divi pūjitāḥ bhūyas jajñe surabhyāḥ ca śrīmān candra aṃśu saprabhaḥ vṛṣaḥ dakṣaḥ iti khyātaḥ kaṇṭhe maṇi tala prabhaḥ sragvī kakudmān dyutimān mṛṇāla sadṛśa prabhaḥ surabhyaḥ kāmarūpiṇyaḥ gāvaḥ puṇya artham uttamāḥ yā dattā lokapālānām indra ādīnām yudhiṣṭhira suṣṭutām kapilām ca eva rohiṇīm ca yaśasvinīm sarva kāma dughām ca eva marutām kāmarūpiṇīm gāvaḥ mṛṣṭadughāḥ hi etāḥ catasraḥ amṛta saṃskṛtāḥ yāsām bhūtvā purā vatsāḥ pibanti amṛtam uttamam suṣṭutām devarājāya vāsavāya mahātmane kapilām dharmarājāya varuṇāya ca rohiṇīm sarva kāma dughām dhenum rājñe vaiśravaṇāya ca iti etāḥ lokam ahitāḥ viśrutāḥ surabheḥ prajāḥ etāsām prajayā pūrṇā pṛthivī muni puṃgava yudhiṣṭhiraḥ kīrtitam brūhi rāja indra kim bhūyas kathayāmi te surabhyāḥ tu tadā devyāḥ kīrtiḥ lakṣmīḥ sarasvatī medhā ca pravarā devī yāḥ catasraḥ abhiviśrutāḥ pṛthak gobhyaḥ kim etāḥ syuḥ uta aho goṣu saṃśritāḥ bhīṣmaḥ devāḥ ke vā āśritāḥ goṣu tat me brūhi pitāmaha yam devam saṃśritāḥ gāvaḥ tam devam deva saṃjñitam yat yat deva āśritam daivam tat tat daivam dvijāḥ viduḥ sarveṣām eva devānām pūrvam kila samudbhave indraḥ bhīṣmaḥ puraṃdaraḥ amṛtam pītvā prahṛṣṭaḥ samupasthitaḥ putraḥ aham tava bhadram te brūhi kim karavāṇi te kṛtam putra tvayā sarvam upayāhi triviṣṭapam pālayasva surān sarvān jahi ye sura śatravaḥ na ca go brāhmaṇe avajñā kāryā te śāntim icchatā go brāhmaṇasya niḥśvāsaḥ śoṣayet api devatāḥ pṛthivyām antarikṣe ca nāka pṛṣṭhe ca vikramet yat ca te anyat bhavet kṛtyam tat me brūyāḥ samāsatas indraḥ ebhiḥ sura gaṇaiḥ sārdham mama anugraham ācara gavām śarīram pratyakṣam etat kauśika lakṣaye sarvam pavitram paramam gavām gātram su pūjitam bhīṣmaḥ tasyāḥ tat vacanam śrutvā surabhyāḥ sura sattamaḥ śṛṅge vaktre ca jihvāyām devarājaḥ samāviśat sarva chidreṣu pavanaḥ pādeṣu marutām gaṇāḥ kakudam sarvagaḥ rudraḥ kukṣau ca eva hutāśanaḥ sarasvatī staneṣu agryā śrīḥ purīṣe jagat priyā mūtre kīrtiḥ su mahatī medhā payasi śāśvatī dharmaḥ pucche kriyā lomni bhāskaraḥ cakṣuṣī śritaḥ siddhāḥ saṃdhiṣu siddhiḥ ca tapaḥ tejaḥ ca ceṣṭane mahatī devatā gāvaḥ brāhmaṇaiḥ parisaṃskṛtāḥ gām āśrayanti sahitāḥ devāḥ hi prabhaviṣṇavaḥ bhāvataḥ parayā bhaktyā pūjayasva nareśvara gāvaḥ tu paramam loke pavitram pāvanam haviḥ nipātya bhakṣitaḥ svargāt bhārgavaḥ phenapaḥ kila sa ca prāṇān punar labdhvā tatas golokam āśritaḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ kaḥ phenapa iti nāmnā asau katham vā bhakṣitaḥ purā mṛtaḥ ujjīvitaḥ kasmāt katham go lokam āśritaḥ ṛte daivam hi duṣprāpam mānuṣeṣu viśeṣataḥ bhīṣmaḥ śrūyate bhārgave vaṃśe sumitraḥ nāma bhārata pitṝn devān ca niyatam atithīn ca eva pūjayan prāṇa saṃdhāraṇa artham ca yat kim cit upahārayan kula hā iti purāṇeṣu viśrutā rudra nirmitā tasyāḥ tīre same deśe puṣpa mālā samākule vanya oṣadhi druma upete nānā pakṣi mṛga āyute vyapeta daṃśa maśake dhvāṅkṣa gṛdhraiḥ a sevite kṛṣṇa darbha tṛṇa prāye su ramye jyotiḥ raśmini sarva unnataiḥ samaiḥ śyāmaiḥ yājñīyaiḥ tarubhiḥ vṛte tatra āśrama padam puṇyam bhṛgūṇām abhavat purā yathā uddiṣṭena pūrveṣām bhṛgūṇām sādhu vartmanām tasmai āṅgirasaḥ kaḥ cit dadau gām śarkarīm śubhām varṣāsu paścime māsi paurṇamāsyām śuci vrataḥ sa tām labdhvā dharma śīlaḥ cintayām āsa tatparaḥ tena saṃdhukṣyamāṇā sā rohiṇī kāma cāriṇī vivṛddhim agamat śreṣṭhā prāṇataḥ ca su darśanā sirā vimukta pārśva antā vipulām kāntim udvahat śyāma pārśva anta pṛṣṭha antā surabhiḥ madhu piṅgalā bṛhatī sūkṣma roma antā rūpa udagrā tanu tvacā kṛṣṇa pucchā śveta vaktrā sama vṛtta payodharā pṛṣṭha unnatā pūrva natā śaṅku karṇī su locanā dīrgha jihvā hrasva śṛṅgī saṃpūrṇa daśana antarā māṃsa adhika gala antā sā prasannā śubha darśanā nityam śama yutā snigdhā saṃpūrṇa udātta nisvanā prājāpatyaiḥ gavām nityam praśastaiḥ lakṣaṇaiḥ yutā yauvana sthā iva vanitā śuśubhe rūpa śobhayā vṛṣeṇa upagatā sā tu kalyā madhura darśanā mithunam janayām āsa tulya rūpam iva ātmanaḥ saṃvardhayām āsa sa tām sa vatsām bhārgavaḥ ṛṣiḥ tayoḥ prajā adhi saṃsargāt sahasram ca gavām abhūt gavām jāti sahasrāṇi saṃbhūtāni parasparam ṛṣabhāṇām ca rāja indra na eva antar pratidṛśyate taiḥ āśrama padam ramyam araṇyam ca eva sarvaśas kāni cit padma varṇāni kiṃśuka ābhāni kāni cit rukma varṇāni ca anyāni candra aṃśu sadṛśāni ca tathā rājata varṇāni kāni cit lohitāni vai nīla lohita tāmrāṇi kṛṣṇāni kapilāni ca nānā rāga vicitrāṇi yūthāni bahulāni ca na ca kṣīram ghṛtam snehāt vatsānām upajīvati bhārgavaḥ kevalam ca āsīt gavām prāṇāyane rataḥ tathā śuśrūṣataḥ tasya gavām hitam avekṣataḥ vyatīyāt su mahān kālaḥ vatsa ucchiṣṭena vartataḥ kṣudh pipāsā pariśrāntaḥ satatam prasnavam gavām vatsaiḥ ucchiṣṭam uditam bahu kṣīra tayā bahu pītavān tena nāma asya phenapa iti abhiviśrutam bhīṣmaḥ kadā cit kāmarūpiṇyaḥ gāvaḥ strī veṣam āsthitāḥ hrade krīḍanti saṃhṛṣṭāḥ gāyantyaḥ puṇya lakṣaṇāḥ ūcuḥ ca kāḥ yūyam iti striyaḥ mānuṣayā girā gāvaḥ eva vayam sarvāḥ karmabhiḥ śobhanaiḥ yutāḥ sarvāḥ strī veṣa dhāriṇyaḥ yathākāmam carāmahe gāvaḥ gavām gāvaḥ param daivam gavām gāvaḥ parā gatiḥ kathayadhvam iha asmākam kena vaḥ sukṛtām gatiḥ asmākam haviṣā devāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ tarpitāḥ tathā prajayā ca tathā asmākam kṛṣiḥ abhyuddhṛtā sadā śakaṭaiḥ ca api saṃyuktāḥ daśa vāha śatena vai tat etaiḥ sukṛtaiḥ sphītaiḥ vayam yāḥ ca eva naḥ prajāḥ go lokam anusaṃprāptāḥ yaḥ paraḥ kāma gocaraḥ yūyam tu sarvāḥ rohiṇyaḥ sa prajāḥ saha puṃgavāḥ gāvaḥ adhogāminyaḥ iti eva paśyāmaḥ divya cakṣuṣā evam gavām param daivam gāvaḥ eva parāyaṇam sva pakṣyāḥ tāraṇīyāḥ vaḥ śaraṇāya gatāḥ vayam kim asmābhiḥ karaṇīyam vartitavyam katham cana prāpnuyāma ca go lokam bhavāma na ca garhitāḥ tatas tasya upayogena paśu tve yajña saṃskṛtāḥ bhīṣmaḥ go lokān prāpsyatha śubhān tena puṇyena saṃyutāḥ etat tāsām vacaḥ śrutvā gavām saṃhṛṣṭa mānasāḥ vayam na ca ananujñātāḥ śaktāḥ gantum katham cana lokān prāptum saha asmābhiḥ niścayaḥ kriyatām ayam na tu tāsām sametānām kā cit ghoreṇa cakṣuṣā śaknoti bhārgavam draṣṭum satkṛtena upasaṃyutā atha padma savarṇa ābhāḥ bhāskara aṃśu sama prabhāḥ japā lohita tāmra akṣyaḥ nirmāṃsa kaṭhina ānanāḥ rohiṇyaḥ kapilāḥ prāhuḥ sarvāsām vai samakṣataḥ vayam hi tam vadhiṣyāmaḥ sumitram na atra saṃśayaḥ gāvaḥ sukṛtam pṛṣṭhatas kṛtvā kim naḥ śreyaḥ vidhāsyatha bhīṣmaḥ gavām śata phalāt ca ekām dattvā phalam avāpsyati etat gavām vacaḥ śrutvā kapilāḥ hṛṣṭa mānasāḥ bhīṣmaḥ yāḥ tu go mātaraḥ tasya kāmacāriṇyaḥ āgatāḥ samīpam hi sumitrasya kṛtajñāḥ samupasthitāḥ abhipraśasya ca eva āhuḥ tam ṛṣim puṇya darśanāḥ go lokāt āgatāḥ veda ṛṣe go mātaraḥ vayam su prītāḥ sma varam gṛhṇa yam icchasi mahā mune yat hi goṣu parām buddhim kṛtavān asi nityadā prītaḥ asmi anugṛhītaḥ asmi yat mām go mātaraḥ śubhāḥ su prīta manasaḥ sarvāḥ tiṣṭhante ca vara pradāḥ go mātaraḥ go ghnāḥ ca eva avasīdantu narāḥ brahma dviṣaḥ ca ye evam etat ṛṣi śreṣṭha hitam vadasi naḥ priyam yūyam iṣṭām gatim yāntu na hi aham gantum utsahe bhīṣmaḥ imāḥ gāvaḥ samutsṛjya tapasvinyaḥ mama priyāḥ tāḥ tu tasya vacaḥ śrutvā kapilānām su dāruṇam niṣkṛṣya karaṇam yogāt anayan bhārgavasya vai sarvam ca asya tat ācakhyuḥ kapilānām viceṣṭitam yad artham haraṇam gobhiḥ golokam loka mātaraḥ yathāpratijñam śṛṅgaiḥ ca khuraiḥ ca api avacūrṇayan atha go mātṛbhiḥ śaptāḥ tāḥ gāvaḥ pṛthivī carāḥ amedhya vadanāḥ kṣipram bhavadhvam brahma ghātakāḥ evam kṛtajñāḥ gāvaḥ hi yathā go mātaraḥ nṛpa ṛṣiḥ ca prāptavān lokam gāvaḥ ca parimokṣitāḥ bhīṣmaḥ tāḥ gāvaḥ rantidevasya gatvā yajñam manīṣiṇaḥ ātmānam jñāpayām āsuḥ mahā ṛṣīṇām samakṣataḥ rantidevaḥ tatas rājā prayataḥ prāñjaliḥ śuciḥ uvāca gāvaḥ praṇataḥ kim āgamanam iti api gāvaḥ na asmi śaktaḥ gavām ghātam kartum śata sahasraśas ghātayitvā tu aham yuṣmān katham ātmānam uddhare gāvaḥ ātmānam ca eva tapasā gāvaḥ samupagamyatām asmākam tāraṇe yuktaḥ dharma ātmā tapasi sthitaḥ śrutaḥ asmābhiḥ bhavān rājan tatas tu svayam āgatāḥ samayena aham etena juhuyām vaḥ hutāśane gāvaḥ tadā samāptiḥ satrasya gavām syāt iti naiṣṭhikī evam astu mahā rāja yathā tvam prabravīṣi naḥ akāmāḥ syuḥ yadā gāvaḥ tadā satram samāpyatām bhīṣmaḥ evam bahūni varṣāṇi vyatītāni narādhipa gavām vai vadhyamānānām na ca antaḥ pratyadṛśyata gavām carma sahasraiḥ tu rāśayaḥ parvata upamāḥ babhūvuḥ kuru śārdūla bahudhā megha saṃnibhāḥ adyā api bhuvi vikhyātā nadī carmaṇvatī śubhā tatas kadā cit svam vatsam gauḥ upāmantrya duḥkhitā ehi vatsa stanam pāhi mā tvam paścāt kṣudhā arditaḥ tapsyase vimanāḥ duḥkham ghātitāyām mayi dhruvam ete hi āyānti caṇḍālāḥ sa śastrāḥ mām jighāṃsavaḥ atha śuśrāva tām vāṇīm mānuṣīm samudāhṛtām satra utsṛṣṭāḥ parityaktāḥ gāvaḥ anyāḥ samupāśritāḥ yāḥ tasya rājñaḥ nihatāḥ gāvaḥ yajñe mahātmanaḥ tāḥ go lokam upājagmuḥ prokṣitāḥ brahma vādibhiḥ rantidevaḥ api rājarṣiḥ iṣṭvā yajñam yathāvidhi phenapaḥ divi goloke mumude śāśvatīḥ samāḥ avaśiṣṭāḥ ca yāḥ gāvaḥ tāḥ babhūvuḥ vanecarāḥ phenapa ākhyānam etat te gavām māhātmyam eva ca kathitam pāvanam puṇyam kṛṣṇadvaipāyana īritam nārāyaṇaḥ api bhagavān dṛṣṭvā goṣu param yaśaḥ śuśrūṣām paramām cakre bhaktim ca bharata ṛṣabha yudhiṣṭhiraḥ pavitrāṇām pavitram yat śreṣṭham lokeṣu pūjitam bhīṣmaḥ mahāvratam mahābhāga tat me brūhi pitāmaha atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam pituḥ putrasya saṃvādam vyāsasya ca śukasya ca ṛṣīṇām uttamam kṛṣṇam bhāvitātmānam acyutam pāraṃparya viśeṣa jñam sarva śāstra artha kovidam kṛta śaucaḥ śukaḥ tatra kṛta japyaḥ kṛta āhnikaḥ param niyamam āsthāya param dharmam upāśritaḥ praṇamya śirasā vyāsam sūkṣma tattva artha darśinam śukaḥ papraccha vai praśnam dāna dharma kutūhalaḥ bahu citrāṇi dānāni bahuśas śaṃsase mune mahārtham pāvanam puṇyam kim svid dānam mahā phalam ke vā yajñasya voḍhāraḥ keṣu yajñaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ kim ca yajñasya yajña tvam kim ca yajñasya bheṣajam yajñānām uttamam kim ca tat bhavān prabravītu me dhanyam yaśasyam āyuṣyam loke śruti sukha āvaham yat pavitram pavitrāṇām maṅgalānām ca maṅgalam sarva pāpa praśamanam tat samāsena me śṛṇu gāvaḥ tāḥ prāpya tiṣṭhanti go loke puṇya darśanāḥ mātaraḥ sarva bhūtānām viśvasya jagataḥ ca ha rudrāṇām atha sādhyānām gāvaḥ eva tu mātaraḥ rudrāṇām mātaraḥ hi etāḥ ādityānām svasā smṛtāḥ vasūnām ca duhitryaḥ tāḥ brahma saṃtāna mūla jāḥ yāsām adhipatiḥ pūṣā marutaḥ vāla bandhanāḥ aiśvaryam varuṇaḥ rājā viśvedevāḥ samāśritāḥ yaḥ evam veda tāḥ gāvaḥ mātaraḥ deva pūjitāḥ sa vipraḥ brahma lokāya gavām lokāya vā dhruvaḥ gāvaḥ tu na avamanyeta karmaṇā manasā girā gavām sthānam param loke prārthayet yaḥ parām gatim na padbhyām tāḍayet gāvaḥ na daṇḍena na muṣṭinā imām vidyām upāśritya pāvanīm brahma nirmitām naraḥ mūtra purīṣasya dṛṣṭvā kuryāt visarjanam śuddhāḥ candana śīta aṅgyaḥ candra raśmi sama prabhāḥ divam ca eva antarikṣam ca gavām vyuṣṭim samaśnute dadhi dadyāt ca prāśeta gavām vyuṣṭim samaśnute ghṛtam ālabhya prāśnīyāt gavām vyuṣṭim samaśnute gāvaḥ saṃjīvanāḥ yāḥ tu gāvaḥ dānam anuttamam tāḥ puṇya gopāḥ su phalāḥ bhajamānam bhajantu mām tat pavitram pavitrāṇām mama mūrdhni pratiṣṭhitam vīṇā mṛdaṅga paṇavāḥ gavām gātram pratiṣṭhitāḥ krīḍā rati vihāra arthe triṣu lokeṣu vartate kṣīram dadhi ghṛtam yāsām rasānām uttamaḥ rasaḥ iḍām āhūya dhenum ca sa vatsām yajña mātaram upāhvayanti yam viprāḥ gāvaḥ yajña haviṣkṛtāḥ paurṇamāsena vatsena kāmam kāma guṇa anvitā sā gauḥ vai prathamā puṇyā sarva bhūta hite ratā dhāraṇāḥ pāvanāḥ puṇyāḥ bhāvanāḥ bhūta bhāvanāḥ eṣa yajñaḥ saha upāṅgaḥ eṣa yajñaḥ sanātanaḥ vedāḥ saha upaniṣadaḥ gavām rūpe pratiṣṭhitāḥ śrutvā labheta tān lokān ye mayā parikīrtitāḥ śrāvayitvā api prīta ātmā lokān tān pratipadyate dhenum ekām sadā dadyāt ahani ahani pāvanīm tat tathā prāpnuyāt vipraḥ paṭhan vai gomatīm sadā dadyāt gomati kalpena tām dhenum sarva pāvanīm āhnikam gomatīm nityam yaḥ paṭheta sadā naraḥ ghṛtam vā nityam ālabhya prāśya vā gomatīm japet snātvā vā go karīṣeṇa paṭhan pāpāt pramucyate gomatīm japamānāḥ hi devāḥ deva tvam āpnuvan ṛṣi tvam ṛṣayaḥ ca api gomatyā sarvam āpnuvan etat tu pṛthivīpāla pāvanam śṛṇvatām sadā putra kāmāḥ ca ye ke cit dhana kāmāḥ ca mānavāḥ pūrva vaira anubandheṣu raṇe ca api ātatāyinaḥ śukaḥ labhate jayam eva āśu sadā gomati pāṭhakaḥ kṣatriyāḥ ca eva śūdrāḥ ca mantra hīnāḥ ca ye dvijāḥ vyāsaḥ kṣatriyāḥ ca eva śūdrāḥ ca mantra hīnāḥ ca ye dvijāḥ kapilām upajīvanti teṣām vakṣyāmi nirṇayam kapilāḥ tu uttamāḥ loke goṣu ca eva uttamāḥ matāḥ tāsām dātā labhet svargam vidhinā yaḥ ca sevate spṛśeta kapilām yaḥ tu daṇḍena caraṇena vā mantra hīnam tu yaḥ yuñjyāt kṛmi yonau prasūyate prahāra hata marma aṅgā duḥkhena ca jaḍīkṛtā yāvat ca bindavaḥ tasyāḥ śoṇitasya kṣitim gatāḥ kapilām ye api jīvanti buddhi moha anvitāḥ narāḥ tasmin loke pramodante lokāḥ ca eṣām anāmayāḥ vidhinā ye na kurvanti śūdrān tān upadhāraya śukaḥ nānā varṇaiḥ upetānām gavām kim muni sattama vyāsaḥ kapilāḥ sarva varṇeṣu variṣṭha tvam avāpnuvan śṛṇu putra yathā goṣu variṣṭhāḥ kapilāḥ smṛtāḥ kapila tvam ca saṃprāptāḥ pūjyāḥ ca satatam nṛṣu devebhyaḥ mām chādayata śaraṇyāḥ śaraṇam gatam ūcuḥ tāḥ sahitāḥ tatra svāgatam tava pāvaka goṣu guptam ca vijñāya tāḥ kṣipram upatasthire adhigamya pāvakam tuṣṭāḥ te devāḥ sadyas eva tu agnim pracodayām āsuḥ kriyatām goṣu anugrahaḥ gavām tu yāsām gātreṣu pāvakaḥ samavasthitaḥ mahā phala tvam loke ca dadau tāsām hutāśanaḥ tasmāt hi sarva varṇānām kapilām gām pradāpaya śrotriyāya praśāntāya prayatāya agnihotriṇe yugāni tāvanti punāti dātṝn pratigrahītṝn ca punāti dattā śukaḥ vyāsaḥ kati vā lakṣaṇāni asyāḥ dṛṣṭāni munibhiḥ purā śṛṇu tāta yathā goṣu vijñeyā kapilā bhavet netrayoḥ śṛṅgayoḥ ca eva khureṣu vṛṣaṇeṣu ca karṇataḥ ghrāṇataḥ ca api ṣaḍvidhāḥ kapilāḥ smṛtāḥ eteṣām lakṣaṇānām tu yadi ekam api dṛśyate kapilām tām vijānīyāt evam āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ āgneyī netra kapilā khuraiḥ māheśvarī bhavet grīvāyām vaiṣṇavī jñeyā pūṣṇaḥ ghrāṇāt ajāyata gāyatryāḥ ca vṛṣaṇayoḥ utpattiḥ ṣaḍguṇā smṛtā evam gāvaḥ ca viprāḥ ca gāyatrī satyam eva ca sa pāpa karmā narake pratiṣṭhām pratipadyate yadā labheta mānuṣyam jāti andhaḥ jāyate naraḥ śṛṅgayoḥ kapilām yaḥ tu vāhayeta duheta vā tiryagyonim sa labhate jāyamānaḥ punar punar kapilām vāladhāneṣu vāhayeta duheta vā nirāśrayaḥ sadā ca eva jāyate yadi ced kṛmiḥ sahasraśas kṛmiḥ bhūtvā mānuṣyam prāpnuyāt atha caṇḍālaḥ pāpa karmā vā jāyate sa nara adhamaḥ ghrāṇena kapilām yaḥ tu pramādāt upajīvati vyādhi grastaḥ jaḍaḥ rogī bhavet mānuṣyam āgataḥ madhu sarpiḥ sugandhāḥ tu kapilāḥ śāstrataḥ smṛtāḥ alpa āyuḥ sa bhavet jātaḥ hīna varṇa kula udbhavaḥ ye tu pāpāḥ hi asūyante kapilām vāhayanti ca nirayeṣu pratiṣṭhante yāvat ābhūtasaṃplavam mayā abhipannāḥ devāḥ ca modante śāśvatīḥ samāḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca evam śrutvā param puṇyam gavām dānam anuttamam dharmarājaḥ prahṛṣṭa ātmā kauravam punar abravīt nidarśitam pūrvam eva ṛṣiṇā nāsiketunā pratyuvāca tatas teṣām vasiṣṭhaḥ bhagavān ṛṣiḥ rāma viprāḥ satkriyantām veda prāmāṇya darśanāt bhūyas ca viprarṣi gaṇāḥ praṣṭavyāḥ pāvanam prati te yat brūyuḥ mahā prājñāḥ tat ca eva samudācara tam eva artham mahā tejāḥ papraccha bhṛgu nandanaḥ jātā matiḥ me vipra indrāḥ katham pūyeyam iti uta yadi vaḥ anugraha kṛtā buddhiḥ mām prati sattamāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ prabrūta pāvanam kim me bhavet iti tapodhanāḥ anyat dānam tu vipra ṛṣe śrūyatām pāvanam mahat dagdhvā lokān purā vīryāt saṃbhūtam iha śuśruma tatas abravīt vasiṣṭhaḥ tam bhagavān saṃśita vrataḥ śṛṇu rāma yathā utpannam suvarṇam anala prabham agnīṣoma ātmakam idam suvarṇam viddhi niścaye ajaḥ agniḥ varuṇaḥ meṣaḥ sūryaḥ aśvaḥ iti darśanam kuñjarāḥ ca smṛtāḥ nāgāḥ mahiṣāḥ ca asurāḥ iti kukkuṭāḥ ca varāhāḥ ca rākṣasāḥ bhṛgu nandana iḍā gāvaḥ payaḥ somaḥ bhūmiḥ iti eva ca śrutiḥ etasmāt kāraṇāt devāḥ gandharva uraga rākṣasāḥ manuṣyāḥ ca piśācāḥ ca prayatāḥ dhārayanti tat mukuṭaiḥ aṅgada yutaiḥ alaṃkāraiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ pṛthivīm gāḥ ca dattvā iha yat ca anyat api kim cana viśiṣyate suvarṇasya dānam paramakam vibho suvarṇam eva sarvāsu dakṣiṇāsu vidhīyate trailokya sārau hi yuvām lokam saṃtāpayiṣyatha vīryam dhārayitum śaktāḥ samastāḥ iti naḥ matiḥ dhairyāt eva nigṛhṇīṣva viveśa kīcaka anantaḥ praviśan eva sūcitaḥ ūṣmāyām cittalaiḥ vaṃśaiḥ tān tyaktvā prāviśat śamīm kriyamāṇā ca te vāṇī bhaviṣyati naraiḥ bhuvi ṛgvedaḥ ca agamat tatra sāmāni ca yajūṃṣi ca yajñam ca śobhayām āsa bahu rūpam pinākadhṛk dyauḥ nabhaḥ pṛthivī kham ca tathā ca eva eṣa bhūpatiḥ sarva vidyā īśvaraḥ śrīmān eṣa ca api vibhāvasuḥ eṣa brahmā śivaḥ rudraḥ varuṇaḥ agniḥ prajāpatiḥ kīrtyate bhagavān devaḥ sarva bhūta patiḥ śivaḥ dīkṣā dīpta vratā devī diśaḥ ca sa digīśvarāḥ dṛṣṭvā devī tu tat skannam retaḥ śūla astra dhāriṇaḥ valmīkasya vapāyām ca karṇe vājasya dakṣiṇe śakaṭa urvyām parasya apsu brāhmaṇasya kare api vā eṣā suvarṇasya utpattiḥ kathitā te mayā anagha kārttikeyaḥ tu saṃvṛddhaḥ kālena mahatā tadā jaghāna tārakam ca api daityam anyān tathā asurān tridaśa indra ājñayā brahman lokānām hita kāmyayā suvarṇa dāne ca mayā kathitāḥ te guṇāḥ vibho janma te kathitam puṇyam pradānam ca yudhiṣṭhira iti rāmam uvāca idam vasiṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭha vāc ṛṣiḥ suvarṇa dāne māhātmyam tat kuruṣva yudhiṣṭhira piṇḍān vā āhāryakam śrāddham kuryāt māsānumāsikam piṇḍānām māsikam śrāddham anvāhāryam viduḥ budhāḥ tat āmiṣeṇa kurvīta prathamaḥ prāñjaliḥ śuciḥ caturdaśīm prakurvāṇaḥ sukha bhāgī bhavet naraḥ yajet vai aśvamedhena nīlam vā vṛṣam utsṛjet phalgunīṣu dadat śrāddham labhet rūpavataḥ sutān uttarāsu dadat śrāddham rūpavān abhijāyate citrāsu ca dadat śrāddham nānā phalam avāpnuyāt śravaṇe śruti saṃpattiḥ dhaniṣṭhāsu dhanam bhavet tathā śatabhiṣaj yoge vyādhi mokṣaḥ balam tathā īdṛśaiḥ brāhmaṇaiḥ bhuktam a pāṅkteyaiḥ yudhiṣṭhira śrāddham bhuktvā tu adhīyīta vṛṣalī talpa gaḥ ca yaḥ purīṣe tasya tam māsam pitaraḥ tasya śerate soma vikrayiṇe viṣṭhā bhiṣaje pūya śoṇitam naṣṭam devalake dattam apratiṣṭham ca vārdhuṣe purīṣam bhuñjate tasya pitaraḥ pretya niścayaḥ tilaiḥ virahitam śrāddham kṛtam krodha vaśena ca sat ācāra parāḥ ca eva vijñeyāḥ sarva pāvanāḥ pañcarātra vidaḥ mukhyāḥ tathā bhāgavatāḥ pare vaikhānasāḥ kula śreṣṭhāḥ vaidika ācāra cāriṇaḥ somapāḥ ca haviṣmantaḥ tathā barhiṣadāḥ api ajaḥ marīciḥ iti eva viśvedevāḥ sanātanāḥ jamadagniḥ vasiṣṭhaḥ ca sādhvī ca eva api arundhatī tasmāt dadāmi vaḥ vittam tat gṛhṇīdhvam tapodhanāḥ dhenūnām dadāmi ayutam samagram daśa sūnā samaḥ cakrī daśa cakri samaḥ dhvajī daśa dhvaji samāḥ veśyāḥ daśa veśyā samaḥ nṛpaḥ daśa sūnā sahasrāṇi yaḥ vāhayati sainikaḥ yat ahobhiḥ su bahubhiḥ saṃcitam paramam tapaḥ dṛṣṭvā phalāni munayaḥ te grahītum upādravan atriḥ na jātu kāmaḥ kāmānām upabhogena śāmyati haviṣā kṛṣṇavartmā iva bhūyas eva abhivardhate yathā caranti vidvāṃsaḥ tathā dharma parāyaṇāḥ yasya syāt vibhava utkaṇṭhā tṛṣṇā yasya adhikā bhavet vinayāt sa samṛddha arthān samupāsīta yatnataḥ kaśmala adhama dānāya pratiyatnāḥ prajā adhamāḥ vaktra eka deśe gaṇḍa iti dhātum etam pracakṣate udapāne śleṣma mūtre ca utsṛjet vṛṣalī patiḥ yātu pāpīyasām lokam bisa stainyam karoti yaḥ śvaśrvā vivādam vadatu bhartṛ hīnā ca jīvatu śukaḥ śūdra yonau dvijātiḥ tu retaḥ muñcatu kāmataḥ yamaḥ śunakaḥ bharadvājaḥ sadā vasatu saṃnyāsī grāme vā nagare api vā vāsudevam jagat yonim sarva ādhāram anāmakam parāṇām paramam yātu yaḥ te harati puṣkaram tvayā loka hita arthāya pītaḥ vai lavaṇa ambudhiḥ vindhyaḥ nivāritaḥ yena vātāpiḥ ca niṣūditaḥ nahuṣaḥ sarpatām nītaḥ bhavatā yat dvijottama jijñāsamānena mayā kṛtam tat kṣantum arhasi puṇya tīrtheṣu gātrāṇi plāvayām āsuḥ añjasā bahuṣu api nimitteṣu puṇyam āśritya dīyate yudhiṣṭhiraḥ śūdrāṇām iha śuśrūṣā nityam eva anuvarṇitā kaiḥ kāraṇaiḥ katividhā śuśrūṣā samudāhṛtā ke ca śuśrūṣayā lokāḥ vihitāḥ bharata ṛṣabha śūdrāṇām bharata śreṣṭha brūhi me dharma lakṣaṇam bhīṣmaḥ atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam śūdrāṇām anukampā artham yat uktam brahma vādinā vṛddhaḥ parāśaraḥ prāha dharmam śubhram anāmayam anugraha artham varṇānām śauca ācāra samanvitam dharma upadeśam akhilam yathāvat anupūrvaśas śiṣyān adhyāpayām āsa śāstram arthavat artha vid kṣānta indriyeṇa mānena śucinā acāpalena vai alubdhena a nṛśaṃsena ṛjunā brahma vādinā arayaḥ ṣaṭ vijetavyāḥ nityam svam deham āśritāḥ vidhinā dhṛtim āsthāya śuśrūṣuḥ anahaṃkṛtaḥ karmaṇā manasā vācā cakṣuṣā ca caturvidham āsthāya niyamam dhīmān śāntaḥ dāntaḥ jita indriyaḥ yadi śūdraḥ tapaḥ kuryāt veda dṛṣṭena karmaṇā iha ca asya parikleśaḥ pretya ca asya aśubhā gatiḥ adharmyam ayaśasyam ca tapaḥ śūdre pratiṣṭhitam amārgeṇa prayātānām pratyakṣāt upalabhyate cāturvarṇya vyapetānām jāti mūrti parigrahaḥ tathā te hi śakāḥ cīnāḥ kāmbhojāḥ pāradāḥ tathā śabarāḥ paplavāḥ ca eva tuṣārayavanāḥ tathā dārvāḥ ca daradāḥ ca eva ujjihānāḥ tathā itarāḥ veṇāḥ ca kaṅkaṇāḥ ca eva siṃhalāḥ madrakāḥ tathā kiṣkindhakāḥ pulindāḥ ca kahvāḥ ca andhrāḥ sa nīragāḥ gandhikāḥ dramiḍāḥ ca eva barbarāḥ cūcukāḥ tathā kirātāḥ pārvateyāḥ ca kolāḥ colāḥ sakhāṣakāḥ ārukāḥ ca eva dohāḥ ca yāḥ ca anyāḥ mleccha jātayaḥ vikṛtāḥ vikṛta ācārāḥ dṛśyante krūra buddhayaḥ amārgeṇa āśritāḥ dharmam tatas jāti antaram gatāḥ na naśyati kṛtam karma śubham vā yadi vā aśubham pāṣaṇḍān arcayiṣyanti dharma kāmāḥ vṛthak āśramāḥ evam caturṇām varṇānām āśramāṇām ca pārthiva viparītam vartamānāḥ mlecchāḥ jāyanti abuddhayaḥ adhyāya dhaninaḥ viprāḥ kṣatriyāṇām balam dhanam vaṇij kṛṣiḥ ca vaiśyānām śūdrāṇām paricārikā punar ca nirayaḥ teṣām tiryagyoniḥ ca śāśvatī ye tu sat patham āsthāya varṇa āśrama kṛtam purā sarvān vimārgān utsṛjya svadharma patham āśritāḥ śāstra dṛṣṭena vidhinā śraddhayā jita manyavaḥ teṣām vidhim pravakṣyāmi yathāvat anupūrvaśas upādāna vidhim kṛtsnam śuśrūṣā adhigamam tathā śiṣṭa upanayanam ca eva mantrāṇi vividhāni ca tathā śiṣya parīkṣām ca śāstra prāmāṇya darśanāt pravakṣyāmi yathātattvam yathāvat anupūrvaśas śauca kṛtyasya śauca arthān sarvān eva viśeṣataḥ mahāśauca prabhṛtayaḥ dṛṣṭāḥ tattva artha darśibhiḥ tatra api śūdraḥ bhikṣūṇām idam śeṣam ca kalpayet bhikṣubhiḥ su kṛta prajñaiḥ kevalam dharmam āśritaiḥ avakāśam imam medhyam nirmitam kāma vīrudham nirjanam saṃvṛtam buddhvā niyata ātmā jita indriyaḥ sa jalam bhājanam sthāpya mṛttikām ca parīkṣitām parīkṣya bhūmim mūtra arthī tatas āsīta vāgyataḥ udak mukhaḥ divā kuryāt rātrau ced dakṣiṇā mukhaḥ antarhitāyām bhūmau tu antarhita śirāḥ tathā asamāpte tathā śauce na vācam kim cit īrayet śauca artham upaviṣṭaḥ tu mṛd bhājana puraskṛtaḥ sthāpyam kamaṇḍalum gṛhya pārśva ūrubhyām atha antare pāṇinā śuddham udakam saṃgṛhya vidhi pūrvakam vipruṣaḥ ca yathā na syuḥ yathā ca ūrū na saṃspṛśet apāne mṛttikāḥ tisraḥ pradeyāḥ tu anupūrvaśas hastābhyām ca tathā vipraḥ hastam hastena saṃspṛśet apāne nava deyāḥ syuḥ iti vṛddha anuśāsanam mṛttikā dīyamānā hi śodhayet deśam añjasā tasmāt pāṇi tale deyāḥ mṛttikāḥ tu punar punar tathā gudam pramārjeta śauca artham tu punar punar savye dvādaśa deyāḥ syuḥ tisraḥ tisraḥ punar punar deyāḥ kūrparake haste pṛṣṭhabandhe punar punar tatas anyām mṛttikām gṛhya kāryam śaucam punar tayoḥ atas anyathā tu yaḥ kuryāt prāyaścittīyate tu saḥ saha pādam atha ūrubhyām hasta śaucam asaṃśayam avadhīrayamāṇasya saṃdehaḥ upajāyate sa kardamam tu varṣāsu gṛham āviśya saṃkaṭam hastayoḥ mṛttikāḥ tisraḥ pādayoḥ ṣaḍ pradāpayet kāmam dattvā gude dadyāt tisraḥ padbhyām tathā eva ca mūtra śauce tathā hastau pādābhyām ca anupūrvaśas naiṣṭhike sthāna śauce tu mahāśaucam vidhīyate kṣāra oṣarābhyām vastrasya kuryāt śaucam mṛdā saha snāna śāṭyām mṛdaḥ tisraḥ hastābhyām ca anupūrvaśas śaucam prayatnataḥ kṛtvā kampayānaḥ samuddharet deyāḥ catasraḥ tisraḥ vā dvis vā api ekā tathā āpadi kālam āsādya deśam ca śaucasya gurulāghavam a viprekṣan asaṃbhrāntaḥ pādau prakṣālya tad paraḥ a prakṣālita pādaḥ tu pāṇim ā maṇibandhanāt adhastāt upariṣṭāt ca tatas pāṇim upaspṛśet manaḥ gatāḥ tu niḥśabdāḥ niḥśabdam tris apaḥ pibet dvis mukham parimṛjyāt ca khāni ca upaspṛśet budhaḥ ṛgvedam tena prīṇāti prathamam yaḥ pibet apaḥ dvitīyam tu yajurvedam tṛtīyam sāmaḥ eva ca mṛjyate prathamam tena atharvā prītim āpnuyāt dvitīyena itihāsam ca purāṇa smṛti devatāḥ yat cakṣuṣi samādhatte tena ādityam tu prīṇayet prīṇāti vāyum ghrāṇam ca diśaḥ ca api atha śrotrayoḥ brahmāṇam tena prīṇāti yat mūrdhani samāpayet samutkṣipati ca apa ūrdhvam ākāśam tena prīṇayet prīṇāti viṣṇum padbhyām tu salilam vai samādadhat prāc mukha udak mukhaḥ vā api antar jānuḥ upaspṛśet anneṣu danta lagneṣu ucchiṣṭaḥ punar ācamet vidhiḥ eṣa samuddiṣṭaḥ śauce ca abhyukṣaṇam smṛtam śūdrasya eṣa vidhiḥ dṛṣṭaḥ gṛhāt niṣkramataḥ sataḥ yaśaskāmena bhikṣubhyaḥ śūdreṇa ātma hita arthinā parāśaraḥ kṣatrāḥ ārambha yajñāḥ tu vīrya yajñāḥ viśaḥ smṛtāḥ śūdrāḥ paricara ayajñāḥ japa yajñāḥ tu brāhmaṇāḥ śuśrūṣā jīvinaḥ śūdrāḥ vaiśyāḥ vipaṇi jīvinaḥ aniṣṭa nigrahāḥ kṣatrāḥ viprāḥ svādhyāya jīvinaḥ tapasā śobhate vipraḥ rājanyaḥ pālana ādibhiḥ ātithyena tathā vaiśyaḥ śūdraḥ dāsyena śobhate yata ātmanā tu śūdreṇa śuśrūṣā nityam eva ca kartavyā triṣu varṇeṣu prāyeṇa āśrama vāsiṣu aśaktena trivargasya sevyāḥ hi āśrama vāsinaḥ yathāśakyam yathāprajñam yathādharmam yathāśrutam viśeṣeṇa eva kartavyā śuśrūṣā bhikṣuka āśrame āśramāṇām tu sarveṣām caturṇām bhikṣuka āśramam pradhānam iti varṇyante śiṣṭāḥ śāstra viniścaye yat ca upadiśyate śiṣṭaiḥ śruti smṛti vidhānataḥ atas anyathā tu kurvāṇaḥ śreyaḥ nā āpnoti mānavaḥ tasmāt bhikṣuṣu śūdreṇa kāryam ātma hitam sadā asūyatā kṛtasya iha phalam duḥkhāt avāpyate kṣamāvān śīla saṃpannaḥ satya dharma parāyaṇaḥ āpad bhāvena kuryāt hi śuśrūṣām bhikṣuka āśrame saṃsāra sāgaram ghoram tariṣyāmi na saṃśayaḥ na atas param mama api anyaḥ eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ evam saṃcintya manasā śūdraḥ buddhi samādhinā kuryāt avimanāḥ nityam śuśrūṣā dharmam uttamam śuśrūṣā niyamena iha bhāvyam śiṣṭa āśinā sadā śama anvitena dāntena kārya akārya vidā sadā sarva kāryeṣu kṛtyāni kṛtāni eva tu darśayet yat āśramasya a viruddham dharma mātra abhisaṃhitam tat kāryam avicāreṇa nityam eva śubha arthinā manasā karmaṇā vācā nityam eva prasādayet sthātavyam tiṣṭhamāneṣu gacchamānān anuvrajet āsīneṣu āsitavyam ca nityam eva anuvartatā udvartana ādīn ca tathā kuryāt a praticoditaḥ naiśa kāryāṇi kṛtvā tu nityam ca eva anucoditaḥ bhikṣūṇām nilayam gatvā praṇamya vidhi pūrvakam tathā ācārya purogāṇām anukuryāt namaskriyām svadharma cāriṇām ca api sukham pṛṣṭvā abhivādya ca saṃmārjanam atho kṛtvā kṛtvā ca api upalepanam tatas puṣpa balim dadyāt puṣpāṇi ādāya dharmataḥ yathā upaghātaḥ na bhavet svādhyāye āśramiṇām tathā upaghātam tu kurvāṇaḥ enasā saṃprayujyate tathā ātmā praṇidhātavyaḥ yathā te prītim āpnuyuḥ paricārakaḥ aham varṇānām trayāṇām dharmataḥ smṛtaḥ kim uta āśrama vṛddhānām yathālabdha upajīvinām bhikṣūṇām gata rāgāṇām kevalam jñāna darśinām viśeṣeṇa mayā kāryā śuśrūṣā niyata ātmanā teṣām prasādāt tapasā prāpsyāmi iṣṭām śubhām gatim vidhinā sva upadiṣṭena prāpnoti paramām gatim parāśaraḥ na tathā saṃpradānena na upavāsa ādibhiḥ tathā iṣṭām gatim avāpnoti yathā śuśrūṣa karmaṇā yādṛśena tu toyena śuddhim prakurute naraḥ śūdraḥ api etena mārgeṇa yādṛśam sevate janam tādṛś bhavati saṃsargāt acireṇa na saṃśayaḥ adhvanā karśitānām ca vyādhitānām tathā eva ca śuśrūṣām niyatam kuryāt teṣām āpadi yatnataḥ yathākāmam ca kāryāṇi sarvāṇi eva upasādhayet prāyaścittam yathā na syāt tathā sarvam samācaret vyādhitānām tu prayataḥ caila prakṣālana ādibhiḥ pratikarma kriyā kāryā bheṣaja ānayanaiḥ tathā piṃṣaṇa ālepanam cūrṇam kaṣāyam atha sādhanam bhikṣāṭanaḥ abhigaccheta bhiṣajaḥ ca vipaścitaḥ tatas viniṣkraya arthāni dravyāṇi samupārjayet yaḥ ca prīta manāḥ dadyāt ādadyāt bheṣajam naraḥ aśraddhayā hi dattāni tāni abhojyāni bhikṣubhiḥ śraddhayā yat upādattam śraddhayā ca upapāditam ā dehapatanāt evam śuśrūṣā artham atandritaiḥ sāgarasya ūrmi sadṛśāḥ vijñātavyāḥ guṇa ātmakāḥ vidyāt evam hi yaḥ dhīmān tattva vid tattva darśanaḥ parāśaraḥ evam prayatitavyam hi śuśrūṣā artham atandritaiḥ śreyaḥ eva abhidhātavyam kartavyam ca prahṛṣṭa vat tūṣṇīṃbhāvena vai tatra na kruddham abhisaṃvadet na ādadīta para svāni na gṛhṇīyāt a yācitam kopinam tu na yāceta jñāna vidveṣa kāritaḥ sthāvareṣu dayām kuryāt jaṃgameṣu ca prāṇiṣu yathā ātmani tathā anyeṣu samām dṛṣṭim nipātayet sarva bhūteṣu ca ātmānam sarva bhūtāni ca ātmani hiṃsām vā yadi vā ahiṃsām na kuryāt ātma kāraṇāt evam sa mucyate doṣāt parān āśritya vartayan ātma āśrayeṇa doṣeṇa lipyate hi alpa buddhimān jarāyu ja aṇḍa jāḥ ca eva udbhid jāḥ sveda jāḥ ca ye avadhyāḥ sarve eva ete budhaiḥ samanuvarṇitāḥ niścaya artham vibuddhānām prāyaścittam vidhīyate hiṃsā yathā anyā vihitā tathā doṣam prayojayet yathā upadiṣṭam guruṇā śiṣyasya carataḥ vidhim na hi lobhaḥ prabhavati hiṃsā vā api tad ātmikā śāstra darśanam etat hi vihitam viśvayoninā kṛtam kṛtavatām gacchet kim punar yaḥ niṣevate na śūdraḥ patate kaḥ cit na ca saṃskāram arhati anugraha artham manunā sarva varṇeṣu varṇitam yadā apavādaḥ tu bhavet strī kṛtaḥ paricārake abhra avakāśa śayanam tasya saṃvatsaram smṛtam varṣa trayam tu vaiśyāyāḥ kṣatriyāyāḥ tu ṣaḍ samāḥ brāhmaṇyā tu sametasya samāḥ dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ śiśna avapātanāt vā api viśuddhim samavāpnuyāt upoṣya ekāham ādadyāt prāṇāyāmān tu dvādaśa tris snānam udake kṛtvā tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate asthibandheṣu dviguṇam prāyaścittam vidhīyate anena vidhinā vā api sthāvareṣu na saṃśayaḥ kāyena padbhyām hastābhyām aparādhāt tu mucyate aduṣṭam kṣapayet yaḥ tu sarva varṇeṣu vācayet tasya api aṣṭaguṇam vidyāt prāyaścittam tat eva tu caturguṇam karma kṛte dviguṇam vāc pradūṣite kṛtvā tu mānasam pāpam tathā eva eka guṇam smṛtam tasmāt etāni sarvāṇi viditvā na samācaret sarva bhūta hita artham hi kuśalāni samācaret tad gatiḥ tad samācāraḥ tad manāḥ tad parāyaṇaḥ kālam eva pratīkṣeta nirveśam bhṛtakaḥ yathā evam pravartamānaḥ tu vinītaḥ prayata ātmavān parāśaraḥ nirṇayam puṇya pāpābhyām acireṇa upagacchati śuśrūṣā nirataḥ nityam ariṣṭāni upalakṣayet traivārṣikam dvi vārṣikam vā vārṣikam vā samutthitam ṣāṇmāsikam māsikam vā sapta rātrikam eva vā sarvān tad arthān vā vidyāt teṣām cihnāni lakṣayet puruṣam hiraṇmayam yaḥ tu tiṣṭhantam dakṣiṇā mukham śuddha maṇḍalam ādityam a raśmim saṃprapaśyataḥ jyotsnāyām ātmanaḥ chāyām sa chidrām yaḥ prapaśyati mṛtyum saṃvatsareṇa eva jānīyāt sa vicakṣaṇaḥ viśiraskām yadā chāyām paśyet puruṣaḥ ātmanaḥ jānīyāt ātmanaḥ mṛtyum ṣaṣ māsena iha buddhimān karṇau pidhāya hastābhyām śabdam na śṛṇute yadi jānīyāt ātmanaḥ mṛtyum māsena eva vicakṣaṇaḥ śava gandham upāghrāti anyat vā surabhim naraḥ karṇa nāsā apanayanam danta dṛṣṭi virāga tā lupta saṃjñam hi karaṇam sadyas mṛtyum samādiśet evam eṣām ariṣṭānām paśyet anyatamam yadi na tam kālam parīkṣeta yathā ariṣṭam prakalpitam tatra prāṇān pramuñceta tam īśānam anusmaran tatas anyam deham āsādya gāndharvam sthānam āpnuyāt nīla vaiḍūrya varṇena vimānena avabhāsayan nabhaḥ sthalam a dīna ātmā sārdham apsarasām gaṇaiḥ patitaḥ ca kṣaye kāle kṣaṇena vimala dyutiḥ adhīyamānaḥ veda arthān guru śuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ adhītya dakṣiṇām dattvā gurave vidhi pūrvakam dharmam saṃcinute nityam mṛdu gāmī jita indriyaḥ sa kāla pariṇāmāt tu mṛtyunā saṃprayujyate saṃskṛtaḥ ca agnihotreṇa kṛta pātra upadhānavān marudbhiḥ sahitaḥ ca api tulya tejāḥ mahā dyutiḥ bāla arka sama varṇena vimānena virājatā viraja umbara saṃvītaḥ tapta kāñcana bhūṣaṇaḥ chanda kāma anusārī ca dviguṇam kālam āvaset pārthivīm śriyam āpannaḥ śrīmān dharmapatiḥ yathā samāvṛttaḥ tatas pitrā yauvarājye abhiṣicyate kṛta dārakriyaḥ śrīmān rājyam saṃprāpya dharmataḥ prajāḥ pālayate samyak ṣaḍbhāga kṛta saṃvidhiḥ yajñaiḥ bahubhiḥ ījānaḥ samyak āpta artha dakṣiṇaiḥ praśāsati mahīm śrīmān rājyam indra sama dyutiḥ kālasya vaśam āpannaḥ prāṇān tyajati saṃyuge rājarṣibhiḥ puṇya kṛdbhiḥ yathā devapatiḥ tathā divya jāmbūnada mayam bhrājamānam samantataḥ yānam āruhya vicaret yathā śakraḥ śacīpatiḥ sa tatra vasate ṣaṣṭim padmāni iha mudā anvitaḥ parāśaraḥ tatas śruti samāpannaḥ saṃskṛtaḥ ca yathāvidhi gāyatreṇa chandasā tu saṃskṛtaḥ carita vrataḥ adhīyamānaḥ medhāvī śuddha ātmā niyata vrataḥ acireṇa eva kālena sa aṅgān vedān avāpnute samāvṛttaḥ sa dharma ātmā samāvṛtta kriyaḥ tathā yājana adhyāpana rataḥ kuśale karmaṇi sthitaḥ nyāya āgata dhana anveṣī nyāya vṛttaḥ tapodhanaḥ sarva bhūta hitaḥ ca eva sarva śāstra viśāradaḥ sva dāra parituṣṭa ātmā ṛtu gāmī jita indriyaḥ sa kāla pariṇāmāt tu saṃyuktaḥ kāla dharmaṇā saṃskṛtaḥ ca agnihotreṇa yathāvat vidhi pūrvakam soma lokam avāpnoti deha nyāsāt na saṃśayaḥ viśvebhiḥ ca eva devaiḥ ca tathā brahmarṣibhiḥ punar devarṣibhiḥ ca apratimaiḥ tathā eva apsarasām gaṇaiḥ saumya rūpāḥ manaḥ kāntāḥ tapta kāñcana bhūṣaṇāḥ soma kanyāḥ vimāna stham ramayanti mudā anvitāḥ sabhām kāma javīm ca api nityam evā abhigacchati sarva loka īśvaram ṛṣim namaskṛtya pitāmaham parameṣṭhiḥ ananta śrīḥ lokānām prabhava apyayaḥ atha kāla kṣayāt tasmāt sthānāt āvartate punar jāti dharmān tathā sarvān sargāt āvartanāni ca garbha kleśāḥ mayā prāptāḥ jāyatā ca punar punar kāya kleśāḥ ca vividhāḥ dvaṃdvāni vividhāni ca śīta uṣṇa sukha duḥkhāni īrṣyā dveṣa kṛtāni ca chittvā bhāryā mayam pāśam tathā eva apatya saṃbhavam yati dharmam upāśritya guru śuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ yoga śāstram ca sāṃkhyam ca viditvā saḥ artha tattvataḥ anujñātaḥ ca guruṇā yathāśāstram avasthitaḥ puṇya tīrtha anusevī ca nadīnām pulina āśrayaḥ śūnya āgāra niketaḥ ca vana vṛkṣa guhā āśayaḥ śīrṇa parṇa puṭe ca api vyahne carati bhikṣukaḥ dharma labdham samaśnāti na kāmāt kim cit aśnute yuga mātra dṛś adhvānam krośāt ūrdhvam na gacchati samaḥ māna avamānābhyām sama loṣṭa aśma kāñcanaḥ nirmamaḥ nirahaṃkāraḥ sarva bhūta nirāśrayaḥ kālasya parimāṇāt tu yathā pakva phalam tathā sa visṛjya svakam deham praviśet brahma śāśvatam nirguṇam ca prakṛtimat nirvikāram ca sarvaśas parāśaraḥ evam sa bhikṣuḥ nirvāṇam prāpnuyāt dagdha kilbiṣaḥ ihasthaḥ deham utsṛjya nīḍam śakuni vat yathā brahmaṇaḥ sthānam acalam sthānāt sthānam avāpnuyāt sā eṣā gatiḥ anādi antā sarvaiḥ api upadhāritā vartitavyam yathāśaktyā yathā proktam manīṣibhiḥ yat karoti tat aśnāti śubham vā yadi vā aśubham na akṛtam bhujyate karma na kṛtam naśyate phalam tathā śubha samācāraḥ śubham eva āpnute phalam tathā aśubha samācāraḥ hi aśubham samavāpnute tathā śubha samācāraḥ hi aśubhāni vivarjayet śubhāni eva samādadyāt yaḥ icchet bhūtim ātmanaḥ bhūtiḥ ca na anyatas śakyā śūdrāṇām iti niścayaḥ ṛte yatīnām śuśrūṣām iti santaḥ vyavasthitāḥ śakyate hi āgamāt eva gatim prāptum anāmayām parā ca eṣām gatiḥ dṛṣṭā yām anveṣanti sādhavaḥ imam hi dharmam āsthāya ye api syuḥ pāpa yonayaḥ striyaḥ vaiśyāḥ ca śūdrāḥ ca prāpnuyuḥ paramām gatim kim punar brāhmaṇaḥ vidvān kṣatriyaḥ vā bahu śrutaḥ na ca api akṣīṇa pāpasya jñānam bhavati dehinaḥ upalabhya tu vijñānam jñānam vā api anasūyakaḥ tathā eva vartet guruṣu bhūyāṃsam vā samāhitaḥ vyartham asya śrutam bhavati jñānam ajñāna tām vrajet gatim ca api aśubhām gacchet nirayāya na saṃśayaḥ adṛṣṭa pūrva kalyāṇaḥ yathā dṛṣṭvā vidhim naraḥ utsekāt moham āpadya tattva jñānam avāptavān phalam jñānasya hi śamaḥ praśamāya yatet sadā upaśāntena dāntena kṣamā yuktena sarvadā śuśrūṣā pratipattavyā nityam eva anasūyatā dhṛtyā śiśna udaram rakṣet pāṇi pādam ca cakṣuṣā dhṛtya āsīta tatas gatvā śuddha deśam su saṃvṛtam labdhvā āsanam yathādṛṣṭam vidhi pūrvam samācaret ādīpyamānam vapuṣā vidhūmam analam yathā saṃsthitam hṛdaye paśyet īśam śāśvatam avyayam na ca ayuktena śakyeta draṣṭum dehe maheśvaraḥ atha tu evam na śaknoti kartum hṛdaya dhāraṇam yathā sāṃkhyam upāsīta yathāvat yogam āsthitaḥ pañca buddhīndriyāṇi iha pañca karmendriyāṇi api pañcabhūta viśeṣāḥ ca manaḥ ca eva tu ṣoḍaśa tanmātrāṇi api pañca eva manaḥ ahaṃkāraḥ eva ca aṣṭamam ca api atha avyaktam etāḥ prakṛti saṃjñitāḥ etāḥ prakṛtayaḥ ca aṣṭau vikārāḥ ca api ṣoḍaśa evam varṣam samuttīrya tīrṇaḥ bhavati na anyathā ahani ahani śānta ātmā pāvanāya hitāya ca bhikṣuka āśramam āsthāya śuśrūṣā nirataḥ budhaḥ vidyā adhikāḥ ca api niṣevitavyāḥ savarṇa tām gacchati saṃnikarṣān bhīṣmaḥ śuśrūṣayā vṛtta gatim samādhinā yudhiṣṭhiraḥ keṣām devāḥ mahābhāgāḥ saṃnamante mahātmanām bhīṣmaḥ loke asmin tān ṛṣīn sarvān śrotum icchāmi sattama itihāsam imam viprāḥ kīrtayanti purāvidaḥ asmin arthe mahā prājñāḥ tam nibodha yudhiṣṭhira vṛtram hatvā api upāvṛttam tridaśānām puraskṛtam mahā indram anusaṃprāptam stūyamānam mahā ṛṣibhiḥ śriyā paramayā yuktam ratha stham hari vāhanam mātaliḥ prāñjaliḥ bhūtvā devam indram uvāca ha namaskṛtānām sarveṣām bhagavan tvam puraskṛtaḥ bhīṣmaḥ yeṣām loke namaskuryāt tān bravītu bhavān mama tasya tat vacanam śrutvā devarājaḥ śacīpatiḥ yantāram paripṛcchantam tam indraḥ pratyuvāca saḥ dharmam ca artham ca kāmam ca yeṣām cintayatām matiḥ ye rūpa guṇa saṃpannāḥ pramadā hṛdayaṃgamāḥ nivṛttāḥ kāma bhogeṣu tān namasyāmi mātale sveṣu bhogeṣu saṃtuṣṭāḥ su vācaḥ vacana kṣamāḥ amāna kāmāḥ ca arghya arhāḥ tān namasyāmi mātale dhanam vidyāḥ tathā aiśvaryam yeṣām na calayet matim calitām ye nigṛhṇanti tān nityam pūjayāmi aham iṣṭaiḥ dāraiḥ upetānām śucīnām agnihotriṇām catuṣpāda kuṭumbānām mātale praṇamāmi aham dharma mūla artha kāmānām brāhmaṇānām gavām api pativratānām nārīṇām praṇāmam prakaromi aham ye bhuktvā mānuṣān bhogān pūrve vayasi mātale tapasā svargam āyānti śaśvat tān pūjayāmi aham a saṃbhogāt na ca a saktān dharma nityān jita indriyān saṃnyastān acala prakhyān manasā pūjayāmi tān jñāna prasanna vidyānām nirūḍham dharmam icchatām paraiḥ kīrtita śaucānām mātale tān namāmi aham mayā bhārata śārdūla kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi vasanta kāle pānīyam taḍāge yasya tiṣṭhati panasa āmra ādayaḥ vṛkṣāḥ gulmāḥ mandāra pūrvakāḥ veṇu kramuka tvac sārāḥ sasyāni tṛṇa jātayaḥ sasyāni ropaṇāt eṣām tṛptiḥ bhavati śāśvatī śata avarān śata parān mātā pitṛ pitāmahān ropayitvā tu yaḥ vṛkṣān kāle śuśrūṣate nagān pūjayanti kṣitiruhāḥ chāyayā hi atithīn sadā agniṣoma ādi sṛṣṭau tu viṣṇoḥ sarvātmanaḥ prabho amṛtam manaḥ hlādayati śriyam ca eva dadāti ha tasya tuṣyanti vai devāḥ tuṣṭāḥ puṣṭim dadati api tasmai sumanasaḥ devāḥ tasmāt sumanasaḥ smṛtāḥ padmam ca tulasī jātiḥ api sarveṣu pūjitāḥ tathā cakāra tat sarvam papāta nahuṣaḥ yathā pitaraḥ ca mahābhāgāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ca tapodhanāḥ śruti smṛtī pramāṇam vā na iti vāde sa devarāj yaḥ idam śṛṇuyāt vā api paṭhate yaḥ dvijottamaḥ pātakānām param hi etat brahma sva haraṇam balāt sa anvayāḥ te vinaśyanti caṇḍālāḥ pretya ca iha ca bhṛtyānām api rājñām tu rajasā dhvaṃsitam makhe tad pānāt ca dvijāḥ sarve kṣipram narakam āviśan etat hi dhanam utkṛṣṭam dvijānām aviśeṣatas brahma sva hārī narakān yātanāḥ ca anubhūya tu maleṣu ca kṛmiḥ bhūtvā śva viṣṭhām upajīvati sādhūnām paribhāva īpsuḥ viprāṇām garvitaḥ dhanaiḥ yaḥ idam śṛṇuyāt nityam yaḥ paṭhet vā jita indriyaḥ sa yāti brahmaṇaḥ lokam brāhmaṇaḥ gautamaḥ yathā śamyā kṣepeṇa pṛthivīm tridhā paryacaram yajan brāhmaṇa arthāya karmāṇi raṇam ca eva karomi yat pūjitaiḥ brāhmaṇaiḥ nityam vacanena aham āgataḥ viprāṇām vacanāt sarvam paratra iha ca sidhyati śubhaiḥ su rakṣaṇaiḥ ca api toṣyāḥ eva dvijāḥ tvayā ācārāt dhanam akṣayyam ācāraḥ hanti kilbiṣam sa vināśam vrajati āśu sūdakaḥ aśuciḥ eva ca utthāya āvaśyakam kṛtvā kṛta śaucaḥ samāhitaḥ na upasṛṣṭam na vāri stham na madhyam nabhasaḥ gatam sa darbha pāṇiḥ tat kurvan vāgyataḥ tad manāḥ śuciḥ yāvaṃtaḥ romakūpāḥ syuḥ strīṇām gātreṣu nirmitāḥ tāvat varṣa sahasrāṇi narakam paryupāsate tādṛśam vidyate kim cit anāyuṣyam nṛṇām iha tādṛśam puruṣasya iha dhana āyuṣya haram nṛṇām annam bubhukṣamāṇaḥ tu tris mukhena spṛśet apaḥ bhokṣyan bhuktvā kṣute adhvānam gatvā mūtra purīṣayoḥ dvis ācāmet yathānyāyam hṛd gatam tu piban apaḥ tatra ācāntaḥ spṛśet agnim tathā mṛdu gatiḥ vrajet agnim ālabhya toyena sarvān prāṇān upaspṛśet gātrāṇi ca eva sarvāṇi nābhim pāṇi talena tu agrāsanaḥ jita krodhī bāla pūrvaḥ tu alaṃkṛtaḥ ghṛta āhuti viśuddha annam huta agniḥ ca ākṣipan graset tasya vedaḥ praṇaśyeta āyuḥ ca parihīyate na śūdra patita abhyāśe caṇḍāla śravaṇena ca karṇi nālīka nārācān nirharanti śarīrataḥ vāc śalyaḥ tu na nirhartum śakyaḥ hṛdiśayaḥ hi saḥ amāvāsyām ṛte nityam dantadhāvanam ācaret itihāsa purāṇāni dānam vedam ca nityaśas gāyatrī mananam nityam kuryāt saṃdhyām samāhitaḥ dantakāṣṭhe ca saṃdhyāyām mala utsarge ca mauna gaḥ na saṃdhyāyām svapet nityam snāyāt śuddhaḥ sadā bhavet bhūmau ekena pāṇinā gurūn dṛṣṭvā samuttiṣṭhet a kṛtvā deva pūjām ca na ācaret dantadhāvanam dāra saṃgrahaṇāt pūrvam na ācaret maithunam budhaḥ anyathā tu avakīrṇaḥ syāt prāyaścittam samācaret na udīkṣet para dārān ca rahasi eka āsanaḥ bhavet na ca api gacchet kāryeṇa samayāt vā api nāstike āsanam tu padā ākṛṣya na prasajjet tathā naraḥ tiryak na ca śayet nityam na antardhānena saṃyutaḥ na niśāyām punar snāyāt āpadi agni dvija antike snāne nirmālyakam varjet snātvā na aṅgāni mārjayet na upavāsam vṛthā kuryāt dhanam na apaharet iha na avalīḍham avajñātam āghrātam bhakṣayet api śuṣkam annam na bhuñjīta śūdra annam ca vivarjayet dadhi saktu na doṣāyām pibet madhu ca nityaśas niḥśabdena tu bhuñjīta mitram bhuñjīta na ahitam yaḥ atithīnām a dattvā ca bālānām jaraṭhaiḥ saha niṣṭhīvane maithune ca kṣute kakṣyā vimocane spṛśet karṇam sa praṇavam sūryam īkṣet sadā tadā kulīnaḥ paṇḍitaḥ iti rakṣyāḥ niḥsvāḥ sva śaktitaḥ devatāḥ pratimā ādarśāḥ candanāḥ puṣpa vallikāḥ śuddham jalam suvarṇam ca rajatam gṛhamaṅgalam taila abhyañjanam aṣṭamyām caturdaśyām ca parvasu sāmudreṇa ambhasā snānam kṣauram śrāddheṣu bhojanam satām gurūṇām vṛddhānām kula strīṇām viśeṣataḥ varjayet ati duṣṭām ca vyādhinīm vyaṅgikām tathā sa romaśām ati sthūlām kanyām mātṛ pitṛ sthitām alajjām bhrātṛ jām duṣṭām varjayet rakta keśinīm ātmānam śuci manyeta brāhmaṇān na ca paspaśet tam brūyāt brāhmaṇa ghnam ca tasya ca āyuḥ vinaśyati brāhmaṇāḥ hi sadā pūjyāḥ nareṇa bhūtim icchatā samāvṛttaḥ śucīn viprān devān atha gurūn namet kuryāt vyāhṛti homa ādīn arghya arhaḥ ca sadā bhavet ekoddiṣṭam na bhuñjīta prathamam tu viśeṣataḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇam varjyam sa vidhānam ca māsikam mānyānām mānanam kuryāt nindyānām nindanam tathā na strīṣu sajjet draṣṭavyam śaktyā dāna ruciḥ bhavet na brāhmaṇān paribhavet kārpaṇyam brāhmaṇaiḥ vṛtam patitān na abhibhāṣeta na āhvayeta rajasvalām ā ṣoḍaśāt ṛtuḥ mukhyaḥ putra janmani śabditaḥ yaḥ idam śṛṇuyāt nityam yaḥ ca api parikīrtayet sa śubhān prāpnuyāt lokān sat ācāra paraḥ nṛpa śriyā abhitaptāḥ tad bhedāt na bhinnāḥ syuḥ samāhitāḥ jyeṣṭhaḥ api durvinītaḥ tu kaniṣṭhaḥ tu viśeṣataḥ gurūṇām aparādhaḥ hi śakyaḥ kṣantavyaḥ eva ca same tu jyeṣṭhatantrāḥ syuḥ kim cit ūne api nityaśas rūpa draviṇa saṃpannaḥ sukhī bhavati nityaśas dhanavān kīrtimān ca eva kule mahati jāyate ramate strī sahasra āḍhye sukṛtī virajāḥ naraḥ agniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalam prāpnoti mānavaḥ haṃsa sārasa yuktam ca vimānam labhate naraḥ nānāvidha su gaṃdhābhiḥ nānā veṣābhiḥ eva ca tāvat brahma ādikāḥ lokāḥ varṣadhārā pramāṇataḥ vaiṣṇavīm vā atha raudrīm vā pañcamīm brahmacāriṇaḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ pitāmahena kathitāḥ dāna dharma āśritāḥ kathāḥ mayā śrutāḥ ṛṣīṇām tu saṃnidhau keśavasya ca punar kautūhalam abhūt tām evā ādhyātmikīm prati eṣa yādava dāyādaḥ tathā anujñātum arhati jñāte tu yasmin jñātavyam jñātam bhavati bhārata paścāt śroṣyāmahe rājñām śrāvyān dharmān pitāmaha kautūhalam ṛṣīṇām tu chettum arhasi sāṃpratam bhīṣmaḥ tathā iti uktaḥ ca provāca bhagavān śaṃkaraḥ tadā kim vā dhyānena draṣṭavyam yat bhavān anupaśyati yat ca tatpuruṣam śuddham iti uktam yoga sāṃkhyayoḥ kāla saṃkhyā ca kā deva draṣṭavyā tasya brahmaṇaḥ saṃkhyā saṃkhyādanasya eva yā proktā parama ṛṣibhiḥ śāstra dṛṣṭena mārgeṇa yathāvat yati sattama budhyamāna aprabuddhābhyām vidyā vedyam tathā eva ca vimokṣam trividham ca eva brūhi mokṣa vidām vara parisaṃkhyām ca sāṃkhyānām dhyānam yogeṣu ca arthavat ekatva darśanam ca eva tathā nānātva darśanam daivatāni ca sarvāṇi nikhilena anupūrvaśas yāni āśritāni deheṣu dehinām yati sattama śreṣṭhaḥ bhavān hi sarveṣām brahma jñānām anindita caturthaḥ tvam trayāṇām tu ye gatāḥ paramām gatim jñānena ca prākṛtena nirmuktaḥ mṛtyu bandhanāt vayam tu vaikṛtam mārgam āśritāḥ vai kṣaram sadā nyūne pathi nimagnāḥ tu aiśvarye aṣṭaguṇe tathā mahimānam pragṛhya imam vicarāmaḥ yathāsukham na punar mṛtyuḥ iti anyam nirmuktānām tu mūrtitaḥ mṛtyu doṣāḥ tu anantāḥ vai utpadyante kṛtātmanām martyeṣu nāka pṛṣṭheṣu nirayeṣu mahā mune tatra majjanti puruṣāḥ sukha duḥkhena ceṣṭitāḥ sanatkumāraḥ yat uktam bhavatā vākyam tattva saṃjñā iti dehinām caturviṃśatim eva atra ke cid āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ ke cit āhuḥ trayoviṃśam yathāśruti nidarśanāt vayam tu pañcaviṃśam vai tat adhiṣṭhāna saṃjñitam hiraṇmayīm praviśya eṣa mūrtim mūrtimatām vara cakāra puruṣaḥ tāta vikāra puruṣau ubhau avyaktāt ekaḥ eva eṣa mahān ātmā prasūyate ahaṃkāreṇa lokān ca vyāpya ca ahaṃkṛtena vai bhūta sargam ahaṃkārāt tṛtīyam viddhi vai kramāt ahaṃkārāt ca bhūteṣu caturtham viddhi vaikṛtam ahaṃkārāt ca jātāni yugapad vibudha īśvara sa viśeṣāṇi bhūtāni pañca prāhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ caturviṃśāt tu vai proktāt pañcaviṃśaḥ adhitiṣṭhati ete sargāḥ mayā proktāḥ catvāraḥ prākṛtāḥ tu iha ahaṃkārāt ca jātāni yugapad vibudha īśvara ahaṃkārāt ca bhūteṣu vividha artham vyajāyata vaikṛtāni pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tāni mahāmate eṣā tattva caturviṃśat mayā śāstra anumānataḥ varṇitā tava deveśa pañcaviṃśa samanvitā anena kāraṇena eva tattvam āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ sanatkumāraḥ tattva saṃkhyā śrutā ca eṣā yeṣām brahma vidām vara sarga saṃkhyā mayā proktā navānām anupūrvaśas yatnena dṛṣṭam paramātma tattvam svabhāva sattva stham anīśam īśam tava etat ākhyāmi muni indra vṛttyā pṛthvīm imām yadi api ratna pūrṇām dadyāt na deyam tu a parīkṣitāya na a śraddadhānāya na ca anya buddheḥ na ajñāna yuktāya na vismitāya svādhyāya yuktāya guṇa anvitāya saṃkṣepam ca api atha eteṣām tattvānām vṛṣabhadhvaja anuloma anujātānām pratilomam pralīyatām pravakṣyāmi tam adhyātmam sa adhibhūta adhidaivatam saṃkṣīṇe brahmadivase jagat jaladhim āviśet līyate agniḥ tathā vāyau vāyuḥ ākāśe eva tu evam asya asakṛt krīḍām āhuḥ tattva vidaḥ janāḥ yathāvat uditam śāstram yoge tu su mahātmabhiḥ prapañcita artham etāvat mahādeva mahātmabhiḥ prabhavam sarva bhūtānām rakṣaṇam tatra karma ca ratham ca yajña vāhaḥ atra karma ahaṃkāraḥ eva ca sarva bhāva abhidhāna artham śabdaḥ karma sadā smṛtam jñāpanam sarva varṇānām rūpam karma sadā smṛtam anubhāvaḥ adhibhūtam tu daivatam ca prajāpatiḥ pādau adhyātmam iti āhuḥ triśūla aṅka manīṣiṇaḥ vāc adhyātmam tathā eva āhuḥ pinākin tattva darśinaḥ etat kīṭa pataṃge ca śvapāke śuni hastini putrikā daṃśa maśake brāhmaṇe gavi pārthive deva dānava gandharva yakṣa rākṣasa kiṃnarāḥ yogeśvaram padmanābham viṣṇum jiṣṇum jagatpatim aprameyam avijñeyam harim nārāyaṇam prabhum kṛtāñjaliḥ śuciḥ bhūtvā praṇamya prayataḥ arcayet anādi antam param brahma na devāḥ ṛṣayaḥ viduḥ ekaḥ ayam veda bhagavān trātā nārāyaṇaḥ hariḥ nārāyaṇāt ṛṣi gaṇāḥ tatas siddhāḥ mahā uragāḥ yam āhuḥ vijita kleśam yasmin ca vihitāḥ prajāḥ yasmin lokāḥ sphuranti ime jāle śakunayaḥ yathā sapta ṛṣayaḥ manaḥ sapta sāṃkhyāḥ tu muni darśanāt sapta ṛṣayaḥ ca indriyāṇi pañca buddhīndriyāṇi ca śrotrayoḥ ca diśaḥ prāhuḥ manasi tu atha candramāḥ manaḥ ṣaṣṭham buddhiḥ saptamī hi ātmani sthāpitāni śarīreṣu na ātmani tasya hi kāraṇāni bhavanti sarvāṇi api sarva karmasu vā viṣayeṣu vā yuñjanti yathā ātmani svāni karmāṇi pravṛttāni saptasu api viṣayāṇām vyāpakāni tāni eva svapataḥ bhūta grāmasya ajam ātmānam deva loka sthāna saṃmitam deha antara gāminam mumukṣum vā anupraviśanti sūkṣmāṇi pralīyante evam praviṣṭeṣu bhūteṣu kaḥ jāgarti iti ucyate nidrā prasupteṣu vā atra jāgrat svapna śīlaḥ atra sadane ca devadyotanaḥ bhagavān ca atra kṣetrajñaḥ buddhiḥ vā sa hi suptasya api svapna darśanāni paśyati sanatkumāraḥ saḥ eṣa prājñaḥ atha taijasaḥ kāya agniḥ sa hi suptasya ātmā vai a viduṣaḥ vā a pratibudhyamānasya annam pacati iti ante avatiṣṭhate etat ātmānam adhikṛtya anujñātam iti prabhavaḥ ca apyayaḥ tāta varṇitaḥ te anupūrvaśas tathā adhyātma adhibhūtam ca tathā eva atra adhidaivatam yāni āśritāni deheṣu yāni pṛcchasi śaṃkara vāci agniḥ tu atha jihvāyām somaḥ prāṇe tu mārutaḥ rūpe ca api atha nakṣatram jihvāyām ca apaḥ eva tu saṃvatsarāḥ parvasu ca ākāśe daivamānuṣe prajñāyām varuṇaḥ ca eva cakṣuṣi ādityaḥ eva ca śarīre pṛthivī ca eva pādayoḥ viṣṇuḥ eva ca mūrdhni ca eva diśaḥ prāhuḥ buddhau brahmā pratiṣṭhitaḥ budhyamānaḥ syāt adhiṣṭhātā tu śaṃkara abuddhaḥ ca abhavat tasmāt budhyamānāt na saṃśayaḥ ābhyām anyaḥ paraḥ buddhaḥ veda vādeṣu śaṃkara yad āśritāni deheṣu daivatāni pṛthak pṛthak sa sarva yajña yājibhyaḥ hi ātma yājī viśiṣyate tathā asya sthānam ākhyāsye pṛthaktvena iha śaṃkara pādābhyām vaiṣṇavam sthānam āpnoti viniyojanāt nābhyā ca vāruṇam sthānam stanābhyām tu bhuvaḥ labhet bāhubhyām vāsavam sthānam śrotrābhyām āpnuyāt diśaḥ ādityam cakṣuṣā sthānam mūrdhnā brahmaṇaḥ eva ca atha mūrdhasu yaḥ prāṇān dhārayeta samāhitaḥ buddhyā mānam avāpnoti dravya avastham na saṃśayaḥ vikāra puruṣaḥ tu eṣa budhyamānaḥ iti smṛtaḥ pañcaviṃśati tattvam tat procyate tatra saṃśayaḥ mahān ātmā mahādeva mahān atra adhitiṣṭhati adhiṣṭhānāt adhiṣṭhātā procyate śāstra darśanāt avyaktasya eva sādharmyam etat āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ yoga kṛtyam tu tāvat me tvam nibodha anupūrvaśas śūnya āgāreṣu araṇyeṣu sāgare vā guhāsu vā viṣṭambhayitvā trīn daṇḍān avāptaḥ hi advayaḥ bhavet prāc mukha udak mukhaḥ vā api tathā paścāt mukhaḥ api vā dakṣiṇā vadanaḥ vā api baddhvā vidhivat āsanam svastikena upasaṃviṣṭaḥ kāyam unnāmya bhāsvaram yathā upadiṣṭam guruṇā tathā tat brahma dhārayet mūtra utsarga purīṣābhyām āhāre ca samāhitaḥ śeṣa kālam tu yuñjīta manasā su samāhitaḥ ekāgraḥ tat vijānīyāt sarvam guhyatamam param manaḥ bhavati deveśa pāṣāṇam iva niścalam cetayānam acetam ca dīpyamānam sva tejasā aṅguṣṭha parva mātram tat naiḥśreyasam aninditam jyotiṣām jyotiṣam devam viṣṇum atyanta nirmalam nārāyaṇam aṇīyāṃsam īśvarāṇām adhīśvaram viśvatas paramam nityam viśvam nārāyaṇam prabhum a vijñāya nimajjanti lokāḥ saṃsāra sāgare yam dṛṣṭvā yatayaḥ tāta na śocanti gata jvarāḥ janma mṛtyu bhayāt muktāḥ tīrṇāḥ saṃsāra sāgaram aṇimā laghimā bhūmā prāptiḥ prākāmyam eva ca īśitvam ca vaśitvam ca yatra kāmāvasāyi tā etat aṣṭaguṇam yogam yogānām abhitas smṛtam dṛṣṭvā ātmānam nirātmānam aprameyam sanātanam te viśanti śarīrāṇi yogena anena bhāsvaram daitya deva manuṣyāṇām balena balavattamāḥ tattvataḥ param asti iti ke cid āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ ke cit āhuḥ param nā asti ye jñāna phalam āśritāḥ jñāna sthaḥ puruṣaḥ tu eṣa vikṛtaḥ svena varṇitaḥ yam dhyānena anupaśyanti nityam yoga parāyaṇāḥ nityam anyatamāḥ prāhuḥ jñānam paramakam smṛtam jñānam eva vinirmuktāḥ sāṃkhyāḥ gacchanti kevalam sanatkumāraḥ sāṃkhya jñānam pravakṣyāmi parisaṃkhyā nidarśanam etāvat etat sāṃkhyānām darśanam deva sattama avyaktam buddhi ahaṃkārau mahābhūtāni pañca ca manaḥ tathā viśeṣāḥ ca daśa ca eva indriyāṇi ca eṣā tattva caturviṃśat mahāpuruṣa saṃmitā samutpāṭya yathā avyaktāt jīvāḥ yānti punar punar ādiḥ eṣa mahān ātmā guṇānām iti naḥ prabho guṇa stha tvāt guṇam tu enam āhuḥ avyakta lakṣaṇam budhyamānaḥ hi ahaṃkārī nityam māna a prabodhanāt vimala udāra śīlaḥ syāt budhyamāna aprabuddhayoḥ draṣṭā bhavati abhoktā ca sattva mūrtiḥ ca nirguṇaḥ upekṣakaḥ śuciḥ tābhyām ubhābhyām ayutaḥ tathā aham eva sṛjati anyam dvitīyam loka sārathiḥ sarva bhāvaiḥ ahaṃkāraiḥ tṛtīyam sarga saṃjñitam tatas bhūtāni ahaṃkāram ahaṃkāraḥ manaḥ asṛjat tathā eva yajñe bhūteṣu viṣaya arthī punar punar indriyaiḥ saha śūla aṅka pañca pañcabhiḥ eva ca manaḥ veda na ca ātmānam ahaṃkāram prajāpatiḥ na veda vā api ahaṃkāraḥ buddhim buddhimatām vara ahaṃkāreṇa saṃyuktaḥ srotasi abhimukhaḥ sadā evam eṣa vikāra ātmā mahāpuruṣa saṃjñakaḥ saṃviśat rajanīm kṛtsnām niśā ante divasa āgame sa punar punar ātmānam na kuryāt ākṣipeta ca pañcaviṃśam mahādeva mahāpuruṣa vaikṛtam guṇān punar ca tān eva saḥ ātmanā ātmani nikṣipet avyaktasya saḥ ajñānasya tama utmanaḥ upekṣakaḥ śuciḥ vyagraḥ saḥ aliṅgaḥ saḥ avraṇaḥ amalaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ bhagavān āste buddhaḥ śuddhaḥ nirāmayaḥ etaiḥ eva vihīnam tu ke cid āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ evam eṣa vikāra ātmā budhyamānaḥ mahā bhuja te avyaktam śaṃkara āgāḍham muñcante śāstra buddhayaḥ teṣām evam tu vadatām śāstra artham sūkṣma darśinām sā eṣā trayī parā vidyā caturthī ānvīkṣikī smṛtā ajñāna bandhanāt muktāḥ tīrṇāḥ saṃsāra bandhanāt ajñāna sāgaram ghoram agādham tam asaṃjñakam eṣā vidyā tathā avidyā kathitā te mayā arthataḥ yasmin deyam ca no grāhyam sāṃkhyāḥ sāṃkhyam tathā eva ca tathā ca eka tva nānātvam akṣaram kṣaram eva ca nigadiṣyāmi deveśa vimokṣam trividham ca te yat ca kim cit śrutam na syāt dṛṣṭam na eva ca kim cana sanatkumāraḥ tat ca te saṃpravakṣyāmi ekāgraḥ śṛṇu tatparaḥ ariṣṭāni pravakṣyāmi tattvena śṛṇu tat bhavān madhye uttaratas tāta dakṣiṇā mukha niṣṭhitam vidyut saṃsthāna puruṣam yadi paśyeta mānavaḥ etat phalam ariṣṭasya śaṃkara āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ śuddha maṇḍalam ādityam a raśmim atha paśyataḥ jānīyāt chidrām candramasaḥ chāyām pādau api anupaśyataḥ saṃvatsareṇa jānīyāt deha nyāsam upasthitam kanīnikāyām aśiraḥ puruṣam yadi paśyati jānīyāt ṣaṭsu māseṣu deha nyāsam upasthitam karṇau pidhāya hastābhyām śabdam na śṛṇuyāt yadi sarva aṅga dhāraṇa avasthām dhārayeta samāhitaḥ atha nā icchet ciram kālam tyajet ātmānam ātmanā atha tam nirvikāram tu prakṛteḥ paramam śuciḥ puruṣam tena sādharmyam deha nyāsam upāśnuyāt jāgrataḥ hi mayā uktāni tava ariṣṭāni tattvataḥ dhāraṇāt ca eva sarva aṅge mṛtyum jīyāt surarṣabha ekatva darśanam bhūyas nānātvam ca nibodha me akṣaram ca kṣaram ca eva catuṣṭaya vidhānataḥ avyakta ādīni tattvāni sarvāṇi eva mahā dyute āhuḥ caturviṃśatimam vikāram puruṣa anvitam ekatva darśanam ca eva nānātvena avaram smṛtam pañcaviṃśati vargaḥ syāt apavargaḥ ajara amaraḥ saḥ eva pañcaviṃśaḥ tu vikāraḥ puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ vikārāḥ vidyamānāḥ tu tattva saṃjñakaḥ ucyate yadi eṣaḥ avyakta tām nā eti vyatirekāt na saṃśayaḥ vikāra guṇa saṃtyāgāt prakṛti anya tvataḥ śuciḥ tadā nānātva tām eti sarga hīnaḥ apavarga bhāj akṣaraḥ ca bhavati eṣa yathā vā cyutavān kṣaṇāt avyaktā vyaktiḥ uktā syāt nirguṇasya guṇa ākarāt vyākhyātam tava śūla aṅka tathā ariṣṭāni ca eva hi vimokṣa lakṣaṇam śeṣam tat api iha bravīmi te yam jñātvā yatayaḥ prāptāḥ kevala tvam anāmayam sāṃkhyāḥ ca api atha yogāḥ ca dagdha paṅkāḥ gata jvarāḥ amūrti tvam anuprāptā nirguṇā nirbhayā bhava vipāpmānaḥ mahādeva muktāḥ saṃsāra sāgarāt saraṇe prajana ādāne guṇānām prakṛtiḥ sadā parā pramattā satatam etāvat kārya kāraṇam vyatiriktaḥ ca śuddhaḥ ca saḥ anyaḥ ca aprakṛtiḥ tathā upekṣakaḥ ca prakṛteḥ vikāra puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ vikāra puruṣeṇa eṣā saṃyuktā sṛjate jagat matsya udakāt sa saṃjñātaḥ prakṛteḥ eva karmaṇaḥ evam eṣa hi asat sat ca vikāra puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ vikāra āpadyamānam tu vikṛtim pravadanti naḥ yadā tu eṣa vikārasya prakṛtāni iti manyate tadā vikāra tām eti vikāra anya tva tām vrajet a yuktam niṣkalam śuddham avyaya ajaram avraṇam udumbarāt vā maśakaḥ pralayāt nirgataḥ yathā tathā avyaktasya saṃtyāgāt nirmamaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ ākāśe iva niḥsaṅgaḥ tathā asaṅgaḥ tathā varaḥ ādisargaḥ mahā bāho tāmasena āvṛtam param sanakaḥ sanandanaḥ ca eva tṛtīyaḥ ca sanātanaḥ avyaktāt paramāt sūkṣmāt avraṇāt mūrti saṃjñakāt kṣetrajñaḥ bhagavān āste nārāyaṇa parāyaṇaḥ asmākam sahajātānām utpannam jñānam uttamam punar punar prajātā vai tatra tatra pinākadhṛk kapilāt mūlaācāryāt tattva buddhi viniścayam tena saṃbodhitāḥ śiṣyāḥ bahavaḥ tattva darśinaḥ tat buddhvā bahavaḥ śiṣyāḥ mayā api etat nidarśitāḥ janma mṛtyu haram tathyam jñānam jñeyam sanātanam yat jñātvā na anuśocanti tattva jñānāt nirindriyāḥ śuddha bīja malāḥ ca eva vipaṅkāḥ vai nirakṣarāḥ svatantrāḥ te svatantreṇa saṃmitāḥ niṣkalāḥ smṛtāḥ śāśvatāḥ ca avyayāḥ ca eva tamaḥ grāhyāḥ ca bhāsvarāḥ vipāpmānaḥ tathā sarve sattva sthāḥ ca api nirvraṇāḥ vimuktāḥ kevalāḥ ca eva vīta moha bhayāḥ tathā amūrtāḥ te mahābhāga sarve ca vigata jvarāḥ hiraṇyanābhaḥ triśirāḥ tathā prahrāda bhāskarau vasuḥ viśvāvasuḥ ca eva sārdham pañcaśikhaḥ tathā ke cit muktāḥ sthitāḥ ke cid chandataḥ ca apare mṛtāḥ darśitāḥ trividham bandham vimokṣam trividham tathā ajñānam ca eva rāgaḥ ca saṃyogam prākṛtam tathā etebhyaḥ bandhanam proktam vimokṣam api me śṛṇu paritas tāvatā samyak saṃbandhaḥ yāvatā kṛtaḥ kṛtsna kṣaya parityāgāt vimokṣaḥ iti naḥ śrutiḥ bhīṣmaḥ nivṛttaḥ sarva saṅgebhyaḥ kevalaḥ puruṣaḥ amalaḥ iti evam uktvā bhagavān īśvarāya mahātmane sanatkumāraḥ prayayau ākāśam samupāśritaḥ nārāyaṇe atha rudre vā bhaktiḥ tīrtham param matam yudhiṣṭhiraḥ sarveṣām upavāsānām yat śreyaḥ su mahat phalam bhīṣmaḥ śṛṇu rājan yathā gītam svayam eva svayaṃbhuvā yat kṛtvā nirvṛtaḥ bhūyāt puruṣaḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ dvādaśyām mārgaśīrṣe tu ahorātreṇa keśavam arcya aśvamedham āpnoti duṣkṛtam ca asya naśyati tathā eva pauṣa māse tu pūjya nārāyaṇa iti ca ahorātreṇa dvādaśyām māgha māse tu mādhavam ahorātreṇa dvādaśyām caitre viṣṇuḥ iti smaran vaiśākha māse dvādaśyām pūjayan madhusūdanam ahorātreṇa dvādaśyām jyeṣṭhe māsi trivikramam āṣāḍhe māsi dvādaśyām vāmana iti ca pūjayet ahorātreṇa dvādaśyām śrāvaṇe māsi śrīdharam pañcayajñān avāpnoti vimāna sthaḥ sa modate tathā bhādrapade māsi hṛṣīkeśa iti pūjayan sautrāmaṇim avāpnoti pūta ātmā bhavate ca hi dvādaśyām āśvine māsi padmanābha iti ca arcayan go sahasra phalam puṇyam prāpnuyāt na atra saṃśayaḥ dvādaśyām kārttike māsi pūjya dāmodara iti ca gavām yajñam avāpnoti pumān strī vā na saṃśayaḥ abhyarcya puṇḍarīkākṣam evam saṃvatsaram tu yaḥ atas param na upavāsaḥ bhavati iti viniścayaḥ uvāca bhagavān viṣṇuḥ svayam eva purātanam vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ śara talpa gatam bhīṣmam vṛddham kuru pitāmaham upagamya mahā prājñaḥ paryapṛcchat yudhiṣṭhiraḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ jñānam rūpam ca saubhāgyam priya tvam ca katham bhavet bhīṣmaḥ dharma artha kāma saṃyuktaḥ sukha bhāgī katham bhavet mārgaśīrṣasya māsasya candre mūlena saṃyute pādau mūlena rāja indra jaṅghāyām atha rohiṇīm aśvinyām sakthinī ca eva ūrū ca āṣāḍhayoḥ tathā guhyam tu phālgunī vidyāt kṛttikā kaṭikāḥ tathā nābhim bhādrapade vidyāt revatyām akṣi maṇḍalam pṛṣṭham eva dhaniṣṭhāsu anurādhā uttarāḥ tathā bāhubhyām tu viśākhāsu hastau hasteṣu nirdiśet punarvasu aṅgulī rājan āśleṣāsu nakhāḥ tathā grīvā jyeṣṭhā ca rāja indra śravaṇena tu karṇayoḥ mukham puṣyeṇa rāja indra danta uṣṭhau svātiḥ ucyate hastam śatabhiṣām ca eva maghām ca eva atha nāsikām netre mṛgaśiraḥ vidyāt lalāṭam mitram eva tu bharaṇyām tu śiraḥ vidyāt keśān ārdrām narādhipa samāpte tu ghṛtam dadyāt brāhmaṇe vedapārage subhagaḥ darśanīyaḥ ca jñānī bhogī ca jāyate jāyate paripūrṇa aṅgaḥ paurṇamāsyām iva uḍurāj tatas asya karma paśyanti śubham vā yadi vā aśubham devatāḥ pañcabhūta sthāḥ kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi bhoga vaśyam karma vaśyam yātanā vaśyam iti api etat trayāṇām āsādya karmataḥ saḥ aśnute phalam iha sthānāni puṇyāni deva tulyāni bhū tale kukkuṭaḥ pañca varṣāṇi pañca varṣāṇi jambukaḥ gardabha tvam tu saṃprāpya daśa varṣāṇi jīvati saṃvatsaram tu kumbhīraḥ tatas jāyati mānavaḥ putrasya mātā pitarau yasya ruṣṭau ubhau api tatas śvā ca api bhavati tatas bhavati mānuṣaḥ asūyakaḥ kutsitaḥ ca caṇḍālaḥ duḥkham aśnute mūṣakaḥ jāyate paścāt sūkaraḥ jāyate tatas śvā biḍālaḥ kukkuṭaḥ ca jāyeta api pipīlikāḥ vilāḷaḥ pañca varṣāṇi pañca varṣāṇi kukkuṭaḥ śvā bhūtvā sapta janmāni tatas jāyati mānavaḥ dvija grāma ādhipatyam hi kṛtvā brahma sva bhakṣakaḥ śvāna yoni śatam prāpya caṇḍālaḥ puṣkalaḥ tatas varṣāt pañcadaśāt pūrvam kramāt bhavati mānuṣaḥ anyāḥ ca yātanāḥ prāpya kṛmi yonau prajāyate mṛgaḥ bhūtvā tatas śastraiḥ vadhyate bhṛśa dāruṇam daśan vai mānuṣān nityam pāpa ātmā sa viśām pate ghṛtam hṛtvā tu durbuddhiḥ kākamadguḥ prajāyate matsya māṃsam atho hṛtvā kākaḥ jāyati durmatiḥ phalāni vānaraḥ hṛtvā apūpam hṛtvā pipīlikaḥ corayitvā tu niṣpāvam jāyate hala golakaḥ kauśikam tu tatas hṛtvā naraḥ jāyati vartakaḥ aṃśukam corayitvā tu śukaḥ jāyati mānavaḥ corayitvā dukūlam tu mṛtaḥ haṃsaḥ prajāyate sarpaḥ kārpāsakam hṛtvā kṣaumam hṛtvā śaśaḥ bhavet chāgaḥ ca api āvikam hṛtvā kūrmaḥ carma apahārakaḥ tatra jīvati varṣāṇi tatas daśa ca pañca ca adharmasya kṣayam gatvā tatas jāyati mānuṣaḥ karmaṇā ca kṛtena iha kena yānti śubhām gatim tathā tathā śarīram tu tena adharmeṇa mucyate yadi vyāharate rājan viprāṇām dharma vādinām suvarṇa dānāt pāpāni naśyanti su bahūni api dattvā vṛtti karīm bhūmim pātakena api mucyate pārāyaṇaiḥ ca vedānām mucyate pātakaiḥ dvijaḥ gāyatryāḥ ca eva lakṣeṇa go sahasrasya tarpaṇāt veda artham jñāpayitvā tu śuddhān viprān yathārthataḥ sarva ātithyam param hi eṣām tasmāt annam param smṛtam śūdra annam nā eva bhoktavyam vipraiḥ dharma parāyaṇaiḥ hiṃsām tu na upayuñjīta tathā hiṃsā caturvidhā kāye manasi vākye ca doṣāḥ hi ete prakīrtitāḥ māṃsam moha samāviṣṭaḥ puruṣaḥ saḥ adhamaḥ smṛtaḥ hiṃsām kṛtvā avaśaḥ pāpaḥ bhūyiṣṭham jāyate tathā pareṣām dhana dhānyānām hiṃsakāḥ stāvakāḥ tathā praśaṃsakāḥ ca māṃsasya nityam svarge bahiṣkṛtāḥ bhasma viṣṭhā kṛmiḥ vā api niṣṭhā yasya īdṛśī dhruvā sa kāyaḥ para pīḍābhiḥ katham dhāryaḥ vipaścitā a viśvāsyaḥ avasīdet saḥ iti ha uvāca nāradaḥ vikīrṇa kaṇṭakena api tṛṇa praspandane bhayam mṛtyutaḥ bhayam asti iti śaṅkāyām duḥkham uttaram rākṣasa indra bhayāt muktāḥ sarva bhūta parāyaṇāḥ udyateṣu ca śastreṣu mṛga vyāla bhayeṣu ca śaraṇyaḥ sarva bhūtānām viśvāsyaḥ sarva jantuṣu bhīṣmaḥ mārkaṇḍeyena yat ca uktam śṛṇu me paramam vacaḥ yudhiṣṭhira mahā bāho ye doṣāḥ māṃsa bhakṣaṇe vidhinā api avaidikena iti dāntānām iha niścayaḥ kṛtaḥ hi manasaḥ yajñaḥ tena devāḥ ca tarpitāḥ satyam vadata mā a satyam satyam dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ etaiḥ ca anyaiḥ ca rāja indra kṛpeṇa bharatena ca virūpākṣeṇa kurūṣeṇa nṛga alarka naraiḥ tathā imam dharmam a māṃsa ādam yaḥ caret śrāvayeta vā paṭhet vā yaḥ idam rājan śṛṇuyāt vā api abhīkṣṇaśas a māṃsa bhakṣaṇa vidhim pavitram ṛṣi pūjitam ahiṃsraḥ dāna śīlaḥ ca madhu māṃsa vivarjitaḥ yathā etat yādṛśam ca etat guṇāḥ ye ca asya varjane nāśaḥ bhakṣaṇa doṣasya dānam eva satām matam kṣudhitānām dvijānām ca sarveṣām ca eva jīvanam dattvā bhavati pūta ātmā śraddhayā lobha varjitaḥ śikṣayanti na yācante darśayantaḥ sva mūrtibhiḥ dāna ādyaiḥ su śuciḥ māṃsam punar nā eva sa bhakṣayet rakṣaṇa arthāya bhūtānām hiṃsrān hanyāt mṛgān punar agastyena purā rājan māṃsa bhakṣam tu jāṅgale tyaktvā akasmāt niśāyām ca na dattam abhayam mayā tena eṣā kṛmi tā prāptā bhayasya jananī sadā śakaṭa vrajaḥ ca su mahān āgataḥ ca yadṛcchayā cakra ākrameṇa bhinnaḥ ca kīṭaḥ prāṇān mumoca ha saṃbhūtaḥ kṣatriya kule prasādāt amita ojasaḥ dadarśa ca mahā rāja saṃprāptam rāja putra tām etāsu eva krameṇa eva yoniṣu āsa sva karmaṇā paryāyeṇa tatas prāpya kṣātram sthānam anuttamam pālanāt ca viśiṣṭānām su yuddha maraṇena ca śiṣṭa saṃsevanāt ca eva sa gatim paramām gataḥ tatas sa para rāṣṭreṇa rājānam pīḍitāḥ prajāḥ ājagāma tatas prājñaḥ kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaḥ tadā pārāśaryam mahā prājñam upayāntam dayā param ajñāna timira ādityam nityam vyāsam namāmi aham iti uktvā patitam padbhyām prītyā prāha mahā muniḥ brāhmaṇān pūjayasva iha japa satya parāyaṇaḥ yajña dāna tapaḥ śīlāḥ narāḥ vai puṇya karmiṇaḥ ye abhidruhyanti bhūtāni te vai pāpa kṛtaḥ janāḥ nityam ca akṛpaṇaḥ bhuṅkte sva janaiḥ dehi yācitaḥ bhāgya kṣayeṇa kṣīyante na upabhogena saṃcayāḥ andham syāt tamaḥ eva idam na prajñāyeta kim cana cāturvarṇyam na varteta dharma adharmau ṛta anṛte a saṃskārāt kṣatra vaiśyau naśyete brāhmaṇāt ṛte śūdraḥ naśyati a śuśrūṣuḥ āśramāṇām yathārhataḥ yaḥ tārayati vai vidvān pitṝn devān samādṛtān tat tat bahu guṇam dānam śraddhayā sva guṇaiḥ api brāhmaṇaḥ sa mahābhāga taret saṃsāra sāgarāt vidyayā gata mohānām tāraṇāya hariḥ smṛtaḥ tad bhaktāḥ na vinaśyanti hi aṣṭa akṣara parāyaṇāḥ praṇava upāsana parāḥ parama artha parāḥ tu iha etaiḥ pāvaya ca ātmānam bhīṣmaḥ agni saṃrakṣaṇa parā gṛha śuddhim ca kāraye pātrāṇām dhana dhānyānām śayana āsana vastunām kumārān pālaye nityam kumārīḥ pariśikṣaye ātma priyāṇi hitvā api garbha saṃrakṣaṇe ratā bālānām varjaye nityam śāpam kopam pratāpanam a vikṣiptāni dhānyāni na anna vikṣepaṇam gṛhe samudgamya ca śuddhā aham bhikṣām dadyām dvijātiṣu yudhiṣṭhiraḥ yat jñeyam paramam kṛtyam anuṣṭheyam mahātmabhiḥ bhīṣmaḥ sāram me sarva śāstrāṇām vaktum arhasi anugrahāt śrūyatām idam atyantam gūḍham saṃsāra mocanam śrotavyam ca tvayā samyak jñātavyam ca viśām pate puṇḍarīkaḥ purā vipraḥ puṇya tīrthe japa anvitaḥ nāradaḥ ca abravīt enam brahmaṇā uktam mahātmanā śṛṇuṣva avahitaḥ tāta jñāna yogam anuttamam aprabhūtam prabhūta artham veda śāstra artha sārakam yaḥ paraḥ prakṛteḥ proktaḥ puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ narāt jātāni tattvāni nārāṇi iti tatas viduḥ tāni eva ca ayanam tasya tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ nārāyaṇāt jagat sarvam sarga kāle prajāyate nārāyaṇaḥ param brahma tattvam nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ parāt api paraḥ ca asau tasmāt na asti parāt param vāsudevam tathā viṣṇum ātmānam ca tathā viduḥ saṃjñā bhedaiḥ saḥ eva ekaḥ sarva śāstra abhisaṃskṛtaḥ āloḍya sarva śāstrāṇi vicārya ca punar punar idam ekam su niṣpannam dhyeyaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ sadā tasmāt tvam gahanān sarvān tyaktvā śāstra artha vistarān an anya cetāḥ dhyāyasva nārāyaṇam ajam vibhum muhūrtam api yaḥ dhyāyet nārāyaṇam atandritaḥ namaḥ nārāyaṇāya iti yaḥ veda brahma śāśvatam śravaṇāt mananāt ca eva gīti stuti arcana ādibhiḥ ārādhyam sarvadā brahma puruṣeṇa hita eṣiṇā lipyate na sa pāpena nārāyaṇa parāyaṇaḥ punāti sakalam lokam sahasrāṃśuḥ iva uditaḥ keśava ārādhanam hitvā nā eva yāti parām gatim svapne api puṇḍarīkākṣam śaṅkha cakra gadā dharam tam dṛṣṭvā devadeveśam prāṇamat saṃbhrama anvitaḥ atha kālena mahatā tathā pratyakṣatām gataḥ gataḥ saṃpūjitaḥ sarvaiḥ sa yogi nilayaḥ hariḥ sa guṇam aguṇam ādyam sthūlam atyanta sūkṣmam tribhuvana gurum īśam tvam prapadyasva viṣṇum nūnam śaktyā samārambhān vihitān su mahā udayān vipanna arthān vijānāsi tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ eka dravya niviṣṭena tulya artha śruta saṃpadā paribhūtaḥ asi nūnam tvam tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ iṣṭa bhāryasya te nūnam prātiveśyaḥ mahādhanaḥ yuvā su lalitaḥ kāmī tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ su durvinītaḥ putraḥ vā jāmātā vā pramārjakaḥ dārāḥ vā pratikūlāḥ te tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ bhrātaraḥ atīva viṣamāḥ pitā vā kṣudh kṣataḥ mṛtaḥ mātā jyeṣṭhaḥ guruḥ vā api tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ vā hataḥ gauḥ vā brahma svam vā hṛtam purā deva svam vā adhikam kāle tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ hṛta dāraḥ atha vṛddhaḥ vā loke dviṣṭaḥ atha vā naraiḥ a vijñātena vā vṛddhaḥ tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ vārdhakya artham dhanam dṛṣṭvā svā śrīḥ vā api paraiḥ hṛtā vṛttiḥ vā durjana apekṣā tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ saṃpad kāle na te dharmaḥ kṣīṇaḥ tāta suhṛd bruvaiḥ a saṃnyāsam atas tatra tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ parokṣa vādibhiḥ mithyā doṣaḥ te saṃpradarśitaḥ alam arthayase hīnān tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ śreyaḥ arthinā daridreṇa kim kartavyam pitāmaha dānānām uttamam yat ca deyam yat ca yathā yathā vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ mānyān pūjyān ca gāṅgeya rahasyam vaktum arhasi evam pṛṣṭaḥ narendreṇa pāṇḍavena yaśasvinā bhīṣmaḥ dharmāṇām paramam guhyam bhīṣmaḥ provāca pārthivam śṛṇuṣva avahitaḥ rājan dharma guhyāni bhārata yathā hi bhagavān vyāsaḥ purā kathitavān mayi deva guhyam idam rājan yamena akliṣṭa karmaṇā ṛṣayaḥ pramathāḥ śrīḥ ca citraguptaḥ diśām gajāḥ ṛṣi dharmaḥ smṛtaḥ yatra sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ mahā dāna phalam ca eva sarva yajña phalam tathā yaḥ ca etat evam jānīyāt jñātvā vā kurute anagha sa doṣaḥ doṣavān ca iha taiḥ guṇaiḥ saha yujyate daśa sūnā samam cakram daśa cakra samaḥ dhvajaḥ daśa dhvaja samāḥ veśyāḥ daśa veśyā samaḥ nṛpaḥ ardhena etāni sarvāṇi nṛpatiḥ kathyate adhikaḥ trivarga sahitam śāstram pavitram puṇya lakṣaṇam dharma vyākaraṇam puṇyam rahasya śravaṇam mahat śrotavyam dharma saṃyuktam vihitam tridaśaiḥ svayam pitṝṇām yatra guhyāni procyante śrāddha karmaṇi devatānām ca sarveṣām rahasyam kathyate akhilam ṛṣi dharmaḥ smṛtaḥ yatra sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ mahāyajña phalam ca eva sarva dāna phalam tathā ye paṭhanti sadā martyāḥ yeṣām ca eva upatiṣṭhati śrutvā ca phalam ācaṣṭe svayam nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ gavām phalam tīrtha phalam yajñānām ca eva yat phalam śrotāraḥ śraddadhānāḥ ca yeṣām śuddham ca mānasam teṣām vyaktam jitāḥ lokāḥ śraddadhānena sādhunā yau tau kāma guṇa upetau aśvinau bhiṣajām varau ājñayā aham tayoḥ prāptaḥ sa narān pitṛ devatān kimartham ca trayaḥ piṇḍāḥ pravibhaktāḥ pṛthak pṛthak śraddadhānena dūtena bhāṣitam dharma saṃhitam pūrva sthāḥ tridaśāḥ sarve pitaraḥ pūjya khecaram svāgatam te astu bhadram te śrūyatām khecara uttama gūḍha arthaḥ paramaḥ praśnaḥ bhavatā samudīritaḥ śrāddham dattvā ca bhuktvā ca puruṣaḥ yaḥ striyam vrajet pravibhāgam tu piṇḍānām pravakṣyāmi anupūrvaśas piṇḍaḥ hi adhastāt gacchan tu apaḥ āviśya bhāvayet piṇḍam tu madhyamam tatra patnī tu ekā samaśnute piṇḍaḥ tṛtīyaḥ yaḥ teṣām tam dadyāt jātavedasi pitaraḥ tasya tuṣyanti prahṛṣṭa manasaḥ sadā deva dūtaḥ prajāḥ vivardhate ca asya akṣayam ca upatiṣṭhati ānupūrvyeṇa piṇḍānām pravibhāgaḥ pṛthak pṛthak pitṝṇām triṣu sarveṣām niruktam kathitam tvayā kam vā prīṇayate devam katham tārayate pitṝn madhyamam tu tadā patnī bhuṅkte anujñātam eva hi etat icchāmi aham śrotum piṇḍeṣu triṣu yā gatiḥ phalam vṛttim ca mārgam ca yaḥ ca enam pratipadyate su mahān eṣa praśnaḥ vai yaḥ tvayā samudīritaḥ rahasyam adbhutam ca api pṛṣṭāḥ sma gaganecara te api evam na abhijānanti pitṛ kārya viniścayam trayāṇām api piṇḍānām śrutvā bhagavataḥ gatim deva dūtena yaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ śrāddhasya vidhi niścayaḥ gatiḥ trayāṇām piṇḍānām śṛṇuṣva avahitaḥ mama apaḥ gacchati yaḥ hi atra śaśinam hi eṣa prīṇayet śaśī prīṇayate devān pitṝn ca eva mahāmate bhuṅkte tu patnī yam ca eṣām anujñātā tu madhyamam putra kāmāya putram tu prayacchanti pitāmahāḥ pitaraḥ tena tṛpyanti prītāḥ kāmān diśanti ca etat te kathitam sarvam triṣu piṇḍeṣu yā gatiḥ śucinā tu sadā śrāddham bhoktavyam khecara uttama ye mayā kathitāḥ doṣāḥ te tathā syuḥ na ca anyathā tasmāt snātaḥ śuciḥ kṣāntaḥ śrāddham bhuñjīta vai dvijaḥ tatas vidyutprabhaḥ nāma ṛṣiḥ āha mahā tapāḥ āditya tejasā tasya tulyam rūpam prakāśate kīṭān pipīlikān sarpān meṣān sa mṛga pakṣiṇaḥ kilbiṣam su bahu prāptāḥ kim svid eṣām pratikriyā tatas deva gaṇāḥ sarve ṛṣayaḥ ca tapodhanāḥ śakraḥ pitaraḥ ca mahābhāgāḥ pūjayanti sma tam munim kurukṣetram gayām gaṅgām prabhāsam puṣkarāṇi ca tathā mucyati pāpena rāhuṇā candramāḥ yathā tri aham snātaḥ sa bhavati nirāhāraḥ ca vartate spṛśate yaḥ gavām pṛṣṭham vāladhim ca namasyati ghṛṣṭaḥ vaṭa kaṣāyeṇa anuliptaḥ priyaṅguṇā kṣīreṇa ṣaṣṭikān bhuktvā sarva pāpaiḥ pramucyate śrūyatām ca aparam guhyam rahasyam ṛṣi cintitam śrutam me bhāṣamāṇasya sthāṇoḥ sthāne bṛhaspateḥ parvata ārohaṇam kṛtvā ekapādaḥ vibhāvasum nirīkṣeta nirāhāraḥ ūrdhva bāhuḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ grīṣma kāle atha vā śīte evam pāpam apohati tatas pāpāt pramuktasya dyutiḥ bhavati śāśvatī dharma guhyam tu bhagavān mānuṣāṇām sukha āvaham bṛhaspatiḥ sa rahasyāḥ ca ye doṣāḥ tān yathāvat udīraya bāla vatsām ca ye dhenum duhanti kṣīra kāraṇāt teṣām doṣān pravakṣyāmi tān nibodha śacīpate bhānumān anilaḥ ca eva havyavāhaḥ ca vāsava lokānām mātaraḥ ca eva gāvaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayaṃbhuvā lokān tārayitum śaktāḥ martyeṣu eteṣu devatāḥ varṣāṇi ṣaḍaśītim tu durvṛttāḥ kula pāṃsanāḥ striyaḥ sarvāḥ ca durvṛttāḥ pratimehanti yāḥ ravim anila dveṣiṇaḥ śakra garbha sthā cyavate prajā agni kāryeṣu vai teṣām havyam na aśnāti pāvakaḥ kṣīram tu bāla vatsānām ye pibanti iha mānavāḥ na teṣām kṣīrapāḥ ke cit jāyante kula vardhanāḥ prajā kṣayeṇa yujyante kula vaṃśa kṣayeṇa ca evam etat purā dṛṣṭam kula vṛddhaiḥ dvijātibhiḥ tasmāt varjyāni varjyāni kāryam kāryam ca nityaśas tatas sarve mahābhāgāḥ devatāḥ sa marut gaṇāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ca mahābhāgāḥ pṛcchanti sma pitṝn tatas ānṛṇyam vā katham martyāḥ gaccheyuḥ kena karmaṇā etat icchāmahe śrotum param kautūhalam hi naḥ nyāyataḥ vai mahābhāgāḥ saṃśayaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ śrūyatām yena tuṣyāmaḥ martyānām sādhu karmaṇām nīla ṣaṇḍa pramokṣeṇa amāvāsyām tila udakaiḥ varṣāsu dīpakaiḥ ca eva pitṝṇām anṛṇaḥ bhavet akṣayam nirvyalīkam ca dānam etat mahā phalam asmākam paritoṣaḥ ca akṣayaḥ parikīrtyate śraddadhānāḥ ca ye martyāḥ āhariṣyanti saṃtatim durgāt te tārayiṣyanti narakāt prapitāmahān pitṝṇām bhāṣitam śrutvā hṛṣṭa romā tapodhanaḥ vṛddhagārgyaḥ mahā tejāḥ tān evam vākyam abravīt ke guṇāḥ nīla ṣaṇḍasya pramuktasya tapodhanāḥ nīlaṣaṇḍasya lāṅgūlam toyam abhyuddharet yadi ṣaṣṭim varṣa sahasrāṇi pitaraḥ tena tarpitāḥ yaḥ tu śṛṅga gatam paṅkam kūlāt uddhṛtya tiṣṭhati varṣāsu dīpa dānena śaśi vat śobhate naraḥ tamaḥ rūpam na tasya asti dīpakam yaḥ prayacchati kṛtam bhavati taiḥ śrāddham sa rahasyam yathārthavat hṛṣṭa puṣṭa manāḥ teṣām prajā bhavati nityadā śraddadhānaḥ tu yaḥ kuryāt pitṝṇām anṛṇaḥ bhavet evam eṣa samuddiṣṭaḥ śrāddha kāla kramaḥ tathā bhīṣmaḥ iti pṛṣṭaḥ surendreṇa provāca hariḥ avyayaḥ brāhmaṇānām parīvādaḥ mama vidveṣaṇam mahat brāhmaṇaiḥ pūjitaiḥ nityam pūjitaḥ aham na saṃśayaḥ nitya abhivādyāḥ vipra indrāḥ bhuktvā pādau tathā ātmanaḥ teṣām tuṣyāmi martyānām yaḥ cakre ca balim haret vāmanam brāhmaṇam dṛṣṭvā varāham ca jala utthitam uddhṛtām dharaṇīm ca eva mūrdhnā dhārayate tu yaḥ na teṣām aśubham kim cit kalmaṣam ca upapadyate aśvattham rocanām gām ca pūjayet yaḥ naraḥ sadā tena rūpeṇa teṣām ca pūjām gṛhṇāmi tattvataḥ pūjā mama eṣā na asti anyā yāvat lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ anyathā hi vṛthā martyāḥ pūjayanti alpabuddhayaḥ indraḥ na aham tat pratigṛhṇāmi na sā tuṣṭi karī mama cakram pādau varāham ca brāhmaṇam ca api vāmanam uddhṛtām dharaṇīm ca eva kimartham tvam praśaṃsasi bhavān sṛjati bhūtāni bhavān saṃharati prajāḥ bhīṣmaḥ prakṛtiḥ sarva bhūtānām sa martyānām sanātanī cakreṇa nihatāḥ daityāḥ padbhyām krāntā vasuṃdharā vārāham rūpam āsthāya uddhṛtā ca vasuṃdharā vāmanam rūpam āsthāya jitaḥ rājā mayā baliḥ parituṣṭaḥ bhavāmi evam mānuṣāṇām mahātmanām tat mām ye pūjayiṣyanti na asti teṣām parābhavaḥ api vā brāhmaṇam dṛṣṭvā brahmacāriṇam āgatam brāhmaṇāya āhutim dattvā amṛtam tasya bhojanam aindrīm saṃdhyām upāsitvā āditya abhimukhaḥ sthitaḥ saṃśayam pṛcchamānānām kim bhūyas kathayāmi aham śrūyatām paramam guhyam mānuṣāṇām sukha āvaham a jānantaḥ yat abudhāḥ
