kliśyante bhūta pīḍitāḥ kalye utthāya yaḥ martyaḥ spṛśet gām vai ghṛtam dadhi bhūtāni ca eva sarvāṇi agratas pṛṣṭhatas api vā ucchiṣṭam vā api chidreṣu varjayanti tapodhanāḥ devāḥ pragṛhya audumbaram pātram toya pūrṇam udak mukhaḥ upavāsam tu gṛhṇīyāt yat vā saṃkalpayet vratam upavāse balau ca api tāmra pātram viśiṣyate baliḥ bhikṣā tathā arghyam ca pitṝṇām ca tila udakam guhyam etat samuddiṣṭam yathā tuṣyanti devatāḥ rāja pauruṣike vipre ghāṇṭike paricārake gorakṣake vāṇijake tathā kāru kuśīlave mitra druhi an adhīyāne yaḥ ca syāt vṛṣalī patiḥ eteṣu daivam pitryam vā na deyam syāt katham cana piṇḍa dāḥ tasya hīyante na ca prīṇāti vai pitṝn agniḥ pādam udyamya yaḥ martyaḥ spṛśet gāḥ ca su durmatiḥ brāhmaṇam vā mahābhāgam dīpyamānam tathā analam tasya doṣān pravakṣyāmi tat śṛṇudhvam samāhitāḥ vaimanasyam ca devānām kṛtam bhavati puṣkalam pāvakaḥ ca mahā tejāḥ havyam na pratigṛhṇati ājanmanām śatam ca eva narake pacyate tu saḥ tasmāt gāvaḥ na pādena spraṣṭavyāḥ vai kadā cana brāhmaṇaḥ ca mahā tejāḥ dīpyamānaḥ tathā analaḥ viśvāmitraḥ ete doṣāḥ mayā proktāḥ triṣu yaḥ pādam utsṛjet śrūyatām paramam guhyam rahasyam dharma saṃhitam paramānnena yaḥ dadyāt pitṝṇām aupahārikam gaja chāyāyām pūrvasyām kutupe dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ śrūyatām tasya dānasya yādṛśaḥ guṇa vistaraḥ kṛtam tena mahat śrāddham varṣāṇi iha trayodaśa gāvaḥ bhūyas ca yā viṣṇu pade sthitā yā vibhāvasoḥ ca api pathe sthitā yā vimucyate pāpa kṛtena karmaṇā etam hi mantram tridaśa abhijuṣṭam na tasya pāpam na bhayam na śokaḥ bhīṣmaḥ atha sapta mahābhāgāḥ ṛṣayaḥ loka viśrutāḥ vasiṣṭha pramukhāḥ sarve brahmāṇam padmasaṃbhavam pradakṣiṇam abhikramya sarve prāñjalayaḥ sthitāḥ uvāca vacanam teṣām vasiṣṭhaḥ brahma vittamaḥ sarva prāṇi hitam praśnam brahmakṣetre viśeṣataḥ dravya hīnāḥ katham martyāḥ daridrāḥ sādhu vṛttayaḥ prāpnuvanti iha yajñasya phalam kena iha karmaṇā aho praśnaḥ mahābhāgāḥ gūḍha arthaḥ paramaḥ śubhaḥ sūkṣmaḥ śreyān ca martyānām bhavadbhiḥ samudāhṛtaḥ śrūyatām sarvam ākhyāsye nikhilena tapodhanāḥ yathā yajña phalam martyaḥ labhate na atra saṃśayaḥ pauṣa māsasya śukle vai yadā yujyeta rohiṇī eka vastraḥ śuciḥ snātaḥ śraddadhānaḥ samāhitaḥ somasya raśmayaḥ pītvā mahāyajña phalam labhet vibhāvasuḥ yat mām bhavantaḥ pṛcchanti sūkṣma tattva artha darśinaḥ salilasya añjalim pūrṇam akṣatāḥ ca ghṛta uttarāḥ somasya uttiṣṭhamānasya tat jalam ca akṣatān ca tān agni kāryam kṛtam tena hutāḥ ca asya agnayaḥ trayaḥ vanaspatim ca yaḥ hanyāt amāvāsyām abuddhimān dantakāṣṭham tu yaḥ khādet amāvāsyām abuddhimān śrīḥ prakīrṇam bhājanam yatra pāna bhāṇḍam atha āsanam devatāḥ pitaraḥ ca eva utsave parvaṇīṣu vā nirāśāḥ pratigacchanti kaśmala upahatāt gṛhāt aṅgirāḥ yaḥ tu saṃvatsaram pūrṇam dadyāt dīpam karañjake gārgyaḥ ātithyam satatam kuryāt dīpam dadyāt pratiśraye varjayānaḥ divāsvapnam na ca māṃsāni bhakṣayet go brāhmaṇam na hiṃsyāt ca puṣkarāṇi ca kīrtayet eṣa śreṣṭhatamaḥ dharmaḥ sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ api kratu śataiḥ iṣṭvā kṣayam gacchati tat haviḥ na tu kṣīyanti te dharmāḥ śraddadhānaiḥ prayojitāḥ rajasvalā ca yā nārī śvitrikā putrikā ca yā etābhiḥ cakṣuṣā dṛṣṭam haviḥ na aśnanti devatāḥ pitaraḥ ca na tuṣyanti varṣāṇi asya trayodaśa śukla vāsāḥ śuciḥ bhūtvā brāhmaṇān svasti vācayet kīrtayet bhāratam ca eva tathā syāt akṣayam haviḥ bhinna bhāṇḍam ca khaṭvām ca kukkuṭam śunakam tathā a praśastāni sarvāṇi yaḥ ca vṛkṣaḥ gṛheruhaḥ bhinna bhāṇḍe kalim prāhuḥ khaṭvāyām tu dhana kṣayaḥ kukkuṭe śunake ca eva haviḥ na aśnanti devatāḥ vṛkṣa mūle dhruvam sattvam tasmāt vṛkṣam na ropayet avākśirāḥ vā lambeta satram vā sphītam āharet na ca asya hṛdayam śuddham narakam sa dhruvam vrajet tulyam yajñaḥ ca satyam ca hṛdayasya ca śuddha tā vāyuḥ kim cit dharmam pravakṣyāmi mānuṣāṇām sukha āvaham sa rahasyāḥ ca ye doṣāḥ tān śṛṇudhvam samāhitāḥ dīpakaḥ ca api kartavyaḥ pitṝṇām sa tila udakaḥ etena vidhinā martyaḥ śraddadhānaḥ samāhitaḥ caturaḥ vārṣikān māsān yaḥ dadāti tila udakam bhojanam ca yathāśaktyā brāhmaṇe vedapārage agneḥ tu vṛṣalaḥ netā haviḥ mūḍhāḥ ca yoṣitaḥ gavām mūtra purīṣeṇa payasā ca ghṛtena ca agnikāryam tri aham kuryāt nirāhāraḥ samāhitaḥ tatas saṃvatsare pūrṇe pratigṛhṇanti devatāḥ hṛṣyanti pitaraḥ ca asya śrāddha kāle upasthite lomaśaḥ martyānām svarga kāmānām pretya svarga sukha āvahaḥ paradāreṣu ye saktāḥ a kṛtvā dāra saṃgraham nirāśāḥ pitaraḥ teṣām śrāddha kāle bhavanti vai para dāra ratiḥ yaḥ syāt yaḥ ca vandhyām upāsate a saṃbhāṣyāḥ bhavanti ete pitṝṇām na atra saṃśayaḥ devatāḥ pitaraḥ ca eṣām na abhinandanti tat haviḥ tasmāt parasya vai dārān tyajet vandhyām ca yoṣitam śrūyatām ca aparam guhyam rahasyam dharma saṃhitam śraddadhānena kartavyam gurūṇām vacanam sadā dvādaśyām paurṇamāsyām ca māsi māsi ghṛta akṣatam aśvamedha catur bhāgam phalam sṛjati vāsavaḥ dānena etena tejasvī vīryavān ca bhavet naraḥ prītaḥ ca bhagavān somaḥ iṣṭān kāmān prayacchati śrūyatām ca aparaḥ dharmaḥ sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ imam kali yugam prāpya manuṣyāṇām sukha āvahaḥ kalyam utthāya yaḥ martyaḥ snātaḥ śuklena vāsasā tila pātram prayaccheta brāhmaṇebhyaḥ samāhitaḥ tila udakam ca yaḥ dadyāt pitṝṇām madhunā saha dīpakam kṛsaram ca eva śrūyatām tasya tat phalam tila pātre phalam prāha bhagavān pākaśāsanaḥ go pradānam ca yaḥ kuryāt bhūmi dānam ca śāśvatam agniṣṭomam ca yaḥ yajñam yajeta bahu dakṣiṇam dīpe ca kṛsare ca eva tuṣyante asya pitāmahāḥ evam etat mayā uddiṣṭam ṛṣi dṛṣṭam purātanam bhīṣmaḥ tatas tu ṛṣi gaṇāḥ sarve pitaraḥ ca sa devatāḥ arundhatīm tapaḥ vṛddhām apṛcchanta samāhitāḥ samāna śīlām vīryeṇa vasiṣṭhasya mahātmanaḥ tapaḥ vṛddhiḥ mayā prāptā bhavatām smaraṇena vai bhavatām ca prasādena dharmān vakṣyāmi śāśvatān sa guhyān sa rahasyān ca tān śṛṇudhvam aśeṣatas śraddadhāne prayoktavyāḥ yasya śuddham tathā manaḥ aśraddadhānaḥ mānī ca brahma hā guru talpa gaḥ a saṃbhāṣyāḥ hi catvāraḥ na eṣām dharmam prakāśayet ahani ahani yaḥ dadyāt kapilām dvādaśīḥ samāḥ gavām śata sahasram ca yaḥ dadyāt jyeṣṭhapuṣkare śrūyatām ca aparaḥ dharmaḥ manuṣyāṇām sukha āvahaḥ niṣiñceta gavām śṛṅge mastakena ca tat jalam pratīccheta nirāhāraḥ tasya dharma phalam śṛṇu śrūyante yāni tīrthāni triṣu lokeṣu kāni cit siddha cāraṇa juṣṭāni sevitāni mahā ṛṣibhiḥ abhiṣekaḥ samaḥ teṣām gavām śṛṅga udakasya ca sādhu sādhu iti ca uddiṣṭam daivataiḥ pitṛbhiḥ tathā pitāmahaḥ bhūtaiḥ ca eva su saṃhṛṣṭaiḥ pūjitā sā api arundhatī yamaḥ ramaṇīyā kathā divyā yuṣmattaḥ yā mayā śrutā śrūyatām citraguptasya bhāṣitam mama ca priyam rahasyam dharma saṃyuktam śakyam śrotum mahā ṛṣibhiḥ na hi puṇyam tathā pāpam kṛtam kim cit vinaśyati parva kāle ca yat kim cit ādityam ca adhitiṣṭhati pānīyam ca eva dīpam ca dātavyam satatam tathā puṣkare kapilā deyā brāhmaṇe vedapārage ayam ca eva aparaḥ dharmaḥ citraguptena bhāṣitaḥ tatra durgam anuprāptāḥ kṣudh tṛṣṇā paripīḍitāḥ pānīyasya guṇāḥ divyāḥ preta loke viśeṣataḥ tatra puṇyodakā nāma nadī teṣām vidhīyate akṣayam salilam tatra śītalam hi amṛta upamam pradīpasya pradānena śrūyatām guṇa vistaraḥ prabhām ca asya prayacchanti soma bhāskara pāvakāḥ devatāḥ ca asya manyante vimalāḥ sarvatodiśaḥ tasmāt dīpaḥ pradātavyaḥ pānīyam ca viśeṣataḥ puṣkare ca viśeṣeṇa śrūyatām tasya yat phalam go śatam sa vṛṣam tena dattam bhavati śāśvatam śodhayet kapilā hi ekā pradattam go śatam yathā tasmāt tu kapilā deyā kaumudyām jyeṣṭhapuṣkare na teṣām viṣamam kim cit na duḥkham na ca kaṇṭakāḥ chatra dāne sukhām chāyām labhate para loka gaḥ citragupta matam śrutvā hṛṣṭa romā vibhāvasuḥ uvāca devatāḥ sarvāḥ pitṝn ca eva mahā dyutiḥ śraddadhānāḥ ca ye martyāḥ brāhmaṇeṣu mahātmasu dharma doṣāḥ tu ime pañca yeṣām na asti iha niṣkṛtiḥ a saṃbhāṣyāḥ anācārāḥ varjanīyāḥ nara adhamāḥ aśraddadhānaḥ ca naraḥ striyam yaḥ ca upajīvati pacyante vai yathā mīnāḥ pūya śoṇita bhojanāḥ a saṃbhāṣyāḥ pitṝṇām ca devānām ca eva pañca te bhīṣmaḥ snātakānām ca viprāṇām ye ca anye ca tapodhanāḥ tatas sarve mahābhāgāḥ devāḥ ca pitaraḥ ca ha ṛṣayaḥ ca mahābhāgāḥ pramathān vākyam abruvan bhavantaḥ vai mahābhāgāḥ aparokṣa niśācarāḥ ucchiṣṭān aśucīn kṣudrān katham hiṃsatha mānavān ke ca smṛtāḥ pratīghātāḥ yena martyān na hiṃsatha rakṣaḥ ghnāni ca kāni syuḥ yaiḥ gṛheṣu praṇaśyatha pramathāḥ śrotum icchāma yuṣmākam sarvam etat niśācarāḥ mohāt māṃsāni khādetha vṛkṣa mūle ca yaḥ svapet āmiṣam śīrṣataḥ yasya pādataḥ yaḥ ca saṃviśet tatas ucchiṣṭakāḥ sarve bahu chidrāḥ ca mānavāḥ udake ca api amedhyāni śleṣmāṇam ca pramuñcati ete bhakṣyāḥ ca vadhyāḥ ca mānuṣāḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ evaṃśīla samācārān dharṣayāmaḥ hi mānavān śrūyatām ca pratīghātān yaiḥ na śaknuma hiṃsitum ghṛta akṣatam ca yaḥ dadyāt mastake tad parāyaṇaḥ ye ca māṃsam na khādanti tān na śaknuma hiṃsitum yasya ca agniḥ gṛhe nityam divā rātrau ca dīpyate tarakṣoḥ carma daṃṣṭrāḥ ca tathā eva giri kacchapaḥ ājya dhūmaḥ biḍālaḥ ca chāgaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ atha piṅgalaḥ yeṣām etāni tiṣṭhanti gṛheṣu gṛhamedhinām tāni adhṛṣyāṇi agārāṇi piśitāśaiḥ su dāruṇaiḥ tasmāt etāni geheṣu rakṣaḥ ghnāni viśām pate bhīṣmaḥ etat vaḥ kathitam sarvam yatra vaḥ saṃśayaḥ mahān tatas padma pratīkāśaḥ padma udbhūtaḥ pitāmahaḥ uvāca vacanam devān vāsavam ca śacīpatim ayam mahā balaḥ nāgaḥ rasātala caraḥ balī tejasvī reṇukaḥ nāma mahā sattva parākramaḥ ati tejasvinaḥ sarve mahā vīryāḥ mahā gajāḥ dhārayanti mahīm kṛtsnām sa śaila vana kānanām bhavadbhiḥ samanujñātaḥ reṇukaḥ tān mahā gajān pitāmaha vacaḥ śrutvā te devāḥ reṇukam tadā reṇukaḥ preṣayām āsuḥ avyagrāḥ yatra te dharaṇīdharāḥ anujñātaḥ asmi devaiḥ ca pitṛbhiḥ ca mahā balāḥ dharma guhyāni yuṣmākam śrotum icchāmi tattvataḥ diggajāḥ kathayadhvam mahābhāgāḥ yat vaḥ tattvam manīṣitam kārttike māsi ca āśleṣā bahulasya aṣṭamī śivā imam mantram japan śrāddhe yata āhāraḥ hi akopanaḥ anantāḥ hi akṣayāḥ nityam bhoginaḥ su mahā balāḥ teṣām kula udbhavāḥ ye ca mahā bhūtāḥ bhujaṃgamāḥ yadā nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān ujjahāra vasuṃdharām gaja indra kusuma ākīrṇam nīla vastra anulepanam evam tuṣṭāḥ tatas sarve adhastāt bhāra pīḍitā śramam tam na avabudhyāmaḥ dhārayantaḥ vasuṃdharām evam manyāmahe dharmam bhāra ārtāḥ nirapekṣiṇaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyaḥ vaiśyaḥ śūdraḥ vā yadi upoṣitaḥ evam saṃvatsaram kṛtvā dānam bahu phalam labhet ye ca nāgāḥ mahā vīryāḥ triṣu lokeṣu kṛtsnaśas kṛta ātithyāḥ bhaveyuḥ te śatam varṣāṇi tattvataḥ diggajānām ca tat śrutvā devatāḥ pitaraḥ tathā maheśvaraḥ ṛṣayaḥ ca mahābhāgāḥ pūjayanti sma reṇukam sāram uddhṛtya yuṣmābhiḥ sādhu dharmaḥ udāhṛtaḥ yeṣām dharma āśritā buddhiḥ śraddha dhānāḥ ca ye narāḥ teṣām syāt upadeṣṭavyaḥ sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ eka bhaktam tathā aśnīyāt śrūyatām tasya yat phalam imāḥ gāvaḥ mahābhāgāḥ pavitram paramam smṛtāḥ trīn lokān dhārayanti sma sa deva asura mānuṣān tāsu ca eva mahā puṇyam śuśrūṣā ca mahā phalam mayā hi etāḥ hi anujñātāḥ pūrvam āsan kṛte yuge tasmāt vraja sthāna gataḥ tiṣṭhati upari me vṛṣaḥ rame aham saha gobhiḥ ca tasmāt pūjyāḥ sadā eva tāḥ mahā prabhāvāḥ vara dāḥ varān dadyuḥ upāsitāḥ mama api anumataḥ dharmaḥ tam śṛṇudhvam samāhitāḥ nīlaṣaṇḍasya śṛṅgābhyām gṛhītvā mṛttikām tu yaḥ pragṛhya audumbaram pātram pakva annam madhunā saha somasya uttiṣṭhamānasya paurṇamāsyām balim haret sādhyāḥ rudrāḥ tathā ādityāḥ viśvedevāḥ tathā aśvinau viṣṇuḥ eṣa dharmaḥ mayā uddiṣṭaḥ sa rahasyaḥ sukha āvahaḥ dharma guhyāni sarvāṇi devatānām mahātmanām ṛṣīṇām ca eva guhyāni yaḥ paṭhet āhnikam sadā śṛṇuyāt vā anasūyuḥ yaḥ śraddadhānaḥ samāhitaḥ ye ca dharmāḥ śubhāḥ puṇyāḥ sa rahasyāḥ udāhṛtāḥ teṣām dharma phalam tasya yaḥ paṭheta jita indriyaḥ paṭhet vā śrāvayet vā api śrutvā vā labhate phalam śrāvayan ca api vipra indrān parvasu prayataḥ naraḥ ṛṣīṇām devatānām ca pitṝṇām ca eva nityadā bhavati abhimataḥ śrīmān dharmeṣu prayataḥ sadā kṛtvā api pāpakam karma mahāpātaka varjitam bhīṣmaḥ vyāsa uddiṣṭam mayā proktam sarva deva namaskṛtam pṛthivī ratna saṃpūrṇā jñānam ca idam anuttamam na aśraddadhānāya na nāstikāya na naṣṭa dharmāya na nirguṇāya na hetu duṣṭāya guru dviṣe vā na an ātma bhūtāya nivedyam etat yudhiṣṭhiraḥ bhīṣmaḥ tathā vaiśyasya ke bhojyāḥ ke ca śūdrasya bhārata brāhmaṇāḥ brāhmaṇasya iha bhojyāḥ ye ca eva kṣatriyāḥ vaiśyāḥ ca api tathā bhojyāḥ śūdrāḥ ca parivarjitāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāḥ vaiśyāḥ bhojyāḥ vai kṣatriyasya ha varjanīyāḥ tu vai śūdrāḥ sarva bhakṣyāḥ vikarmiṇaḥ vaiśyāḥ tu bhojyāḥ viprāṇām kṣatriyāṇām tathā eva ca nitya agnayaḥ viviktāḥ ca cāturmāsya ratāḥ ca ye śūdrāṇām atha yaḥ bhuṅkte sa bhuṅkte pṛthivī malam malam nṛṇām sa pibati malam bhuṅkte janasya ca śūdrāṇām yaḥ tathā bhuṅkte sa bhuṅkte pṛthivī malam pṛthivī malam aśnanti ye dvijāḥ śūdra bhojinaḥ śūdrasya karma niṣṭhāyām vikarma sthaḥ api pacyate svādhyāya niratāḥ viprāḥ tathā svastyayane nṛṇām rakṣaṇe kṣatriyam prāhuḥ vaiśyam puṣṭi artham eva ca kṛṣi gorakṣya vāṇijyam akutsā vaiśya karmaṇi sa vijñeyaḥ yathā śūdraḥ na ca bhojyaḥ kadā cana cikitsakaḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaḥ purādhyakṣaḥ purohitaḥ sāṃvatsaraḥ vṛthādhyāyī sarve te śūdra saṃmitāḥ puṃścalī annam ca mūtram syāt kārukā annam ca śoṇitam vacanīyasya yaḥ bhuṅkte tam āhuḥ śoṇitam hradam piśunam bhojanam bhuṅkte brahmahatyā samam viduḥ asatkṛtam avajñātam na bhoktavyam kadā cana vyādhim kula kṣayam ca eva kṣipram prāpnoti brāhmaṇaḥ nagarī rakṣiṇaḥ bhuṅkte śvapaca pravaṇaḥ bhavet bhuktvā annam jāyate vipraḥ rakṣasām kula vardhanaḥ nyāsa apahāriṇaḥ bhuktvā kṛtaghne klība vartini jāyate śabara āvāse madhyadeśa bahiṣkṛte abhojyāḥ ca eva bhojyāḥ ca mayā proktāḥ yathāvidhi yudhiṣṭhiraḥ uktāḥ tu bhavatā bhojyāḥ tathā abhojyāḥ ca sarvaśas brāhmaṇānām viśeṣeṇa havya kavya pratigrahe bhīṣmaḥ nānāvidheṣu bhojyeṣu prāyaścittāni śaṃsa me hanta vakṣyāmi te rājan brāhmaṇānām mahātmanām āditya udayanam sthitvā pūtaḥ bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ kāñcanam pratigṛhya atha japamānaḥ guru śrutim kṛṣṇāyasam ca trivṛtam dhārayan mucyate dvijaḥ yāvake dadhi dugdhe ca sāvitrīm śataśas anvitām trīṇi rātrāṇi upoṣitvā tena pāpmā vimucyate kṛṣṇa pakṣe tu yaḥ śrāddham pitṝṇām aśnute dvijaḥ annam etat ahar rātrāt pūtaḥ bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ na saṃkiret tat annam ca tathā pūyeta brāhmaṇaḥ ityartham aparāhṇe tu pitṝṇām śrāddham ucyate yathā uktānām yat aśnīyuḥ brāhmaṇāḥ pūrva ketitāḥ mṛtakasya tṛtīya ahe brāhmaṇaḥ yaḥ annam aśnute dvādaśa ahe vyatīte tu kṛta śaucaḥ viśeṣataḥ mṛtasya daśa rātreṇa prāyaścittāni dāpayet sāvitrīm raivatīm iṣṭim kūṣmāṇḍam aghamarṣaṇam mṛtakasya tri rātre yaḥ samuddiṣṭe samaśnute sapta triṣavaṇam snātvā pūtaḥ bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ siddhim āpnoti vipulām āpadam ca eva na āpnuyāt yaḥ tu śūdraiḥ saha aśnīyāt brāhmaṇaḥ api eka bhojane aśaucam vidhivat tasya śauca mātram vidhīyate yaḥ tu vaiśyaiḥ saha aśnīyāt brāhmaṇaḥ api eka bhojane sa vai tri rātram dīkṣitvā mucyate tena karmaṇā kṣatriyaiḥ saha yaḥ aśnīyāt brāhmaṇaḥ api eka bhojane śūdrasya tu kulam hanti vaiśyasya paśu bāndhavān prāyaścittam ca śāntim ca juhuyāt tena mucyate sāvitrīm raivatīm iṣṭim kūṣmāṇḍam aghamarṣaṇam atha ucchiṣṭam atha anyonyam saṃprāśet na atra saṃśayaḥ rocanā virajāḥ rātriḥ maṅgala ālambhanāni ca yudhiṣṭhiraḥ kim svid pṛthivyām hi etat me bhavān śaṃsitum arhati bhīṣmaḥ śṛṇu yaiḥ dharma nirataiḥ tapasā bhāvitātmabhiḥ lokāḥ hi asaṃśayam prāptāḥ dāna puṇya rataiḥ nṛpaiḥ satkṛtaḥ ca tathā ātreyaḥ śiṣyebhyaḥ brahma nirguṇam śibiḥ auśīnaraḥ prāṇān priyasya tanayasya ca brāhmaṇa artham upākṛtya nāka pṛṣṭham itas gataḥ pratardanaḥ kāśi patiḥ pradāya tanayam svakam brāhmaṇāya atulām kīrtim iha ca amutra ca aśnute rantidevaḥ ca sāṃkṛtyaḥ vasiṣṭhāya mahātmane divyam śata śalākam ca yajña artham kāñcanam śubham chatram devāvṛdhaḥ dattvā brāhmaṇāya āsthitaḥ divam bhagavān ambarīṣaḥ ca brāhmaṇāya amita ojase pradāya sakalam rāṣṭram sura lokam avāptavān sāvitraḥ kuṇḍalam divyam yānam ca janamejayaḥ brāhmaṇāya ca gāḥ dattvā gataḥ lokān anuttamān vṛṣādarbhiḥ ca rājarṣiḥ ratnāni vividhāni ca ramyān ca āvasathān dattvā dvijebhyaḥ divam āgamat nimiḥ rāṣṭram ca vaidarbhiḥ kanyām dattvā mahātmane jāmadagnyaḥ ca viprāya bhūmim dattvā mahā yaśāḥ rāmaḥ akṣayān tathā lokān jagāma manasaḥ adhikān vasiṣṭhaḥ jīvayām āsa yena yātaḥ akṣayām gatim rāmaḥ dāśarathiḥ ca eva hutvā yajñeṣu vai vasu kakṣasenaḥ ca rājarṣiḥ vasiṣṭhāya mahātmane nyāsam yathāvat saṃnyasya jagāma su mahā yaśāḥ kanyām aṅgirase dattvā divam āśu jagāma saḥ brahmadattaḥ ca pāñcālyaḥ rājā dharma bhṛtām varaḥ nidhim śaṅkham anujñāpya jagāma paramām gatim rājā mitrasahaḥ ca eva vasiṣṭhāya mahātmane madayantīm priyām bhāryām dattvā ca tridivam gataḥ daṇḍam uddhṛtya dharmeṇa gataḥ lokān anuttamān sahasracityaḥ rājarṣiḥ prāṇān iṣṭān mahā yaśāḥ sarva kāmaiḥ ca saṃpūrṇam dattvā veśma hiraṇmayam bhakṣya bhojyasya ca kṛtān rāśayaḥ parvata upamān śāṇḍilyāya purā dattvā sa manyuḥ divam āsthitaḥ nāmnā ca dyutimān nāma śālva rājaḥ mahā dyutiḥ dattvā rājyam ṛcīkāya gataḥ lokān anuttamān madirāśvaḥ ca rājarṣiḥ dattvā kanyām sumadhyamām hiraṇyahastāya gataḥ lokān devaiḥ adhiṣṭhitān lomapādaḥ ca rājarṣiḥ śāntām dattvā sutām prabhuḥ ṛṣyaśṛṅgāya vipulaiḥ sarvaiḥ kāmaiḥ ayujyata kautsāya dattvā kanyām tu haṃsīm nāma yaśasvinīm gataḥ akṣayān atas lokān rājarṣiḥ ca bhagīrathaḥ dattvā śata sahasram tu gavām rājā bhagīrathaḥ sa vatsānām kohalāya gataḥ lokān anuttamān yudhiṣṭhira gatāḥ svargam nivartante punar punar teṣām pratiṣṭhitā kīrtiḥ yāvat sthāsyati medinī śiṣṭānām caritam hi etat kīrtitam me yudhiṣṭhira dāna yajña prajā sargaiḥ ete hi divam āsthitāḥ dattvā tu satatam te astu kauravāṇām dhuraṃdhara dāna yajña kriyā yuktā buddhiḥ dharma upacāyinī yatra te nṛpa śārdūla saṃdehaḥ vai bhaviṣyati yudhiṣṭhiraḥ śvas prabhāte hi vakṣyāmi saṃdhyā hi samupasthitā dāna dharmeṇa mahatā ye prāptāḥ tridivam nṛpāḥ imān tu śrotum icchāmi dharmān dharma bhṛtām vara katham kebhyaḥ ca dharmyam ca dānam dātavyam iṣyate bhīṣmaḥ kaiḥ kāraṇaiḥ katividham śrotum icchāmi tattvataḥ śṛṇu tattvena kaunteya dānam prati mama anagha yathā dānam pradātavyam sarva varṇeṣu bhārata dharmāt arthāt bhayāt kāmāt kāruṇyāt iti bhārata dānam pañcavidham jñeyam kāraṇaiḥ yaiḥ nibodha tat iti dānam pradātavyam brāhmaṇebhyaḥ anasūyatā dadāti vā dāsyati vā mahyam dattam anena vā na asya aham na madīyaḥ ayam pāpam kuryāt vimānitaḥ iti dadyāt bhayāt eva dṛḍham mūḍhāya paṇḍitaḥ vayasyāya evam akliṣṭam dānam dadyāt atandritaḥ dīnaḥ ca yācate ca ayam alpena api hi tuṣyati iti dadyāt daridrāya kāruṇyāt iti sarvathā iti pañcavidham dānam puṇya kīrti vivardhanam yathāśaktyā pradātavyam evam āha prajāpatiḥ vānaprastha uñcha vṛttīnām tapaḥ vistṛta cetasām nāradaḥ uvāca evam uktvā mahādevaḥ śrotu kāmaḥ svayam prabhuḥ anukūlām priyām bhāryām pārśva sthām samabhāṣata guṇavān sa bhaviṣyati daṃpatyoḥ eṣa vai dharmaḥ sahadharma kṛtaḥ śubhaḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ anuśāsya śubhaiḥ vākyaiḥ bhīṣmaḥ tu āha mahāmatim prītyā punar sa śuśrūṣuḥ vacanam yat yudhiṣṭhire pitāmahaḥ me viprarṣe bhīṣmam kāla vaśam gatam kim apṛcchat tadā rājā sarvasāmāsikam hitam vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ tat me vada mahā prājña śrotum kautūhalam hi me bhūyas eva mahā rāja śṛṇu dharmasamuccayam yat apṛcchat tadā rājā kuntī putraḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ śara talpa gatam bhīṣmam sarva pārthiva saṃnidhau yudhiṣṭhiraḥ ajātaśatruḥ prīta ātmā punar eva abhyabhāṣata pitāmaha mahā prājña sarva śāstra viśārada parāvara jñaḥ bhūtānām dayāvān sarva jantuṣu tvādṛśaḥ durlabhaḥ loke sāṃpratam jñāna darśanaḥ ayam sa kālaḥ saṃprāptaḥ durlabhaḥ jñāti bāndhavaiḥ śāstā nu nā asti naḥ kaḥ cit tvat ṛte puruṣa ṛṣabha āścaryam paramam vākyam śrotum icchāmi bhārata ayam nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān sarva pārthiva saṃnidhau asya eva tu samakṣam naḥ pārthivānām tathā eva ca itivṛttam purāṇam ca śrotṝṇām paramam hitam vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ tasya tat vacanam śrutvā snehāt āgata viklavaḥ prapiban iva tam dṛṣṭvā bhīṣmaḥ vacanam abravīt śṛṇu rājan purā vṛttam itihāsam purātanam etāvat uktvā gāṅgeyaḥ praṇamya śirasā harim dharmarājam samīkṣya idam punar vaktum samārabhat ayam nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān putra artham vrata kāṅkṣayā dīkṣitaḥ abhūt mahābāhuḥ purā dvādaśa vārṣikam dīkṣitam keśavam draṣṭum abhijagmuḥ mahā ṛṣayaḥ sevitum ca mahātmānaḥ prīyamāṇāḥ janārdanam nāradaḥ parvataḥ ca eva kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaḥ tathā devalaḥ kāśyapaḥ ca eva hastikāśyapaḥ eva ca apare api tapaḥ siddhāḥ satya vrata parāyaṇāḥ keśavaḥ tān abhigatān prītyā saṃpratigṛhya ca teṣām atithi satkāram pūjana artham kula ucitam upaviṣṭeṣu sarveṣu viṣṭareṣu tadā anagha viśvasteṣu hi tuṣṭeṣu keśava arcanayā punar parasparam kathāḥ divyāḥ prāvartanta manoramāḥ viṣṇoḥ nārāyaṇasya eva prasādāt kathayāmi tāḥ yaḥ ca go vṛṣabhāṅkasya prabhāvaḥ abhūt mahātmanaḥ yatra devī mahādevam apṛcchat saṃśayān purā kathayām āsa śarvaḥ tān devyāḥ priya cikīrṣayā umāpatyoḥ ca saṃvādam śṛṇu tāta manoramam varṇa āśramāṇām dharmaḥ ca tatra tāta samāhitaḥ ṛṣi dharmaḥ ca nikhilaḥ rāja dharmaḥ ca puṣkalaḥ prāṇamokṣa vidhiḥ ca eva tīrtha caryā ca puṣkalā strī dharmaḥ ca svayam devyā devadevāya bhāṣitaḥ evamādi śubham sarvam tatra tāta samāhitam rudrāṇyāḥ saṃśaya praśnaḥ yatra tāta pravartate dhanyam yaśasyam āyuṣyam dharmyam ca paramam hitam puṣṭi yogam idam divyam kathyamānam mayā śṛṇu itihāsam imam divyam pavitram paramam śubham sāyam prātar sadā samyak śrotavyam ca bubhūṣatā tatas nārāyaṇaḥ devaḥ saṃkliṣṭaḥ vrata caryayā vahniḥ viniḥsṛtaḥ vaktrāt kṛṣṇasya adbhuta darśanaḥ agninā tena mahatā niḥsṛtena mukhāt vibhoḥ paśyatām eva sarveṣām dagdhaḥ eva naga uttamaḥ mṛga pakṣi samākīrṇaḥ śvāpadaiḥ abhisaṃkulaḥ vṛkṣa gulma latā ākīrṇaḥ mathitaḥ dīna darśanaḥ punar sa dṛṣṭa mātreṇa hariṇā saumya cetasā matta vāraṇa saṃyuktaḥ nānā pakṣi gaṇaiḥ yutaḥ tam dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭa romāṇaḥ sarve muni gaṇāḥ tadā vismitāḥ parama āyastāḥ sādhvasa ākula locanāḥ tān samīkṣya eva madhuram babhāṣe puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ etam me saṃśayam sarve yāthātathyam aninditāḥ ṛṣayaḥ vaktum arhanti niścayena artha kovidāḥ keśavasya vacaḥ śrutvā tuṣṭuvuḥ muni puṃgavāḥ bhavān sṛjati vai lokān bhavān saṃharati prajāḥ bhavān śītam bhavān uṣṇam bhavān satyam bhavān kratuḥ nityam hita artham lokānām bhavadbhiḥ kriyate tapaḥ tasmāt loka hitam guhyam śrūyatām kathayāmi vaḥ asuraḥ sāṃpratam kaḥ cit ahitaḥ loka nāśanaḥ babhūva sa mayā vadhyaḥ lokānām hita kāmyayā putreṇa me vadhaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ tasya vai muni puṃgavāḥ tad artham putram eva aham sisṛkṣuḥ vanam āgataḥ ātmanaḥ sadṛśam putram aham janayitum vrataiḥ atha ātmā mama deha sthaḥ saḥ agniḥ bhūtvā viniḥsṛtaḥ viniḥsṛtya gataḥ draṣṭum kṣaṇena ca pitāmaham anujñātaḥ ca tena eva punar āyāt mama antikam etat me vaiṣṇavam tejaḥ mama vaktrāt viniḥsṛtam dṛṣṭvā nāśam gireḥ tasya saumya bhāva tayā mama punar sa dṛṣṭa mātreṇa giriḥ āsīt yathāpuram etat guhyam mayā tathyam kathitam vaḥ samāsatas ṛṣīṇām evam uktvā tu tān punar pratyabhāṣata bhavatām darśanāt eva prītiḥ abhyadhikā mama sarvatra gatimantaḥ ca jñāna vijñāna bhāvitāḥ gatāgata jñāḥ lokānām sarve nirdhūta kalmaṣāḥ tasmāt bhavadbhiḥ yat kim cit dṛṣṭam vā api atha vā śrutam yuṣmābhiḥ kathitam yat syāt tapasā bhāvitātmabhiḥ tat syāt amṛta saṃkāśam vāc madhu śravaṇe spṛhā rāga dveṣa viyuktānām satatam satya vādinām śraddheyam śravaṇīyam ca vacanam hi satām bhavet tat aham sat jana mukhāt niḥsṛtam jana saṃsadi kathayiṣyāmi aharahar buddhi dīpa karam nṛṇām vāc śalya vigatāḥ ca anye praśaṃsanti purātanam bhīṣmaḥ evam bruvati govinde śravaṇa artham mahā ṛṣayaḥ vāgbhiḥ sa añjali mālābhiḥ idam ūcuḥ janārdanam ayuktam asmān evam tvam vācā vara da bhāṣitum tvad śāsana mukhāḥ sarve tvad adhīna pariśramāḥ evam pūjayitum ca asmān na ca eva arhasi keśava evam uktaḥ hṛṣīkeśaḥ sa smitam ca idam abravīt aham mānuṣa yoni sthaḥ sāṃpratam muni puṃgavāḥ tasmāt mānuṣa vat vīryam mama jānīta suvratāḥ bhīṣmaḥ evam saṃcoditāḥ sarve keśavena mahātmanā ṛṣayaḥ ca anuvartante vāsudevasya śāsanam tatas tu ṛṣi gaṇāḥ sarve nāradam devadarśanam ṛṣiḥ ugra tapāḥ ca ayam keśavasya priyaḥ adhikam purāṇa jñaḥ ca vāgmī ca kāraṇaiḥ tam ca menire sarve tad arhaṇam kṛtvā nāradam vākyam abruvan dṛṣṭam vai yat tat āścaryam śrotṝṇām paramam priyam tattvam tvam aviśeṣeṇa hita artham sarvam āditas bhīṣmaḥ tadā saṃcoditaḥ sarvaiḥ ṛṣibhiḥ nāradaḥ tathā praṇamya śirasā viṣṇum sarva loka hite ratam samudvīkṣya hṛṣīkeśam vaktum evā upacakrame tatas nārāyaṇa suhṛd nāradaḥ vadatām varaḥ nāradaḥ śaṃkarasya umayā sārdham saṃvādam anvabhāṣata bhagavan tīrtha yātrā artham tathā eva caratā mayā divyam adbhuta saṃkāśam dṛṣṭam haimavatam vanam nānā vṛkṣa latā yuktam nānā pakṣi gaṇaiḥ yutam nānā ratna samākīrṇam nānā bhāva samanvitam siddha cāraṇa saṃvāsam bhūta saṃghaiḥ niṣevitam variṣṭha apsarasā ākīrṇam nānā gandharva saṃkulam mṛdaṅga muraja udghuṣṭam śaṅkha vīṇā abhināditam nānā rūpaiḥ virūpaiḥ ca bhīma rūpaiḥ bhayānakaiḥ ulūka śyena vadanaiḥ kāka gṛdhra mukhaiḥ tathā evam bahuvidha ākāraiḥ bhūta saṃghaiḥ bhṛśa ākulam nānadyamānam bahudhā hara pāriṣadaiḥ bhṛśam ghora rūpam su durdarśam rakṣaḥ gaṇa śataiḥ vṛtam samājam tad vane dṛṣṭam mayā bhūtapateḥ purā ṣaṭpadaiḥ upagītam ca prathame māsi mādhave utkrośat krauñca kuraraiḥ sārasaiḥ jīvajīvakaiḥ mattābhiḥ parapuṣṭābhiḥ kūjantībhiḥ samākulam uttama utsava saṃkāśam bhīma rūpataram tatas mārtāṇḍa raśmi saṃcārāḥ viśvedeva gaṇāḥ tathā tathā nāgāḥ tathā ādityāḥ lokapālāḥ hutāśanāḥ vātāḥ ca sarve vāyanti divya puṣpa samākulāḥ kirantaḥ sarva puṣpāṇi kirantaḥ adbhuta darśanāḥ paryaṅke iva babhrāje upaviṣṭaḥ mahā dyutiḥ vyāla yajñopavītaḥ ca lohita antra vibhūṣitaḥ hari śmaśru jaṭaḥ bhīmaḥ bhaya kartā suradviṣām bhaya ghnaḥ sarva bhūtānām bhaktānām abhayaṃkaraḥ ṛṣibhiḥ ca apsarobhiḥ ca sarvatas ca api śobhitaḥ tatra bhūtapateḥ sthānam deva dānava saṃkulam mahā uraga samākīrṇam sarveṣām loma harṣaṇam bhīma rūpam anirdeśyam apradhṛṣyatamam vibho tatra bhūtapatim devam āsīnam śikhara uttame ṛṣayaḥ bhūta saṃghāḥ ca praṇamya śirasā haram gīrbhiḥ parama śuddhābhiḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ ca maheśvaram ṛṣayaḥ vālakhilyāḥ ca tathā viprarṣayaḥ ca ye a yoni jāḥ yoni jāḥ ca tapaḥ siddhāḥ mahā ṛṣayaḥ tatas tasmin kṣaṇe devī bhūta strī gaṇa sevitā hara tulya ambara dharā samāna vrata cāriṇī kāñcanam kalaśam gṛhya sarva tīrtha ambu pūritam puṣpa vṛṣṭyā abhivarṣantī divya gandha samāyutā sarit varābhiḥ sarvābhiḥ pṛṣṭhatas anugatā varā sevitum bhagavat pārśvam ājagāma śuci smitā manaḥ priyam cikīrṣantī krīḍā artham śaṃkara antike manoharābhyām pāṇibhyām hara netre pidhāya tu avekṣya hṛṣṭā sva gaṇān smayantī pṛṣṭhatas sthitā devyā ca andhīkṛte deve kaśmalam samapadyata nimīlite bhūtapatau naṣṭa candra arka tārakam niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkāram tamasā ca abhisaṃvṛtam viṣaṇṇam bhaya saṃtrastam jagat āsīt bhaya ākulam hāhākāraḥ tu ṛṣīṇām ca lokānām abhavat tadā tamaḥ abhibhūte saṃbhrānte loke jīvita śaṅkite tṛtīyam ca asya saṃbhūtam lalāṭe netram āyatam dvādaśa āditya saṃkāśam lokān bhāsau abhāsayat paśyatām eva sarveṣām deva dānava rakṣasām tam dṛṣṭvā mathitam śailam śailaputrī sa viklavā pituḥ śamanam icchantī papāta bhuvi pādayoḥ tat dṛṣṭvā devadeveśaḥ devyāḥ duḥkham anuttamam dṛṣṭa mātraḥ bhagavatā saumya yuktena cetasā kṣaṇena himavān śailaḥ prakṛti sthaḥ abhavat punar hṛṣṭa puṣṭa vihaṃgaḥ ca praphulla druma kānanaḥ siddha cāraṇa saṃghaiḥ ca prīti yuktaiḥ samākulaḥ pitaram prakṛti stham tu dṛṣṭvā haimavatī bhṛśam devī vismaya saṃyuktā praṣṭu kāmā maheśvaram hita artham sarva lokānām prajānām hita kāmyayā devadevam mahādevī babhāṣa idam vacaḥ arthavat bhagavan devadeveśa śūlapāṇe mahā dyute kimartham devadeveśa lalāṭe asmin prakāśate ati sūrya agni saṃkāśam tṛtīyam netram āyatam punar sa dṛṣṭa mātraḥ tu prakṛti sthaḥ pitā mama nāradaḥ evam uktaḥ tayā devyā prīyamāṇaḥ abravīt bhavaḥ sthāne saṃśayitum devi dharma jñe priya bhāṣiṇi tvat ṛte mām hi vai praṣṭum na śakyam kena cit priye prakāśam yadi vā guhyam tvad priya artham bravīmi aham śṛṇu tat sarvam akhilam asyām saṃsadi bhāmini sarveṣām eva lokānām kūṭastham viddhi mām priye mad adhīnāḥ trayaḥ lokāḥ yathā viṣṇau tathā mayi sraṣṭā viṣṇuḥ aham goptā iti tat viddhi bhāmini tasmāt yadā mām spṛśati śubham vā yadi vā itarat netre me pihite devi krīḍana artham dṛḍha vrate tvad kṛte naṣṭa candra arkam jagat āsīt bhṛśa ākulam tṛtīyam locanam sṛṣṭam lokān saṃrakṣitum mayā asya eva tejasā akṣṇaḥ me mathitaḥ ayam giriḥ śubhe vṛkṣa gulma latā ākīrṇaḥ vyāla adhyuṣita kaṃdaraḥ nāradaḥ kathitam saṃśaya sthānam nirviśaṅkā bhava priye kṣaṇa jñā devadevasya śrotu kāmā priyam hitam uttaram ca api bhagavan paścimam śubha darśanam dakṣiṇam ca mukham raudram kena ūrdhvam jaṭila āvṛtam yathādiśam mahādeva śrotum icchāmi kāraṇam maheśvaraḥ tayoḥ eva vināśāya nirmitāḥ viśvakarmaṇā mṛga pakṣi gaṇānām ca sthāvarāṇām tathā eva ca sarvatas sāram uddhṛtya tilaśas loka pūjitā tilottamā iti vikhyātā apsarāḥ sā babhūva ha deva kāryam kariṣyantī hāva bhāva samanvitā sā tu paryantam āgamya rūpeṇa apratimā bhuvi mayā bahu matā ca iyam deva kāryam kariṣyati iti matvā tadā ca aham kurvantīm mām pradakṣiṇam aindram mukham idam pūrvam tapaḥ caryā param sadā dakṣiṇam me mukham divyam raudram saṃharati prajāḥ umā etat te sarvam ākhyātam kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi bhagavan śrotum icchāmi śūlapāṇe vara prada maheśvaraḥ kimartham nīla tā kaṇṭhe bhāti barha nibhā tava hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā purā yuga antare yatnāt amṛta artham sura asuraiḥ viṣam tatra samudbhūtam sarva loka vināśanam tat dṛṣṭvā vibudhāḥ sarve tadā vimanasaḥ abhavan grastam hi tat mayā devi lokānām hita kāraṇāt tad kṛtā nīla tā ca āsīt kaṇṭhe barhi nibhā śubhe tadā prabhṛti ca eva aham nīlakaṇṭhaḥ iti smṛtaḥ umā etat te sarvam ākhyātam kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi bahūnām āyudhānām tvam pinākam dhartum icchasi maheśvaraḥ śastra āgamam te vakṣyāmi śṛṇu dharmyam śuci smite yuga antare mahādevi kaṇvaḥ nāma mahā muniḥ dharamāṇaḥ ca tat sarvam tapaḥ caryām atha akarot tasmai brahmā varam dātum jagāma tapasā arcitaḥ dattvā tasmai varam devaḥ veṇum dṛṣṭvā tu acintayat loka kāryam samuddiśya veṇunā anena bhāmini viṣṇoḥ mama ca sāmarthyam jñātvā lokapitāmahaḥ dhanuṣī dve tadā prādāt viṣṇave ca mama eva ca pinākam nāma me cāpam śārṅgam nāma hareḥ dhanuḥ tṛtīyam avaśeṣeṇa gāṇḍīvam abhavat dhanuḥ umā ayam tu vṛṣabhaḥ kasmāt vāhana tvam upāgamat maheśvaraḥ eṣa me saṃśayaḥ deva tam me śaṃsitum arhasi tat aham te pravakṣyāmi vāhanam sa yathā bhavat ādisarge purā gāvaḥ śveta varṇāḥ śuci smite bala saṃhanana upetāḥ darpa yuktāḥ caranti tāḥ aham tu tapaḥ ātiṣṭhe tasmin kāle śubha ānane gāvaḥ me pārśvam āviśya darpa utsiktāḥ samantataḥ apacāreṇa ca etāsām manaḥ kṣobhaḥ abhavat mama tasmāt dagdhāḥ mayā gāvaḥ roṣa āviṣṭena cetasā anena vṛṣabheṇa aham śamitaḥ saṃprasādanaiḥ tadā prabhṛti śāntāḥ ca varṇa bheda tvam āgatāḥ śvetaḥ ayam vṛṣabhaḥ devi pūrva saṃskāra saṃyutaḥ umā prasannaḥ ca abhavam devi tadā gopati tām gataḥ bhagavan sarva bhūta īśa śūlapāṇe vṛṣadhvaja āvāseṣu vicitreṣu ramyeṣu ca śubheṣu ca satsu ca anyeṣu deśeṣu śmaśāne ramase katham keśa asthi kalile bhīme kapāla śata saṃkule sṛgāla gṛdhra saṃpūrṇe śava dhūma samākule maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā āvāsa artham purā devi śuddha anveṣī śuci smite na adhyagaccham ciram kālam deśam śucitamam śubhe eṣa me abhiniveśaḥ abhūt tasmin kāle prajāḥ prati ākulam su mahat ghoram prādurāsīt samantataḥ saṃbhūtā bhūta sṛṣṭiḥ ca ghorā loka bhaya āvahā nānā varṇa virūpāḥ ca tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭrāḥ prahāriṇaḥ piśāca rakṣaḥ vadanāḥ prāṇinām prāṇa hāriṇaḥ evam loke kṣayam yāte prāṇi hīne pitāmahaḥ cintayan tad pratīkāram mām ca śaktam hi nigrahe evam jñātvā tadā brahmā tasmin karmaṇi ayojayat tat ca prāṇi dayā artham tu mayā api anumatam priye tasmāt saṃrakṣitāḥ devi bhūtebhyaḥ prāṇinaḥ bhayāt asmāt śmaśānāt medhyam tu na asti kim cit anindite niḥsaṃpātam manuṣyāṇām tasmāt śucitamam smṛtam bhūta sṛṣṭim tu tām ca aham śmaśāne saṃnyaveśayam tasmāt me saṃnivāsāya śmaśāne rocate manaḥ kapāla śata saṃpūrṇam bhīma rūpam bhayānakam tatrasthaḥ aham prajāḥ sarvāḥ pālayāmi dine dine tasmāt loka hita arthāya śmaśāne ramayāmi aham umā etat te sarvam ākhyātam kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi piṅgalam vikṛtam bhāti rūpam te su bhayānakam bhasma digdham virūpa akṣam tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭram jaṭā ākulam vyāghra uraga tvac saṃvītam kapila śmaśru saṃtatam raudram bhayānakam ghoram śūla paṭṭasa saṃyutam maheśvaraḥ kimartham īdṛśam rūpam tat me śaṃsitum arhasi tat aham kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu tattvam samāhitā dvividhaḥ laukikaḥ bhāvaḥ śītam uṣṇam iti priye saumya tvam satatam viṣṇau mayi āgneyam pratiṣṭhitam raudra ākṛti virūpa akṣam śūla paṭṭasa saṃyutam yadi aham viparītaḥ syām etat tyaktvā śubha ānane tasmāt mayā idam dhriyate rūpam loka hita eṣiṇā umā bhagavan devadeveśa śūlapāṇe vṛṣadhvaja maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam purā aham kāraṇāt devi kopa yuktaḥ śuci smite dakṣa yajña vadha arthāya bhūta saṃgha samāvṛtaḥ devāḥ vibhraṃśitāḥ te vai yeṣām bhāgaḥ kratau kṛtaḥ paśyan ca anaparādhī san pādāṅguṣṭhena pīḍitaḥ tathā vinikṛtena aham sāma pūrvam prasāditaḥ tat me cintayataḥ ca āsīt paścāttāpaḥ punar priye tadā prabhṛti somam hi śirasā dhārayāmi aham tadā prabhṛti vai somaḥ mūrdhni saṃdṛśyate sadā nāradaḥ evam bruvati deveśe vismitāḥ parama ṛṣayaḥ vāgbhiḥ sa añjali mālābhiḥ abhituṣṭuvuḥ īśvaram namaḥ deva ādidevāya namaḥ śaśi kalā dhara namaḥ śāntatarāt śānta namaḥ candrasya pālaka namaḥ triśūla hastāya pannaga ābharaṇāya ca namaḥ astu viṣama akṣāya dakṣa yajña pradāhaka aho devasya māhātmyam mahādevasya vai kṛpā prīti artham ca munīnām sā kṣaṇajñā paramam hitam varṇa āśrama gatam dharmam vaktum arhasi aśeṣatas na tṛptiḥ asti deveśa śravaṇīyam hi te vacaḥ vaktum arhasi deveśa lokānām hita kāmyayā maheśvaraḥ śṛṇu tat sarvam akhilam dharmam varṇa āśrama āśritam brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāḥ vaiśyāḥ śūdrāḥ ca iti caturvidhāḥ brahmaṇā vihitāḥ pūrvam lokatantram abhīpsatā karmāṇi ca tad arhāṇi śāstreṣu vihitāni vai mukhataḥ brāhmaṇāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ tasmāt te vāc viśāradāḥ bāhubhyām kṣatriyāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ tasmāt bāhu bala anvitāḥ udarāt udgatāḥ vaiśyāḥ tasmāt vārttā upajīvinaḥ śūdrāḥ ca pādataḥ sṛṣṭāḥ tasmāt te paricārakāḥ teṣām dharmān ca karmāṇi śṛṇu devi samāhitā viprāḥ kṛtāḥ bhūmidevāḥ lokānām dhāraṇe kṛtāḥ te kaiḥ cit na avamantavyāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ hitam icchubhiḥ yadi te brāhmaṇāḥ na syuḥ dāna yoga vahāḥ sadā abudhaḥ vā daridraḥ vā pūjanīyaḥ sadā eva saḥ prahareta haret vā api dhanam teṣām nara adhamaḥ kārayet dhīna karmāṇi kāma lobha vimohanāt mām eva praharet mūḍhaḥ mad dhanasya apahārakaḥ mama eva prekṣaṇam kṛtvā nandate mūḍha cetanaḥ karmāṇi adhyāpanam ca eva yājanam ca pratigrahaḥ satyam śāntiḥ tapaḥ śaucam tasya dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ vikrayaḥ rasadhānyānām brāhmaṇasya vigarhitaḥ anāpadi ca śūdra annam vṛṣalī saṃgrahaḥ tathā tapaḥ eva sadā dharmaḥ brāhmaṇasya na saṃśayaḥ sa tu dharma artham utpannaḥ pūrvam dhātrā tapaḥ balāt agni kāryam paraḥ dharmaḥ nityam yajña upavīti tā amṛtāśyam param dharmaḥ gṛha puṣpa baliḥ tathā gṛha saṃmārjanam dharmaḥ ālepana vidhiḥ tathā iṣṭayaḥ paśubandhāḥ ca vidhi pūrvam param tapaḥ daṃpatyoḥ sama śīla tvam dharmaḥ vai gṛhamedhinām tam aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā kṣatriyāḥ tu tathā devi prajānām pālane smṛtāḥ rakṣaṇāt kṣatriyaiḥ eva jagat bhavati śāśvatam tasya api adhyayanam dānam yajanam dharmataḥ smṛtam bhītānām rakṣaṇam ca eva pāpānām anuśāsanam satām saṃpoṣaṇam ca eva kara ṣaṣ bhāga jīvanam utsāhaḥ śastra jīvi tvam tasya dharmāḥ sanātanāḥ bhṛtyānām bharaṇam dharmaḥ kṛte karmaṇi amogha tā evam te vihitāḥ pūrvaiḥ dharmāḥ karma vidhānataḥ tathā eva devi vaiśyāḥ ca lokayātrā hitāḥ smṛtāḥ teṣām adhyayanam dānam yajanam dharmaḥ iṣyate vaiśyasya satatam dharmaḥ pāśupālyam kṛṣiḥ tathā vāṇijyam sat pathe sthānam ātitheyam sadā bhavet viprāṇām svāgatam nyāyaḥ vaiśya dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ evam te vihitā devi lokayātrā svayaṃbhuvā tathā eva śūdrāḥ vihitāḥ sarva dharma prasādhakāḥ śūdrāḥ ca yadi te na syuḥ karma kartā na vidyate trayaḥ pūrve śūdra mūlāḥ tasmāt karmakarāḥ smṛtāḥ brāhmaṇa ādiṣu śuśrūṣā dāsa dharmaḥ iti smṛtaḥ vārtā ca kāru karmāṇi śilpam nāṭyam tathā eva ca te api evam vihitāḥ loke karma yogāḥ śubha ānane evam caturṇām varṇānām puṇya lokāḥ paratra ca vihitāḥ ca tathā dṛṣṭāḥ yathāvat dharma cāriṇām eṣa varṇa āśrayaḥ dharmaḥ karma ca eva tad arpaṇam umā kathitam śrotu kāmāyāḥ kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi maheśvaraḥ śrotum icchāmi aham deva dharmam āśramiṇām vibho atha āśrama gatam dharmam śṛṇu devi samāhitā āśramāṇām tu yaḥ dharmaḥ kriyate brahma vādibhiḥ gṛhasthaḥ pravaraḥ teṣām gārhasthya vratam āśritaḥ pañcayajña kriyā śaucam dāra tuṣṭiḥ atandri tā ṛtu kāla abhigamanam dāna yajña tapāṃsi ca a vipravāsaḥ tasya iṣṭam svādhyāyaḥ ca agni pūjanam atithīnām ābhimukhyam śaktyā ca iṣṭa nimantraṇam anugrahaḥ ca sarveṣām manaḥ vāc kāya karmabhiḥ evamādi śubham ca anyat kuryāt tad vṛttavān gṛhī evam saṃcarataḥ tasya puṇya lokāḥ na saṃśayaḥ tathā eva vānaprasthasya dharmāḥ proktāḥ sanātanāḥ gṛha vāsam samutsṛjya niścitya ekamanāḥ śubhaiḥ bhūmiśayyā jaṭāḥ śmaśru carma valkala dhāraṇam devatā atithi satkāraḥ mahā kṛcchra abhipūjanam agnihotram triṣavaṇam nityam tasya vidhīyate brahmacaryam kṣamā śaucam tasya dharmāḥ sanātanāḥ yati dharmāḥ tathā devi saṅgān tyaktvā yatas tatas ākiṃcanyam anārambhaḥ sarvatas śaucam ārjavam sadā dhyāna para tvam ca doṣa śuddhiḥ kṣamā dayā brahmacārī ca yaḥ devi janma prabhṛti dīkṣitaḥ sarva kāleṣu sarvatra guru pūjām samācaret bhaikṣa caryā agni kāryam ca sadā jala niṣevaṇam svādhyāyaḥ satatam devi tasya dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ tasya ceṣṭā tu guru artham ā prāṇa antam iti sthitiḥ evam saḥ api amalān lokān brāhmaṇaḥ pratipadyate eṣa te kathitaḥ devi dharmaḥ ca āśrama vāsinām umā kathitam te samāsena kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi tapasā karśitāḥ nityam tapoḥ jana parāyaṇāḥ asya kiṃlakṣaṇaḥ dharmaḥ kīdṛśaḥ ca āgamaḥ tathā nāradaḥ evam vadantyām rudrāṇyām ṛṣayaḥ sādhu sādhu iti abruvan hṛṣṭa manasaḥ sarve tad gata mānasāḥ śṛṇvantīm ṛṣi dharmān tu ṛṣayaḥ ca abhyapūjayan tvad prasādāt vayam devi śroṣyāmaḥ paramam hitam dhanyāḥ khalu vayam sarve pāda mūlam tava āśritāḥ maheśvaraḥ iti sarve tadā devīm vācā samabhipūjayan nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā manasvini bahu śākhaḥ bahuvidhaḥ ṛṣi dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ prāyaśas svarga bhoga artham ṛṣibhiḥ kriyate tapaḥ tathā saṃcaratām teṣām devi dharma vidhim śṛṇu bhūtvā pūrvam gṛhasthaḥ tu putra ānṛṇyam avāpya ca kalatra kāryam saṃsthāpya kāraṇāt saṃtyajet gṛham avasthāpya manaḥ dhṛtyā vyavasāya puraḥsaraḥ nirdvaṃdvaḥ vā sa dāraḥ vā vana vāsāya saṃvrajet deśāḥ parama puṇyāḥ ye nadī vana samanvitāḥ ābādha muktāḥ prāyeṇa tīrtha āyatana saṃyutāḥ tatra gatvā vidhim jñātvā dīkṣām kuryāt yathākramam dīkṣitvā ekamanāḥ bhūtvā paricaryām samācaret kālya utthānam ca śaucam ca sarva deva praṇāmanam śakṛt ālepanam kāye tyakta doṣa apramādi tā sāyam prātar ca abhiṣekam agnihotram yathāvidhi kāya śaucam ca kāryam ca jaṭā valkala dhāraṇam nīvāra grahaṇam kāle śāka mūla upacāyanam atithīnām ābhimukhyam tad para tvam ca sarvaśas pādya āsanābhyām saṃpūjā tathā āhāra nimantraṇam a grāmyapacanam kāle pitṛ deva arcanam tathā paścāt atithi satkāraḥ tasya dharmāḥ sanātanāḥ śiṣṭaiḥ dharma āsane ca eva dharma artha sahitāḥ kathāḥ pratiśraya vibhāgaḥ ca bhūmi śayyā śilāsu vā divā rātrau yathāyogam śaucam dharma artha cintanā evam dharmāḥ purā dṛṣṭāḥ sāmānyāḥ vanavāsinām paribhraṣṭaḥ yadā svargāt viśiṣṭaḥ tu bhavet nṛpa evam dharmaḥ sadā devi sarveṣām vana vāsinām umā bhagavan devadeveśaḥ ṛṣīṇām caritam śubham maheśvaraḥ viśeṣa dharmān icchāmi śrotum kautūhalam hi me tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā vīraśayyām upāsadbhiḥ vīrasthāna upasevibhiḥ pañcayajña paraiḥ nityam paurṇamāsya parāyaṇaiḥ maṇḍūkaśāyaiḥ hemante śaivāla aṅkura bhojanaiḥ cīra valkala saṃvītaiḥ mṛga ajina dharaiḥ tathā cāturmāsya paraiḥ kaiḥ cit deva dharma parāyaṇaiḥ evam kṛtvā śubham karma paścāt yāti triviṣṭapam umā tapaḥ viśeṣa saṃyuktāḥ kathitāḥ te śuci smite bhagavan sarva bhūta īśa teṣu ye dāra saṃyutāḥ maheśvaraḥ kīdṛśam caritam teṣām tat me śaṃsitum arhasi ye eka patnī dharmāṇaḥ caranti vipulam tapaḥ puṣkareṣu ca ye ca anye nadī vana samāśritāḥ hiṃsā droha vimuktāḥ ca sarva bhūta anukampinaḥ śāntāḥ dāntāḥ jita krodhāḥ sarva ātithya parāyaṇāḥ prāṇiṣu ātma upamāḥ nityam ṛtu kāla abhigāminaḥ teṣām paricchada ārambhāḥ kṛtyā upakaraṇāni ca gṛhastha vat vidhīyante yathāyogam pramāṇataḥ poṣaṇa artham ca dārāṇām agni kārya artham eva ca gāvaḥ ca karṣaṇam ca eva sarvam etat vidhīyate te sva dāraiḥ samāyānti puṇyān lokān dṛḍha vratāḥ patibhiḥ saha ye dārāḥ caranti vipulam tapaḥ umā maheśvaraḥ vānaprastha gatam sarvam phala pākam śṛṇu priye agniyogam vrajan grīṣme naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam rudra loke hi jāyeta vidhi dṛṣṭena karmaṇā uda vāsa vratam kurvan varṣa kāle dṛḍha vrataḥ kāṣṭha vat maunam āsthāya naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam saṃviśet varṣāṇām bhoga saṃyuktaḥ naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam vīrāsana gataḥ yaḥ tu kaṇṭaka āphalaka āśritaḥ gandharveṣu abhijāyeta naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam vīra sthāyī ca ūrdhvabāhuḥ naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam pādāṅguṣṭhena yaḥ tiṣṭhet ūrdhvabāhuḥ jita indriyaḥ āhāra niyamam kṛtvā muniḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam nāga loke abhijāyeta saṃvatsara gaṇān bahūn evam dṛḍha vratāḥ devi vānaprasthāḥ ca karmabhiḥ sthāneṣu teṣu tiṣṭhanti tadvat bhoga samanvitāḥ tebhyaḥ bhraṣṭāḥ punar devi jāyante nṛṣu bhoginaḥ umā eṣām yāyāvarāṇām tu dharmam icchāmi mānada maheśvaraḥ kṛpayā parayā āviṣṭaḥ tam me brūhi maheśvara dharmam yāyāvarāṇām tu śṛṇu bhāmini tad parā vrata upavāsa śuddha aṅgāḥ tīrtha snāna parāyaṇāḥ dhṛtimantaḥ kṣamā yuktāḥ satya vrata parāyaṇāḥ pakṣa māsa upavāsaiḥ ca karśitāḥ dharma darśinaḥ varṣa śīta ātapaiḥ eva kurvantaḥ paramam tapaḥ divya bhūṣaṇa saṃyuktāḥ vimāna varam āsthitāḥ vicaranti yathākāmam divya strī gaṇa saṃyutāḥ umā maheśvaraḥ teṣām cakracarāṇām tu dharmam icchāmi vai prabho hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu śākaṭikam śubhe saṃvahantaḥ dhuram dāraiḥ śākaṭānām tu sarvadā prārthayante yathā grāmyāḥ śakaṭaiḥ bhaikṣa caryayā tapoḥ jana para adhīnāḥ tapasā kṣīṇa kalmaṣāḥ paryaṭantaḥ diśaḥ sarvāḥ kāma krodha vivarjitāḥ sva vaśāt eva te mṛtyum abhikāṅkṣanti nityaśas indra loke tathā teṣām nirmitāḥ bhoga saṃcayāḥ vara apsarobhiḥ saṃyuktāḥ cira kālam anindite umā maheśvaraḥ vaikhānasānām vai dharmam śrotum icchāmi aham prabho teṣu vaikhānasāḥ nāma vānaprasthāḥ śubha īkṣaṇe tīvreṇa tapasā yuktāḥ dīptimantaḥ sva tejasā satya vrata para adhīnāḥ teṣām niṣkalmaṣam tapaḥ aśmakuṭṭāḥ tathā anye ca dantolūkhalinaḥ tathā śīrṇa parṇa āśinaḥ ca anye uñchayanti tathā apare kapotavratinaḥ ca anye kāpotīm vṛttim āsthitāḥ paśu pracāra niratāḥ phenapāḥ ca tathā apare mṛga vat mṛga caryāyām saṃcaranti tathā apare abbhakṣāḥ vāyubhakṣāḥ ca nirāhārāḥ tathā eva ca sva vaśāt eva te mṛtyum abhikāṅkṣanti nityaśas indra loke tathā teṣām nirmitāḥ bhoga saṃcayāḥ vara apsarobhiḥ saṃyuktāḥ cira kālam anindite umā maheśvaraḥ bhagavan śrotum icchāmi vālakhilyān tapodhanān dharma caryām tathā devi vālakhilya gatām śṛṇu mṛga nirmoka vasanāḥ nirdvaṃdvāḥ te tapodhanāḥ aṅguṣṭha mātrāḥ suśroṇi sveṣu eva aṅgeṣu saṃyutāḥ te mṛtyu viṣaya atītāḥ tapaḥ siddhāḥ tapaḥ balāḥ jita āhārāḥ jita krodhāḥ sarva bhogeṣu niḥspṛhāḥ dyotayante diśaḥ sarvāḥ dharma jñāḥ satya vādinaḥ teṣu eva nirmalam satyam loka artham tu pratiṣṭhitam kṣamayā ca mahābhāge bhūtānām saṃsthitim viduḥ dharma anveṣī tapaḥ kartum yatate satatam priye amara tvam vaśi tvam ca tapasā prārthayet sadā etat te kathitam sarvam śṛṇvantyāḥ te śrutam priye priya artham ṛṣi saṃghasya lokānām hita kāmyayā nāradaḥ iti bruvantam deveśam ṛṣayaḥ ca api tuṣṭuvuḥ umā bhūyas paramakam yatnam tadā prabhṛti cakrire maheśvaraḥ brāhmaṇāḥ loka saṃsāre sṛṣṭāḥ dhātrā guṇa arthinā lokān tārayitum yuktāḥ martyeṣu kṣitidevatāḥ brāhmaṇeṣu abhayaḥ dharmaḥ paramaḥ śubha lakṣaṇaḥ ime hi dharmāḥ loka artham pūrvam sṛṣṭāḥ svayaṃbhuvā pṛthivyām sat janaiḥ nityam kīrtyamānam nibodha me śūdraḥ dharma paraḥ nityam śuśrūṣā abhimanāḥ bhavet ṣaḍ karmāṇi ca proktāni sṛṣṭāni brahmaṇā purā dharmiṣṭhāni variṣṭhāni yāni tāni śṛṇu uttame yajanam yājanam ca eva dānam pātre pratigrahaḥ adhyāpanam adhyayanam ṣaṣ karmā dharma bhāj ṛjuḥ dānam praśasyate nityam brāhmaṇeṣu tri karmasu dāntaḥ brāhmaṇa satkartā su saṃmṛṣṭa niveśanaḥ pādyam arghyam yathānyāyam āsanam śayanam tathā dīpam pratiśrayam ca eva yaḥ dadāti sa dhārmikaḥ pravṛtti lakṣaṇaḥ dharmaḥ gṛhastheṣu vidhīyate tat aham kīrtayiṣyāmi trivargeṣu ca yat yathā ekena aṃśena kāma artham ekam aṃśam vivardhayet nivṛtti lakṣaṇaḥ puṇyaḥ dharmaḥ mokṣe vidhīyate tasya vṛttim pravakṣyāmi tām śṛṇuṣva samāhitā bubhukṣitam pipāsā ārtam atithim śrāntam āgatam pātram iti eva dātavyam sarvasmai dharma kāṅkṣibhiḥ āgamiṣyati yat pātram tat pātram tārayiṣyati kedārāḥ ca taṭākāni sarāṃsi saritaḥ tathā toya pūrṇāni dṛśyante apratarkyāṇi śobhane pīḍayā api ca bhṛtyānām dānam eva viśiṣyate putra dārāḥ dhanam dhānyam na mṛtān anugacchati śreyaḥ dānam ca bhogaḥ ca dhanam prāpya yaśasvini dānena hi mahābhāgāḥ bhavanti manuja adhipāḥ na asti bhūmi samam dānam na asti dāna samaḥ nidhiḥ na asti satyāt paraḥ dharmaḥ na anṛtāt pātakam param āśrame yaḥ tu tapyeta tapaḥ mūla phala aśanaḥ āditya abhimukhaḥ bhūtvā jaṭā valkala saṃvṛtaḥ maṇḍūkaśāyī hemante grīṣme pañcatapāḥ bhavet samyak tapaḥ caranti iha śraddadhānāḥ vana āśrame gṛha āśramasya te devi kalām na arhanti ṣoḍaśīm umā gṛhāśramasya yā caryā vratāni niyamāḥ ca ye yathā ca devatāḥ pūjyāḥ satatam gṛhamedhinā maheśvaraḥ gṛha āśramasya yat mūlam phalam dharmaḥ ayam uttamaḥ pādaiḥ caturbhiḥ satatam dharmaḥ yatra pratiṣṭhitaḥ sāra bhūtam varārohe dadhnaḥ ghṛtam iva uddhṛtam tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śrūyatām dharma cāriṇi śuśrūṣate yaḥ pitaraḥ mātaram ca gṛha āśrame bhartāram ca eva yāḥ nārī agnihotram ca ye dvijāḥ teṣu teṣu ca prīṇanti devāḥ indra purogamāḥ umā mātā pitṛ viyuktānām kā caryā gṛhamedhinām vidhavānām ca nārīṇām bhavān etat bravītu me maheśvaraḥ devatā atithi śuśrūṣā guru vṛddha abhivādanam ahiṃsā sarva bhūtānām alobhaḥ satyasaṃdhatā brahmacaryam śaraṇya tvam śaucam pūrva abhibhāṣaṇam kṛtajña tvam apaiśunyam satatam dharma śīla tā gavāhnika pradānam ca saṃvibhāgaḥ atithiṣu api dīpam pratiśrayam ca eva dadyāt pādya āsanam tathā pañcame ahani ṣaṣṭhe vā dvādaśe vā api atha aṣṭame caturdaśe pañcadaśe brahmacārī sadā bhavet śmaśrukarma śiraḥ abhyaṅgam añjanam dantadhāvanam na eteṣu ahaḥsu kurvīta teṣu alakṣmīḥ pratiṣṭhitā bharaṇam bhṛtya vargasya dīnānām anukampanam para dāra nivṛttiḥ ca sva dāreṣu ratiḥ sadā śarīram ekam daṃpatyoḥ vidhātrā pūrva nirmitam śīla vṛtta vinītasya nigṛhīta indriyasya ca ārjave vartamānasya sarva bhūta hita eṣiṇaḥ gṛha āśrama pada sthasya kim anyaiḥ kṛtyam āśramaiḥ yathā mātaram āśritya sarve jīvanti jantavaḥ tathā gṛha āśramam prāpya sarve jīvanti ca āśramāḥ rājānaḥ sarva pāṣaṇḍāḥ sarve raṅga upajīvinaḥ vyāla grāhāḥ ca dambhāḥ ca corāḥ rāja bhaṭāḥ tathā sa vidyāḥ sarva śilpa jñāḥ sarve ca eva cikitsakāḥ dūra adhvānam prapannāḥ ca kṣīṇa pathya odanāḥ narāḥ mārjārāḥ mūṣikāḥ śvānaḥ sūkarāḥ ca śukāḥ tathā kapota kāka caṭakāḥ sarīsṛpa niṣeviṇaḥ araṇya vāsinaḥ ca anye saṃghāḥ ye mṛga pakṣiṇām gṛhe kṣetre bile ca eva śataśas atha sahasraśas upayuktam ca yat teṣām matimān na anuśocati āśā pāśa vimokṣaḥ ca viddhi dharmam anuttamam vṛkṣa mūla caraḥ nityam śūnya āgāra niveśanam nadī pulina śāyī ca nadī tīram anuvrajan ātma bhūtaḥ yata āhāraḥ mokṣa dṛṣṭena karmaṇā umā saṃgṛhītam mayā tat ca tava vākyam anuttamam idānīm asti saṃdehaḥ mānuṣeṣu eva kaḥ cana tulya pāṇi śiraḥ kāyaḥ rājā ayam iti dṛśyate pretyabhāve katham lokān labhate puṇya karmaṇām maheśvaraḥ rāja vṛttam aham tasmāt śrotum icchāmi mānada tat aham te pravakṣyāmi rāja dharmam śubha ānane rāja āyattam hi tat sarvam loka vṛttam śubha aśubham tapaḥ dāna mayam rājyam param sthānam vidhīyate tasmāt rājñaḥ sadā martyāḥ praṇamanti yatas tatas nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā asi bhāmini arājake purā tu āsīt prajānām saṃkulam mahat tat dṛṣṭvā saṃkulam brahmā manum rājye nyaveśayat tadā prabhṛti saṃdṛṣṭam rājñām vṛttam śubha aśubham yathā pretya labhet svargam yathā vīryam yaśaḥ tathā pitryam vā bhūta pūrvam vā svayam utpādya vā punar anu bhṛtyān prajāḥ paścāt iti eṣa vinaya kramaḥ svāminam ca upamām kṛtvā prajāḥ tad vṛtta kāṅkṣayā svayam vinaya saṃpannāḥ bhavanti iha śubha īkṣaṇe tasmāt pūrvataram rājā ātmānam vinaye nayet yaḥ vinītaḥ svayam rājā vinayati eva vai prajāḥ apahāsyaḥ bhavet tādṛś sva doṣasya anavekṣaṇāt vidyā abhyāsaiḥ vṛddha yogaiḥ ātmānam vinayam nayet vidyā dharma artha phalinī tad vidaḥ vṛddha saṃjñitāḥ indriyāṇām jayaḥ devi atas ūrdhvam udāhṛtam ajaye su mahān doṣaḥ rājānam vinipātayet pañca eva sva vaśān kṛtvā tad arthān pañca śoṣayet ṣaṭ utsṛjya yathāyogam jñānena vinayena ca śāstra cakṣuḥ naya paraḥ bhūtvā bhṛtyān samāharet vṛtta śruta kula upetān upadhābhiḥ parīkṣitān amātyān upadhā atītān sa apasarpān jita indriyān amātyāḥ buddhi saṃpannāḥ rāṣṭram bahu jana priyam durādharṣam pura śreṣṭham kośaḥ kṛcchra sahaḥ smṛtaḥ etāḥ prakṛtayaḥ ṣaḍ ca svāmī ca naya tattva vid prajānām rakṣaṇa arthāya sarvam etat vinirmitam ābhiḥ karaṇa bhūtābhiḥ kuryāt loka hitam nṛpaḥ ātma rakṣā nṛpa indrasya prajā rakṣā artham iṣyate tasmāt satatam ātmānam saṃrakṣet a pramādavān bhojana ācchādanāt snānāt bahis niṣkramaṇāt api nityam strī gaṇa saṃyogāt rakṣet ātmānam ātmavān satatam putra dārebhyaḥ rakṣet ātmānam ātmavān prajānām rakṣaṇa arthāya prajā hita karaḥ bhavet prajā kāryam tu tat kāryam prajā saukhyam tu tat sukham prakṛtīnām vivāda artham rāga dveṣau vyudasya ca ubhayoḥ pakṣayoḥ vādam śrutvā ca eva yathātatham tam artham vimṛśet buddhyā svayam tattva darśanāt kartāram aparādham ca deśa kālau naya anayau jñātvā samyak yathāśāstram tatas daṇḍam nayet nṛṣu pratyakṣa āpta upadeśābhyām anumānena vā punar boddhavyam satatam rājñā deśa vṛttam śubha aśubham cāraiḥ karma pravṛttyā ca tat vijñāya vicārayan daṇḍyān ca daṇḍayet devi na atra kāryā vicāraṇā pañca apekṣam sadā mantram kuryāt buddhi yutaiḥ naraiḥ rājā rāṣṭra hita apekṣaḥ satya dharmāṇi kārayet sarva udyogam svayam kuryāt durga ādiṣu sadā nṛpaḥ deśa vṛddhi karān bhṛtyān apramādena kārayet deśa kṣaya karān sarvān saṃpriyān api varjayet su mukhaḥ su priyaḥ bhūtvā samyak vṛttam samādiśet adharmyam paruṣam tīkṣṇam vākyam vaktum na ca arhati sva iṅgitam vṛṇuyāt dhairyāt na kuryāt kṣudra saṃvidam para iṅgita jñaḥ loka jñaḥ bhūtvā saṃsargam ācaret amānuṣa bhayebhyaḥ ca svāḥ prajāḥ pālayet nṛpaḥ lubdhāḥ kaṭhorāḥ ca adhyakṣāḥ mānavāḥ dasyu vṛttayaḥ nigrāhyāḥ eva te rājñā saṃgṛhītāḥ yatas tatas teṣām ātma guṇa upetam yauvarājye niyojayet prakṛtīnām yathā na syāt rājya bhraṃśa udbhavam bhayam arājakam kṣaṇam api rāṣṭram na syāt hi śobhane ātmanaḥ anuvidhānāya yauvarājyam sadā iṣyate kula jānām ca vaidyānām śrotriyāṇām tapasvinām anyeṣām vṛtta yuktānām viśeṣam kartum arhati ātma artham rājya tantra artham kośa saṃcayam ārabhet durgāt rāṣṭrāt samudrāt ca vaṇigbhyaḥ puruṣa atyayāt parātma guṇa sārābhyām bhṛtya poṣaṇam ācaret vāhanānām vidhāya agre poṣaṇam yaudha karmasu sa ādaraḥ satatam bhūtvā apekṣā vratam ācaret caturdhā vibhajet kośam dharma bhṛtya ātma kāraṇāt āpad artham ca nīti jñaḥ deśa kāla vaśena tu anāthān vyādhitān vṛddhān sva deśe poṣayet nṛpaḥ saṃdhim ca vigraham ca eva tad viśeṣān tathā aparān yathāvat saṃvimṛśya eva buddhi pūrvam samācaret sarveṣām sa priyaḥ bhūtvā maṇḍalam satatam caret śubheṣu api ca kāryeṣu na ca ekaḥ antar samācaret pareṇa dhārmikān nyāyān na atīyāt dveṣa lobhataḥ ku nṛpaiḥ pīḍitāḥ tasmāt prajāḥ sarvatra pālayet yātrā kālam avekṣya eva paścāt kopaḥ phala udayam tad yuktāḥ ca āpadaḥ ca eva prāk yānāt iti cintayet prāyaśas varjayet yuddham prāṇa rakṣaṇa kāraṇāt kāraṇāt eva yoddhavyam na ātmanaḥ para doṣataḥ su yuddhe prāṇa mokṣaḥ ca tasya dharmāya iṣyate abhiyuktaḥ balavatā kuryāt āpad vidhim nṛpaḥ anunīya tathā sarvān prajānām hita kāraṇāt anya prakṛti yuktānām rājñām vṛtta vicāriṇām anyān ca āpad prapannānām na tān saṃyoktum arhati śubha aśubham yadā devi vṛttam tu ubhayatas smṛtam etat evam samāsena rāja dharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ evam saṃvartamānaḥ tu daṇḍayan bhartsayan prajāḥ svarga loke tadā rājā tridaśaiḥ saha toṣyate evam nyāya anugam vṛttam kathitam te śubha īkṣaṇe rājyam nyāya anugam nāma buddhi śāstra anugam bhavet daivapakṣye sati nṛpe rājyam nyāya anugam bhavet kriyante yatra karmāṇi śāstra yuktim vimṛśya vai rājyam bhoga anugam nāma ayathāvat pradṛśyate yatra bhāgya vaśāt bhṛtyāḥ labhante na viśeṣataḥ yatra daṇḍyāḥ na daṇḍyante pūjyante vā nara adhamāḥ śubha aśubham yadā yatra viparītam pradṛśyate rājñi vāsura pakṣe tu tatra bhāgya anugam bhavet bhāgya anuge tu rājānaḥ vartamānāḥ yathā tathā prāpya a kīrtim anartham ca iha loke śubha īkṣaṇe mokṣaṇam duṣkṛtīnām tu vidyate kāla paryayāt na asti eva mokṣaṇam devi rājñām duṣkṛta kāriṇām etat sarvam samāsena rāja vṛttam śubha aśubham umā kathitam te mahābhāge bhūyas śrotum kim icchasi maheśvaraḥ rahasyam śrūyatām devi mānuṣāṇām sukha āvaham yatra vā tatra vā bījam dharma arthī na utsṛjet punar naraḥ bīja vināśena lipyate bhrūṇa hatyayā ahiṃsā paramaḥ dharmaḥ ahiṃsā paramam sukham ahiṃsā dharma śāstreṣu sarveṣu paramam padam devatā atithi śuśrūṣā satatam dharma śīla tā veda adhyayana yajñāḥ ca tapaḥ dānam damaḥ tathā ācārya guru śuśrūṣā tīrtha abhigamanam tathā ahiṃsāyāḥ varārohe kalām na arhanti ṣoḍaśīm umā yajñeṣu paśubandheṣu hanyante paśavaḥ dvijaiḥ katham ca bhagavan bhūyas hiṃsamānāḥ narādhipāḥ yasya eva go sahasrāṇi viṃśatiḥ sādhikāni tu ahani ahani hanyante dvijānām māṃsa kāraṇāt sa māṃsam tu sa dattvā annam rantidevaḥ narādhipaḥ kim nu dharmam na śṛṇvanti na śraddadhati vā śrutam mṛgayāyai vinirgatya mṛgān ghnanti narādhipāḥ etat sarvam viśeṣeṇa vistareṇa vṛṣadhvaja maheśvaraḥ bahu mānyam idam devi nā asti kaḥ cit ahiṃsakaḥ śrūyatām kāraṇam ca atra yathā anekavidham bhavet dṛśyate ca api loke asmin na kaḥ cit avihiṃsakaḥ dharaṇī saṃśritān jīvān su sūkṣmān ca eva madhyamān saṃcaran caraṇābhyām ca hanti jīvān anekaśas ajñānāt jñānataḥ vā api ye jīvāḥ śayana āsane upāviśan śayānaḥ ca hanti jīvān anekaśas śiraḥ vastreṣu ye jīvāḥ narāṇām sveda saṃbhavāḥ tān ca hiṃsanti satatam daṃśān ca maśakān api jale jīvāḥ tathā ākāśe pṛthivī jīva mālinī evam jīva ākule loke kaḥ asau syāt yaḥ tu ahiṃsakaḥ sthūla madhyama sūkṣmaiḥ ca sveda vāta mahīruhaiḥ dṛśya rūpaiḥ adṛśyaiḥ ca nānā rūpaiḥ ca bhāmini jīvaiḥ tatam idam sarvam ākāśam pṛthivī tathā matsyāḥ matsyān grasanti iha khagān ca eva khagāḥ tathā sarīsṛpaiḥ ca jīvanti kapota ādyāḥ vihaṃgamāḥ bhūcarāḥ khecarāḥ ca anye kravyādāḥ māṃsa gṛddhinaḥ samṛddhāḥ para māṃsaiḥ tu bhakṣeran ye api ca aparaiḥ sattvaiḥ sattvāni jīvanti śataśas atha sahasraśas a pīḍayitvā na eva anyam jīvāḥ jīvanti sundari jīvasya ekasya māṃsena payasā rudhireṇa vā tṛpyante bahavaḥ jīvāḥ kravyādāḥ māṃsa bhojinaḥ ekaḥ jīva sahasrāṇi sadā khādati mānavaḥ annādi alpa upabhogena dhānya saṃjñāni yāni tu māṃsa dhānyaiḥ sa bījaiḥ ca bhojanam parivarjayet tri rātram pañca rātram vā sapta rātram atha api vā dhānyāni yaḥ na hiṃseta ahiṃsakaḥ parikīrtitaḥ na aśnāti yāvataḥ jīvān tāvat puṇyena yujyate tapyamāne śarīre tu śarīram ca indriyāṇi api vaśe tiṣṭhanti suśroṇi nṛpāṇām iva kiṃkarāḥ niruṇaddhi indriyāṇi eva sa sukhī sa vicakṣaṇaḥ etat mūlam ahiṃsāyāḥ upavāsaḥ prakīrtitaḥ āhāram kurute yaḥ tu bhūmim ākramate ca yaḥ sarve te hiṃsakāḥ devi yathā dharmeṣu dṛśyate yathā iha ahiṃsakaḥ devi tattva jñaḥ jñāyate naraḥ tathā te aham pravakṣyāmi śrūyatām dharma cāriṇi phalāni jīrṇa parṇāni bhasma vā yaḥ api bhakṣayet ālekhyam iva niśceṣṭam tam manye aham ahiṃsakam ārambhāḥ hiṃsayā yuktāḥ dhūmena agniḥ iva āvṛtāḥ yaḥ tu sarvam samutsṛjya dīkṣitaḥ niyataḥ śuciḥ kṛtvā maṇḍala maryādām saṃkalpam kurute naraḥ yāvajjīvam an āśitvā kāla ākāṅkṣī dṛḍha vrataḥ anyathā hi na paśyāmi naraḥ yaḥ syāt ahiṃsakaḥ yatas yatas mahābhāge hiṃsā syāt mahatī tatas nivṛttau madhu māṃsābhyām hiṃsā tu alpatarā bhavet manasā vinivṛttānām dharmasya nicayaḥ mahān manaḥ pūrvaṃgamāḥ dharmāḥ adharmāḥ ca na saṃśayaḥ nigṛhīte bhavet svargaḥ visṛṣṭe narakaḥ dhruvaḥ āyuḥ kṣayam gate adya eṣām mṛte tu praharāmi aham vidhinā nihataḥ pūrvam nimittam sa tu ghātakaḥ jīvāḥ purākṛtena eva tiryagyoni sarīsṛpāḥ nānā yoniṣu jāyante sva karma pariveṣṭitāḥ nānāvidha vicitra aṅgāḥ nānā vīrya parākramāḥ nānā bhūmi pradeśeṣu nānā āhārāḥ ca jantavaḥ jāyamānasya jīvasya mṛtyuḥ pūrvam prajāyate sukham vā yadi vā duḥkham yathā pūrvam kṛtam tathā na atikrāntum hi śakyam syāt nideśaḥ pūrva karmaṇaḥ samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu kālaḥ kālam nirīkṣate gata āyuṣaḥ hi ākṣipati jīvam sarvasya dehinaḥ araṇye sva gṛhe vā api rātrau vā ahani vā tathā maheśvaraḥ śrūyatām kāraṇam devi yathā hi duratikramaḥ āyuḥ kṣayeṇa upahatāḥ samāgamya varānane kīṭāḥ pataṃgāḥ bahavaḥ sthūlāḥ sūkṣmāḥ ca madhyamāḥ bahūnām mṛga yūthānām nānā vana niṣeviṇām sūnā artham devi baddhānām kṣīṇa āyuḥ yaḥ nibadhyate yathā pakṣi gaṇāḥ kṣipram vistīrṇa ākāśa gāminaḥ kṣīṇa āyuṣaḥ nibadhyante śaktāḥ api palāyitum yathā vāri carāḥ mīnāḥ bahavaḥ bahu jātayaḥ āyuḥ kṣayeṇa upahatāḥ nipadyante sarīsṛpāḥ kṛṣatām karṣakāṇām ca nā asti buddhiḥ vihiṃsane atha eṣām lāṅgala agra ādyaiḥ hanyante jantavaḥ akṣayāḥ pāda agreṇa eva ca ekena yām hiṃsām kurute naraḥ mātaṃgaḥ api na tām kuryāt krūraḥ janma śataiḥ api kṛṣāmi iti matiḥ tasya na asti cintā vihiṃsane vidhinā vihitaḥ pūrvam paścāt prāṇī vipadyate gata āyuṣā na śakyam hi muhūrtam api jīvitum tathā tābhiḥ ca martavyam bhoktavyāḥ ca eva tāḥ tathā gavām vai hanyamānānām rudhira prabhavā nadī carmaṇvatī iti vikhyātā khura śṛṅga asthi durgamā rudhiram tām nadīm prāpya toyam bhavati śobhane tatra abhiṣekam kurvanti kṛta japyāḥ kṛta āhnikāḥ dvijāḥ deva gaṇāḥ ca api lokapālāḥ maheśvarāḥ vidhinā pūrva dṛṣṭena tat māṃsam upakalpitam sa māṃsam ca sadā hi annam śataśas atha sahasraśas bhuñjānānām dvijātīnām astam eti divākaraḥ paṭhyamāneṣu mantreṣu yathānyāyam yathāvidhi tāḥ ca svargam gatāḥ gāvaḥ rantidevaḥ ca pārthivaḥ bhogānām saṃvibhāgena vastra ābharaṇa bhūṣaṇaiḥ sahāya kāle saṃprāpte saṃgrāme śastram uddharet vyūḍha anīke yathā śastram senayoḥ ubhayoḥ api hasti aśva ratha saṃpūrṇe padāti bala saṃkule śakti tomara kunta asi śūla paṭṭasa dhāribhiḥ kūṭamudgara cāpa iṣu bhusuṇḍhī juṣṭa muṣṭibhiḥ bhindipāla gadā cakra prāsa karpaṇa dhāribhiḥ yuddha śauṇḍaiḥ pragarjadbhiḥ vṛṣeṣu vṛṣabhaiḥ iva śaṅkha duṃdubhi nādena nānā tūrya raveṇa ca haya heṣita śabdena kuñjarāṇām ca bṛṃhitaiḥ yodhānām siṃhanādaiḥ ca ghaṇṭānām śiñjita svanaiḥ diśaḥ ca vidiśaḥ ca eva samantāt badhirīkṛtāḥ grīṣma anteṣu vigarjadbhiḥ nabhasi iva balāhakaiḥ kapilābhiḥ iva ākāśe chādyamāne samantataḥ pravṛtte śastra saṃpāte yodhānām tatra senayoḥ palāśa aśoka puṣpāṇām jaṃgamāḥ iva rāśayaḥ śobhamānāḥ raṇe śūrāḥ āhvayantaḥ parasparam hanyamāneṣu abhighnatsu śūreṣu raṇa saṃkaṭe pṛṣṭham dattvā atha ye tatra nāyakasya nara adhamāḥ te duṣkṛtam prapadyante nāyakasya akhilam narāḥ yat ca asti sukṛtam teṣām yujyate tena nāyakaḥ saṃgrāmeṣu na yudhyante bhṛtāḥ ca eva anurūpatas narakam yānti te ghoram bhartṛ piṇḍa apahāriṇaḥ āviṣṭaḥ ca eva sattvena nirghṛṇaḥ jāyate naraḥ yaḥ tu svam nāyakam rakṣan ati ghore raṇa aṅgaṇe tāpayan ari sainyāni siṃhaḥ mṛga gaṇān iva ādityaḥ iva madhyāhne durnirīkṣyaḥ raṇa ajire yajate sa tu pūta ātmā saṃgrāmeṇa mahā kratum varma kṛṣṇājinam tasya daṇḍa kāṣṭham dhanuḥ smṛtam rathaḥ vedī dhvajaḥ yūpaḥ kuśāḥ ca ratha raśmayaḥ mānaḥ darpaḥ tu ahaṃkāraḥ trayaḥ tretāgnayaḥ smṛtāḥ sruc bhāṇḍam ca api yat kim cit yajña upakaraṇāni ca āyudhāni asya tat sarvam samidhaḥ sāyakāḥ smṛtāḥ sveda srāvaḥ ca gātrebhyaḥ kṣaudram tasya yaśasvinaḥ puroḍāśāḥ nṛ śīrṣāṇi rudhiram ca āhutiḥ smṛtam tūṇaḥ ca eva caruḥ jñeyaḥ vasoḥ dhārā vasā smṛtā kravyādāḥ bhūta saṃghāḥ ca tasmin yajñe dvijātayaḥ teṣām bhakṣa anna pānāni hatā nṛ gaja vājinaḥ bhuñjate te yathākāmam yathā yajñe kim icchake nihatānām tu yodhānām vastra ābharaṇa bhūṣaṇam hiraṇyam ca suvarṇam ca yat vai yajñasya dakṣiṇā yathā nadī sahasrāṇi praviṣṭāni mahā udadhim praviṣṭā rāja dharmeṇa ācārya vinayaḥ tathā veda uktāḥ ca eva ye dharmāḥ pāṣaṇḍeṣu ca kīrtitāḥ tathā eva mānavāḥ dharmāḥ dharmāḥ ca anye tathā apare deśa jāti kulānām ca grāma dharmāḥ tathā eva ca ye dharmāḥ pārvatīyeṣu ye dharmāḥ pattana ādiṣu teṣām pūrva pravṛttānām kartavyam parirakṣaṇam prajāḥ pālayate yatra dharmeṇa vasudhādhipaḥ ṣaṭkarma niratāḥ viprāḥ pūjyante pitṛ devatāḥ na eva tasmin anāvṛṣṭiḥ na rogāḥ na api upadravāḥ dharma śīlāḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ svadharma nirate nṛpe eṣṭavyaḥ satatam devi yukta ācāraḥ narādhipaḥ chidra jñaḥ ca eva śatrūṇām apramattaḥ pratāpavān śūdrāḥ pṛthivyām bahavaḥ rājñām bahu vināśakāḥ tasmāt pramādam suśroṇi na kuryāt paṇḍitaḥ nṛpaḥ teṣu mitreṣu tyakteṣu tathā martyeṣu hastiṣu yaḥ tu rāṣṭra hita arthāya go brāhmaṇa kṛte tathā bandi grāhāya mitra arthe prāṇān tyajati dustyajān sarva kāma dughām dhenum dharaṇīm loka dhāriṇīm samudra antām varārohe sa śaila vana kānanām dadyāt devi dvijātibhyaḥ vasu pūrṇām vasuṃdharām na tad samam varārohe prāṇatyāgī viśiṣyate yajñaiḥ tasya kim āścaryam prāṇatyāgaḥ su duṣkaraḥ tasmāt sarveṣu yajñeṣu śastra yajñaḥ viśiṣyate maheśvaraḥ evam saṃgrāma yajñāḥ te yathātattvam udāhṛtāḥ mṛga yātrām tu vakṣyāmi śṛṇu tām dharma cāriṇi mṛgān hatvā mahīpālaḥ yathā pāpaiḥ na lipyate nirmānuṣām imām sarve mṛgāḥ icchanti medinīm bhakṣayanti ca sasyāni śāsitavyāḥ nṛpeṇa te duṣṭānām śāsanam dharmaḥ śiṣṭānām paripālanam kartavyam bhūmipālena nityam kāryeṣu ca ārjavam svargam mṛgāḥ ca gacchanti svayam nṛpatinā hatāḥ yathā gāvaḥ hi a gopālāḥ tathā rāṣṭram anāyakam tasmāt aṃśāḥ tu devānām gandharva uraga rakṣasām rājye niyuktāḥ rāṣṭreṣu prajā pālana kāraṇāt aśiṣṭa śāsane ca eva śiṣṭānām paripālane teṣām caryām pravakṣyāmi śrūyatām anupūrvaśas yathā pracaratām teṣām pārthivānām yaśasvini rāṣṭram dharmaḥ dhanam ca eva yaśaḥ kīrtiḥ ca vardhate nṛpāṇām pūrvam eva ayam dharmaḥ dharma bhṛtām vare sabhā prapāta ṭākāni devatāyatanāni ca brāhmaṇa āvasathāḥ ca eva kartavyāḥ nṛpa sattamaiḥ brāhmaṇāḥ na avamantavyāḥ bhasma channāḥ iva agnayaḥ kulam utsādayeyuḥ te krodha āviṣṭāḥ dvijātayaḥ tathā krodha agninā viprāḥ daheyuḥ pṛthivīm imām na hi vipreṣu kruddheṣu rājyam bhuñjanti bhūmipāḥ paribhūya dvijān mohāt vātāpi nahuṣa ādayaḥ sa bandhu mitrāḥ naṣṭāḥ te dagdhāḥ brāhmaṇa manyubhiḥ śarīram ca api śakrasya kṛtam bhaga nirantaram prasādam kārayām āsuḥ praṇāma stuti vandanaiḥ indrasya vṛṣaṇau bhūtvā kṣipram vai śleṣam āgatau evam vipreṣu kruddheṣu devarājaḥ śatakratuḥ aśaktaḥ śāsitum rājyam kim punar mānuṣaḥ bhuvi krodha āviṣṭaḥ dahet vipraḥ śuṣka indhanam iva analaḥ adevān api devān sa kuryāt devān adevatāḥ tasmāt na utpādayet manyum manyu praharaṇāḥ dvijāḥ mahatsu api aparādheṣu śāsanam na arhati dvijaḥ dṛśyate triṣu lokeṣu brāhmaṇānām anindite krodhāḥ ca vipulāḥ ghorāḥ prasādāḥ ca api anuttamāḥ tasmāt nā utpādayet krodham nityam pūjyāḥ dvijātayaḥ kruddheṣu yaḥ vai vipreṣu rājyam bhuṅkte narādhipaḥ saṃprahāsaḥ ca bhṛtyeṣu na kartavyaḥ narādhipaiḥ laghu tvam ca eva prāpnoti ājñā ca asya nivartate bhṛtyānām saṃprahāsena pārthivaḥ paribhūyate tasmāt bhṛtyeṣu nṛpatiḥ saṃprahāsam vivarjayet sagotreṣu viśeṣeṇa sarva upāyaiḥ na viśvaset viśvāsāt bhayam utpannam hanyāt vṛkṣam iva aśaniḥ pramādāt dhanyate rājā lobhena ca vaśīkṛtaḥ bhaya ārtānām paritrātā dīna anugraha kārakaḥ tasmāt kṛtya viśeṣa jñaḥ nityam rāṣṭra hite rataḥ alubdhaḥ nyāya vādī ca ṣaḍbhāgam ca upajīvati kārya akārya viśeṣa jñaḥ sarvam dharmeṇa paśyati sva rāṣṭreṣu dayām kuryāt akāryam na pravartate aparādha anurūpeṇa duṣṭam daṇḍena śāsayet na adharmaḥ vidyate tatra yatra rājā kṣamā anvitaḥ aśiṣṭa śāsanam dharmaḥ śiṣṭānām paripālanam avadhyāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ gāvaḥ dūtaḥ ca eva pitā tathā vidyām grāhayate yaḥ ca ye ca pūrva upakāriṇaḥ dharaṇīm gām hiraṇyam ca siddha annam ca tilān ghṛtam evam carati yaḥ nityam rājā rāṣṭra hite rataḥ tasya rāṣṭram dhanam dharmaḥ yaśaḥ kīrtiḥ ca vardhate ṣaḍbhāgam upayuñjan yaḥ prajāḥ rājā na rakṣati nirudyogaḥ nṛpaḥ yaḥ ca para rāṣṭra nighātane sva rāṣṭram niṣpratāpasya para cakreṇa hanyate tat pāpam sakalam rājā hatarāṣṭraḥ prapadyate mātulam bhāgineyam vā mātaram śvaśuram gurum svasya rāṣṭrasya rakṣā artham yudhyamānaḥ tu yaḥ hataḥ saṃgrāme para cakreṇa śrūyatām tasya yā gatiḥ śakra lokam itas yāti saṃgrāme nihataḥ nṛpaḥ yāvantaḥ lomakūpāḥ syuḥ tasya gātreṣu sundari tāvat varṣa sahasrāṇi śakra loke mahīyate rājā vā rājamātraḥ vā bhūyas bhavati vīryavān vyavahāraḥ ca cāraḥ ca satatam satya saṃdha tā apramādaḥ pramodaḥ ca vyavasāye api a caṇḍa tā bharaṇam ca eva bhṛtyānām vāhanānām ca poṣaṇam yodhānām ca eva satkāraḥ kṛte karmaṇi amogha tā maheśvaraḥ yūpe nibaddhāḥ mantraiḥ ca yathānyāyam yathāvidhi mantra āhuti vipūtāḥ te svargam yānti yaśasvini tarpitāḥ yajña bhāgeṣu teṣām māṃsaiḥ varānane agnayaḥ tridaśāḥ ca eva lokapālāḥ saha īśvarāḥ teṣu tuṣṭeṣu jāyeta tasya yajñasya yat phalam sa patnīkaḥ sa putraḥ sa sa pitṛ bhrātṛbhiḥ saha umā etat te sarvam ākhyātam bhūyas śrotum kim icchasi bhagavan sarva bhūta īśa śūlapāṇe mahā dyute śrotum icchāmi aham vṛttam sarveṣām gṛhamedhinām kīdṛśam caritam teṣām trivarga sahitam prabho pratyāyatiḥ katham teṣām jīvana artham udāhṛtam vartamānāḥ katham sarve prāpnuvanti uttamām gatim maheśvaraḥ nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā asi bhāmini prāyaśas loka sat vṛttam iṣyate gṛha vāsinām teṣām saṃrakṣaṇa arthāya rājānaḥ saṃsmṛtāḥ bhuvi sarveṣām atha martyānām vṛttim sāmānyatas śṛṇu vidyā vārttā ca sevā ca kāru tvam nāṭya tā tathā iti ete jīvana arthāya martyānām vihitāḥ priye api janma phalam tāvat mānuṣāṇām viśeṣataḥ vihitam tat sva vṛttena tat me nigadataḥ śṛṇu karma kṣetram hi mānuṣyam bhoga duḥkha yutāḥ pare sarveṣām prāṇinām tasmāt mānuṣye vṛttam iṣyate vidyā yogaḥ tu sarveṣām pūrvam eva vidhīyate kārya akāryam vijānāti vidyayā devi nā anyathā vidyayā sphīyate jñānam jñānāt tattva vidarśanam dṛṣṭa tattvaḥ vinīta ātmā sarva arthasya ca bhājanam śakyam vidyā vinītena loke saṃjīvanam sukham ātmānam vidyayā tasmāt pūrvam kṛtvā tu bhājanam bhāvayitvā tathā ātmānam pūjanīyaḥ satām api kula anuvṛttam vṛttam vā pūrvam eva samāśrayet iṣyate gṛha vāsāya dārakarma yathāpuram yadi ced vidyayā ca eva vṛttim kāṅkṣet atha ātmanaḥ rāja vidyām tu vāde api loka vidyām atha api vā tīrthataḥ ca api gṛhṇīyāt śuśrūṣā ādi guṇaiḥ yutaḥ granthataḥ ca arthataḥ ca eva dṛḍhīkuryāt prayatnataḥ evam vidyā phalam devi prāpnuyāt na anyathā naraḥ yadi ced vārttayā vṛttim kāṅkṣeta vidhi pūrvakam kṣetre jala upapanne ca tat yogyām kṛṣim ācaret vāṇijyam vā yathākālam kuryāt tad deśa yogataḥ paśu saṃjīvakaḥ ca eva deśe gāḥ poṣayet dhruvam bahu prakārāḥ bahavaḥ paśavaḥ tasya sādhakāḥ yaḥ kaḥ cit sevayā vṛttim kāṅkṣeta matimān naraḥ yata ātmā śravaṇīyānām bhavet vai saṃprayojakaḥ buddhyā vā karma yogāt vā yodhanāt vā samāśrayet yathā yathā sa tuṣyeta tathā saṃtoṣayeta tam anujīvi guṇa upetaḥ kuryāt ātma artham āśritaḥ vipriyam na ācaret tasya eṣā sevā samāsatas kāru karma ca nāṭyam ca prāyaśas nīca yoniṣu sarveṣām pūrvam ārambhān cintayet naya pūrvakam ātma śaktim upāyān ca deśa kālau ca śaktitaḥ kāraṇāni pravāsam ca prakṣepam ca phala udayam evamādīni saṃcintya dṛṣṭvā daiva anukūla tām vṛttim evam samāsādya tām sadā paripālayet pālayan vardhayan bhuñjan tām prāpya na vināśayet kṣīyate giri saṃkāśam aśnataḥ hi anapekṣayā dharmāya arthāya kāmāya āpad praśamanāya ca caturṣu api vibhāgeṣu vidhānam śṛṇu śobhane yajña artham ca anna dāna artham dīna anugraha kāraṇāt deva brāhmaṇa pūjā artham pitṛ pūjā artham eva ca kula artham saṃnivāsa artham kriyā nityaiḥ ca dhārmikaiḥ evamādiṣu ca anyeṣu dharma artham saṃtyajet dhanam aiśvarya sthāna lābha artham rāja vāllabhya kāraṇāt āvāhe ca vivāhe ca putrāṇām vṛtti kāraṇāt evamādiṣu ca anyeṣu artha artham vibhajet dhanam anubandham phalam hetum dṛṣṭvā vittam parityajet na adhanāḥ prāpnuvanti arthān narāḥ yatna śataiḥ api tasmāt dhanam rakṣitavyam dātavyam ca vidhānataḥ śarīra poṣaṇa arthāya āhārasya viśeṣaṇe naṭa gāndharva saṃyoge kāma yātrā vihārayoḥ manaḥ priyāṇām saṃyoge prīti dāne tathā eva ca evamādiṣu ca anyeṣu kāma artham visṛjet dhanam vicārya guṇa doṣau tu mātrayā tatra saṃtyajet rājya bhraṃśa vināśa artham durbhikṣa artham ca śobhane mahā vyādhi vimokṣa artham vārddhakasya eva kāraṇāt śatrūṇām pratikārāya sāhasaiḥ ca api amarṣaṇāt prasthāne ca anya deśa artham āpadām vipramokṣaṇe sukham arthavatām loke kṛcchrāṇām vipramokṣaṇam aśanāt indriyāṇi iva sarvam arthāt pravartate nidhānam mātrayā kuryāt anyathā vilayam vrajet evam devi manuṣyāṇām loke tu ājīvanam prati evam yuktasya lokasya laukyam vṛttam punar śṛṇu dhanyam yaśasyam āyuṣyam svargīyam paramam vacaḥ trivarge ca yathā yuktam sarveṣām saṃvidhīyate tathā saṃvartamānāḥ tu lokayoḥ hitam āpnuyuḥ kālya utthānam ca śaucam ca deva brāhmaṇa bhakta tā gurūṇām eva śuśrūṣā brāhmaṇeṣu abhivādanam pratyutthānam ca vṛddhānām deva sthāna praṇāmanam ābhimukhyam puraskṛtya atithīnām ca bhojanam vṛddha upadeśa karaṇam śravaṇam hita pathyayoḥ poṣaṇam bhṛtya vargasya sāntva dāna parigrahaiḥ samyak vṛttam sva dāreṣu doṣāṇām pratiṣedhanam putrāṇām vinayam kuryāt tad tad kārye niyojayet varjanam ca aśubha arthānām śubhānām joṣaṇam tathā kula ucitānām dharmāṇām yathāvat paripālanam varjanam kalaha ādīnām bhaktyā saṃśamanam tathā dīnānām saṃgraham ca eva na ānṛśaṃsya vyapekṣayā abaddha anṛta ghorāṇām vacanānām vivarjanam kula saṃdhāraṇam ca eva pauruṣeṇa eva sarvaśas evamādi śubham sarvam tasya vṛttam iti smṛtam vṛddha sevī bhavet nityam hita artham jñāna kāṅkṣayā na yāceta parān dhīraḥ sva bāhu balam āśrayet sva śarīram sadā rakṣet āhāra ācārayoḥ api hitam pathyam sat āhāram jīrṇam bhuñjīta mātrayā devatā atithi satkāram kṛtvā sarvam yathāvidhi śeṣam bhuñjet śuciḥ bhūtvā na ca bhāṣeta vipriyam pratiśrayam ca pānīyam balim bhikṣām ca sarvaśas gṛhasthaḥ pravahet dadyāt gāḥ ca vatsān ca poṣayet bahis niṣkramaṇam ca eva kuryāt kāraṇataḥ divā viṣamāt na avagāheta sva śaktyā samam ācaret yathā āya vyaya tā loke gṛhasthānām prapūjitā bhayāt vā yadi vā lobhāt na kurvīta kadā cana buddhi pūrvam samālokya dūratas guṇa doṣataḥ ātmā sākṣī bhavet nityam ātmanaḥ tu śubha aśubhe umā manasā karmaṇā vācā na ca kāṅkṣeta pātakam ime tu varṇāḥ catvāraḥ vihitāḥ svid svabhāvataḥ uta aho kriyayā varṇāḥ saṃbhavanti maheśvara maheśvaraḥ svabhāvāt eva vidyante catvāraḥ brāhmaṇa ādayaḥ eka jātyāḥ su duṣprāpam anya varṇa tvam āgatam tasmāt te saṃpravakṣyāmi tat sarvam karma pākataḥ brāhmaṇaḥ tu naraḥ bhūtvā sva jātim anupālayet dṛḍham brāhmaṇa karmāṇi veda uktāni samācaret satya ārjava paraḥ bhūtvā dāna yajña paraḥ tathā tasyām jātyām samuditaḥ jāti dharmān na hāpayet evam saṃvartamānaḥ tu kāladharmam gataḥ punar tad kṣaye brāhmaṇaḥ bhūtvā tathā eva nṛṣu jāyate evam sva karmaṇā martyaḥ sva jātim labhate punar avamatya eva tām jātim ajñāna tamasā āvṛtaḥ śūdra vat vicaret loke śūdra karma abhilāṣayā śūdraiḥ saha caran nityam śauca maṅgala varjitaḥ sa ca api kāladharma sthaḥ yama lokeṣu daṇḍitaḥ yadi jāyeta martyeṣu śūdraḥ eva abhijāyate śūdraḥ eva bhavet devi brāhmaṇaḥ api sva karmaṇā tathā eva śūdraḥ tu aparaḥ śūdra karmāṇi varjayan evam yukta samācāraḥ śūdraḥ api maraṇam gataḥ brāhmaṇānām kule mukhye veda svādhyāya saṃyute evam eva sadā loke śūdraḥ brāhmaṇyam āpnuyāt evam kṣatriya vaiśyāḥ ca jāti dharmeṇa saṃyutāḥ sva karmaṇā eva jāyante viśiṣṭeṣu adhameṣu ca evam jāti viparyāsaḥ pretyabhāve bhavet nṛṇām anyathā tu na śakyante loka saṃsthiti kāraṇāt tasmāt jātim viśiṣṭām tu katham cit prāpya paṇḍitaḥ umā maheśvaraḥ śobhana aśobhanam sarvam adhikāra vaśāt svayam śaṭheṣu śaṭha vat kuryāt āryeṣu ṛju vat ācaret āpatsu na avasīdet ca ghorān saṃkrāmayet parān sāmnā eva sarva kāryāṇi kartum pūrvam samārabhet anartha adharma śokān tu yathā na prāpnuyāt svayam etat vṛttam samāsādya gṛham āśritya mānavāḥ nirābādhāḥ nirudvegāḥ prāpnuvanti uttamām gatim etat janma phalam nityam sarveṣām gṛha vāsinām etat sarvam mayā proktam kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi umā mānuṣeṣu eva ye ke cit āḍhyāḥ kleśa vivarjitāḥ bhuñjānāḥ vividhān bhogān dṛśyante nirupadravāḥ apare kleśa saṃyuktāḥ daridrāḥ bhoga varjitāḥ maheśvaraḥ nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā asi bhāmini śṛṇu tat sarvam akhilam mānuṣāṇām hitam vacaḥ ādi sarge purā brahmā sama tvena asṛjat prajāḥ tadā tasmāt samāḥ sarve babhūvuḥ sarvatas narāḥ teṣām hānim tatas dṛṣṭvā tulyānām eva bhāmini brahmāṇam te samājagmuḥ tad kāraṇa nivedakāḥ kartum nā arhasi deveśa pakṣapātam tvam īdṛśam yūyam mā kārṣṭa me roṣam sva kṛtam smarata prajāḥ yuṣmābhiḥ eva yuṣmākam grathitam hi śubha aśubham yādṛśam kurute karma tādṛśam phalam aśnute evam saṃbodhitāḥ tena lokakartrā svayaṃbhuvā evam vijñāta tattvāḥ te dāna dharma parāyaṇāḥ śubhāni vidhivat kṛtvā kāladharma gatāḥ punar apare dharma kāryebhyaḥ nivṛttāḥ tu śubha īkṣaṇe kadaryāḥ niranukrośāḥ prāyeṇa ātma parāyaṇāḥ tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā daridrāḥ kleśa saṃyuktāḥ bhavanti eva na saṃśayaḥ mānuṣeṣu eva ye ke cit dhana dhānya samanvitāḥ maheśvaraḥ tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane phalāni tāni saṃprāpya bhuñjate na kadā cana umā maheśvaraḥ mānuṣāḥ saṃpradṛśyante tat me śaṃsitum arhasi ānṛśaṃsya parāḥ ye tu dharma kāmāḥ ca durgatāḥ para upakāram kurvanti dīna anugraha kāraṇāt pratidadyuḥ para dhanam naṣṭam vā anyaiḥ hṛtam dhanam kāladharma vaśam prāptāḥ punarjanmani te narāḥ dharma dāna upadeśam vā kartavyam iti niścayaḥ umā mānuṣāḥ trividhāḥ deva dṛśyante satatam vibho āsīnāḥ eva bhuñjante sthāna aiśvarya parigrahaiḥ maheśvaraḥ nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā asi bhāmini ye loke mānuṣāḥ devi dāna dharma parāyaṇāḥ pātrāṇi vidhivat jñātvā dūratas hi anumānataḥ dānāni ca iṅgitaiḥ eva taiḥ a vijñātam eva vā punarjanmani deveśi tādṛśāḥ śobhanāḥ narāḥ āsīnāḥ eva bhuñjante bhogān sukṛta bhoginaḥ yathā yathā te dīna tvāt punar dāsyanti yācitāḥ tāvat kālam tathā devi punarjanmani te narāḥ yatnataḥ śrama saṃyuktāḥ punar tāni āpnuvanti uta umā maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu tat tvam samāhitā dharma kāryam ciram kālam vismṛtya dhana saṃyutāḥ prāṇa anta kāle saṃprāpte vyādhibhiḥ ca nipīḍitāḥ ārabhante punar dharmam dātum dānāni vā narāḥ labhante pūrva dattānām phalāni śubha lakṣaṇe umā maheśvaraḥ vyādhi yoga parikliṣṭāḥ ye nirāśāḥ sva jīvite ārabhante tadā kartum dānāni śubha lakṣaṇe umā rūpa yuktāḥ pradṛśyante śubha aṅgā priya darśanāḥ maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu tat tvam samāhitā ye purā manujāḥ devi lajjā yuktāḥ priyaṃvadāḥ śaktāḥ su madhurāḥ nityam bhūtvā ca eva svabhāvataḥ a māṃsa bhojinaḥ ca eva sadā prāṇi dayā yutāḥ pratikarma pradāḥ vā api vastra dāḥ dharma kāraṇāt bhūmi śuddhi karāḥ vā api kāraṇāt agni pūjakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani te narāḥ umā rūpeṇa spṛhaṇīyāḥ tu bhavanti eva na saṃśayaḥ virūpāḥ ca pradṛśyante mānuṣeṣu eva ke cana maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam rūpa yogāt purā martyāḥ darpa ahaṃkāra saṃyutāḥ virūpa hāsakāḥ ca eva stuti nindā ādibhiḥ bhṛśam para upatāpinaḥ ca eva māṃsa ādāḥ ca tathā eva ca abhyasūyā parāḥ ca eva aśuddhāḥ ca tathā eva ca evam yukta samācārāḥ yama loke su daṇḍitāḥ katham cit prāpya mānuṣyam tatra te rūpa varjitāḥ virūpāḥ saṃbhavanti eva nā asti tatra vicāraṇā umā rūpa bhoga vihīnāḥ ca dṛśyante pramadā priyāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi saumya śīlāḥ priyaṃvadāḥ sva dāraiḥ eva saṃtuṣṭāḥ dāreṣu sama vṛttayaḥ na tu pratyādiśanti eva strī doṣān guhya saṃśritān anna pānīya dāḥ kāle nṛṇām svādu pradāḥ ca ye evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane mānuṣāḥ te bhavanti eva satatam subhagāḥ bhṛśam umā durbhagāḥ saṃpradṛśyante āḍhyāḥ bhoga yutāḥ api maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu sarvam samāhitā ye purā manujāḥ devi sva dāreṣu anapekṣayā yathā iṣṭa vṛttayaḥ ca eva nirlajjāḥ vīta saṃbhramāḥ pareṣām vipriya karāḥ vāc manaḥ kāya karmabhiḥ nirāśrayāḥ nirannādyāḥ strīṇām hṛdaya kopanāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani te narāḥ durbhagāḥ saṃbhavanti eva strīṇām hṛdaya vipriyāḥ umā nā asti teṣām rati sukham sva dāreṣu api kim cana jñāna vijñāna saṃpannāḥ buddhimantaḥ vicakṣaṇāḥ durgatāḥ tu pradṛśyante yatamānāḥ yathāvidhi maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam nirāśrayāḥ nirannādyāḥ bhṛśam ātma parāyaṇāḥ umā mūrkhāḥ loke pradṛśyante dṛḍham mūḍhāḥ vicetasaḥ jñāna vijñāna rahitāḥ samṛddhāḥ ca samantataḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi bāliśāḥ api sarvatas samācaranti dānāni dīna anugraha kāraṇāt paṇḍitaḥ apaṇḍitaḥ vā api bhuṅkte dāna phalam naraḥ umā medhāvinaḥ śruti dharāḥ bhavanti viśada akṣarāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi guru śuśrūṣakāḥ bhṛśam jñāna artham te tu saṃgṛhya tīrthataḥ vidhi pūrvakam a ślāghamānāḥ jñānena praśāntāḥ yata vācakāḥ tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā medhāvinaḥ śruti dharāḥ bhavanti viśada akṣarāḥ bahiṣkṛtāḥ pradṛśyante śruta vijñāna buddhibhiḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi jñāna darpa samanvitāḥ ślāghamānāḥ ca tat prāpya jñāna ahaṃkāra mohitāḥ tapanti ye parān nityam jñāna ādhikyena darpitāḥ jñānāt asūyām kurvanti na sahanti eva ca aparān tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane mānuṣyam su ciram prāpya tatra bodha vivarjitāḥ umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit sarva kalyāṇa saṃyutāḥ vyādhi hīnāḥ nirābādhāḥ rūpa ārogya balaiḥ yutāḥ dhana dhānyena saṃpannāḥ prāsādaiḥ yāna vāhanaiḥ maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu sarvam samāhitā ye purā manujāḥ devi āḍhyāḥ vā itare api vā śruta vṛtta samāyuktāḥ dāna kāmāḥ śruta priyāḥ para iṅgita parāḥ nityam dātavyam iti niścitāḥ satya saṃdhāḥ kṣamā śīlāḥ lobha moha vivarjitāḥ dātāraḥ pātrataḥ dānam vrataiḥ niyama saṃyutāḥ sva duḥkham iva saṃsmṛtya para duḥkham mahātmanaḥ saumya śīlāḥ śubha ācārāḥ deva brāhmaṇa pūjakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane mānuṣeṣu api ye jātāḥ tādṛśāḥ saṃbhavanti te yādṛśāḥ tu tvayā proktāḥ sarve kalyāṇa saṃyutāḥ rūpam dravyam balam ca āyuḥ bhoga aiśvaryam kulam śrutam iti etat sarva sādguṇyam dānāt bhavati na anyathā umā atha ke cit pradṛśyante mānuṣeṣu eva mānuṣāḥ durgatāḥ kleśa bhūyiṣṭhāḥ dāna bhoga vivarjitāḥ bhayaiḥ tribhiḥ sadā juṣṭāḥ vyādhi kṣudh bhaya pīḍitāḥ duṣkalatra abhibhūtāḥ ca satatam vighna darśakāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi āsuram bhāvam āśritāḥ krodha lobha ratāḥ nityam nirannādyāḥ ca niṣkriyāḥ nāstikāḥ ca eva dhūrtāḥ ca mūrkhāḥ ca ātma parāyaṇāḥ para upatāpinaḥ devi prāyaśas prāṇi nirdayāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane katham cit prāpya mānuṣyam tatra te duḥkha pīḍitāḥ śubha aśubham kṛtam karma sukha duḥkha phala udayam umā jāti andhāḥ ca eva dṛśyante jātāḥ vā naṣṭa cakṣuṣaḥ maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam ye purā kāma rāgeṇa para veśmasu lolupāḥ para striyaḥ abhivīkṣante duṣṭena eva sva cakṣuṣā lakṣaṇa jñāḥ ca rūpeṣu ayathāvat pradarśakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ kāladharma gatāḥ tu te daṇḍitāḥ yama daṇḍena niraya sthāḥ ciram priye yadi ced mānuṣam janma labheran tādṛśāḥ narāḥ svabhāvataḥ vā jātāḥ vai andhāḥ eva bhavanti te akṣi roga yutā vā api nā asti tatra vicāraṇā umā mukha roga yutāḥ ke cit kliśyante satatam narāḥ ādi prabhṛti martyāḥ vā jātāḥ vā api atha kāraṇāt maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā kuvaktāraḥ tu ye devi jihvayā kaṭukam bhṛśam asatyam paruṣam ghoram gurūn prati parān prati jihvā bādhām tathā anyeṣām kurvate kopa kāraṇāt prāyaśas anṛta bhūyiṣṭhāḥ narāḥ kārya vaśena vā teṣām jihvā pradeśa sthāḥ vyādhayaḥ saṃbhavanti te ku śrotāraḥ tu ye ca artham pareṣām karṇa nāśakāḥ umā anye mukha āśritāḥ doṣāḥ sarve ca ātma kṛtam phalam pīḍyante satatam deva mānuṣeṣu eva ke cana kukṣi pakṣa āśritaiḥ doṣaiḥ vyādhibhiḥ ca udara āśritaiḥ tīkṣṇa śūlaiḥ ca pīḍyante narāḥ duḥkha pariplutāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi kāma krodha yutāḥ bhṛśam ātma artham eva ca āhāram bhuñjate nirapekṣakāḥ abhakṣya āhāra dāḥ ca eva viśvastānām viṣa pradāḥ abhakṣya bhakṣya dāḥ ca eva śauca maṅgala varjitāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane katham cit prāpya mānuṣyam tatra te vyādhi pīḍitāḥ taiḥ taiḥ bahuvidha ākāraiḥ vyādhibhiḥ duḥkha pīḍitāḥ umā dṛśyante satatam deva vyādhibhiḥ mehana āśritaiḥ pīḍyamānāḥ tathā martyāḥ aśmarī śarkarā ādibhiḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi para dāra pradharṣakāḥ kāma doṣeṇa ye dhūrtāḥ kanyāsu vidhavāsu ca balātkāreṇa gacchanti rūpa darpa dhana anvitāḥ tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane yadi ced mānuṣam janma labheran te tathāvidhāḥ umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit dṛśyante śoṣiṇaḥ kṛśāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi māṃsa lubdhāḥ su lolupāḥ ātma artham svādu gṛddhāḥ ca para bhoga upatāpinaḥ abhyasūyā parāḥ ca eva para bhogeṣu ye narāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane śoṣa vyādhi yutāḥ tatra narāḥ dhamani saṃtatāḥ umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit kliśyante kuṣṭha rogiṇaḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi pareṣām rūpa nāśakāḥ āghāta vadha bandhaiḥ ca vṛthā daṇḍena mohitāḥ iṣṭa nāśa karāḥ ye tu apathya āhāra dāḥ narāḥ cikitsakāḥ vā duṣṭāḥ ca lobha moha samanvitāḥ nirdayāḥ prāṇi hiṃsāyām mala dāḥ citta nāśakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane yadi vai mānuṣam janma labheran te su duḥkhitāḥ atra te kleśa saṃyuktāḥ kuṣṭha roga samanvitāḥ ke cit tvac doṣa saṃyuktāḥ vraṇa kuṣṭhaiḥ ca saṃyutāḥ śvitra kuṣṭha yutāḥ vā api bahudhā kuṣṭha saṃyutāḥ umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cid aṅga hīnāḥ ca paṅgavaḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi lobha moha samāvṛtāḥ prāṇinām prāṇa hiṃsā artham aṅga hīnam prakurvate śastreṇa utkṛtya vā devi prāṇinām ceṣṭana aśakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā svabhāvataḥ vā jātāḥ vā paṅgavaḥ tu bhavanti te bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cid granthibhiḥ piṭakaiḥ tathā maheśvaraḥ kliśyamānāḥ pradṛśyante tat me śaṃsitum arhasi ye purā manujāḥ devi granthibheda karāḥ nṛṇām muṣṭi prahāra paruṣāḥ nṛśaṃsāḥ pāpa kāriṇaḥ pāṭakāḥ todakāḥ ca eva śūlatunnāḥ tathā eva ca evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā granthibhiḥ piṭakaiḥ ca eva kliśyante bhṛśa duḥkhitāḥ bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit pāda roga samanvitāḥ maheśvaraḥ manujāḥ devatā sthānam sva pādaiḥ bhraṃśayanti uta
