agnau ca vartamānaḥ viceṣṭate sa jantuḥ sarva bhūta ātmā puruṣaḥ sa sanātanaḥ manaḥ buddhiḥ ahaṃkāraḥ bhūtāni viṣayān ca saḥ basti mūlam gudam ca eva pāvakam ca samāśritaḥ vahan mūtram purīṣam ca api apānaḥ parivartate udānaḥ iti tam vidyuḥ adhyātma kuśalāḥ janāḥ saṃdhau saṃdhau saṃniviṣṭaḥ sarva ceṣṭā pravartakaḥ śarīreṣu manuṣyāṇām vyānaḥ iti upadiśyate dhātuṣu agnau ca vitataḥ samānaḥ agniḥ samīraṇaḥ saḥ eva sarva ceṣṭānām anta kāle nivartakaḥ prāṇānām saṃnipāteṣu saṃsargāt yaḥ prajāyate ūṣmā saḥ agniḥ iti jñeyaḥ saḥ annam pacati dehinām apāna prāṇayoḥ madhye prāṇa apāna samāhitaḥ pṛṣṭhatas tu samānena svām svām gatim upāśritaḥ śarīra madhye nābhiḥ syāt nābhyām agniḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ agnau prāṇāḥ ca saṃsaktāḥ prāṇeṣu ātmā vyavasthitaḥ pakvāśayaḥ tu adhas nābheḥ ūrdhvam āmāśayaḥ smṛtaḥ nābhiḥ madhye śarīrasya sarva prāṇāḥ tam āśritāḥ sthitāḥ prāṇa ādayaḥ sarve tiryak ūrdhvam adhas carāḥ vahanti anna rasān nāḍyaḥ daśa prāṇa pracoditāḥ yoginām eṣa mārgaḥ tu pañcasu eteṣu tiṣṭhati jita śramaḥ samāsīnaḥ mūrdhani ātmānam ādadhet saṃnirudhya tatas prāṇān ātmānam cintayet param prāṇe tu apānam yuñjīta prāṇān ca apāna karmaṇi prāṇa apāna gatī ruddhvā prāṇāyāma paraḥ bhavet evam antar prayuñjīta pañca prāṇān parasparam vijane saṃmita āhāraḥ muniḥ tūṣṇīm nirucchvasan aśrāntaḥ cintayet yogam utthāya ca punar punar prasīdati manaḥ kṣipram prasanne dṛśyate paraḥ vaidyutaḥ agniḥ iva ākāśe dṛśyate puruṣaḥ avyayaḥ dṛṣṭvā tat ātmānaḥ jyotiḥ aiśvarya aṣṭaguṇaiḥ yutam prāpnoti paramam sthānam spṛhaṇīyam suraiḥ api imān yogasya doṣān ca daśa eva paricakṣate doṣaiḥ vighnaḥ varārohe yoginām kavibhiḥ smṛtaḥ kāmam krodham bhayam svapnam sneham ati aśanam tathā vaicityam vyādhim ālasyam lobham ca daśamam smṛtam tasmāt etān apāsya ādau yuñjīta capalam manaḥ imān api guṇān aṣṭau yogasya paricakṣate aṇimā mahimā ca eva prāptiḥ prākāśya lāghave īśitvam ca vaśitvam ca yatra kāmāvasāyi tā etān aṣṭau guṇān prāpya katham cit yoginām varāḥ īśāḥ sarvasya lokasya devān api atiśerate na ca ati svapna śīlasya na ati jāgarataḥ tathā sāyujyam devasātkṛtya prayuñjīta ātma bhaktitaḥ arcayitvā yathāśakti svakam devam viśanti te sāyujyānām viśiṣṭe dve māmakam vaiṣṇavam tathā mām prāpya na nivartante viṣṇum vā śubha locane umā triyakṣa tridaśa śreṣṭha tryambaka tridaśādhipa dakṣa yajña pramathana śūlapāṇe ari sūdana bhavatā parituṣṭena śṛṇvantyā mama bhāṣitam idānīm śrotum icchāmi sāyujyam tvad gatam vibho ācāraḥ kīdṛśaḥ teṣām kena tuṣṭaḥ bhavet bhavān maheśvaraḥ varṇyamānam tvayā sākṣāt prīṇayati adhikam hi mām sāṃkhya yogau mayā sṛṣṭau sarvam ca api carācaram arcanīyaḥ aham īśaḥ aham avyayaḥ aham sanātanaḥ aham prasannaḥ bhaktānām dadāmi amara tām api na mām viduḥ sura gaṇāḥ munayaḥ ca tapodhanāḥ āśramebhyaḥ caturbhyaḥ aham caturaḥ brāhmaṇān śubhe mad bhaktān nirmalān puṇyān samānīya tapasvinaḥ vyācakhye aham tadā devi yogam pāśupatam mahat gṛhītam tat ca taiḥ sarvam mukhāt ca mama dakṣiṇāt śrutvā ca triṣu lokeṣu sthāpitam ca api taiḥ punar idānīm ca tvayā pṛṣṭaḥ tvad priya artham anindite tat sarvam kathayiṣyāmi tat tvam ekamanāḥ śṛṇu aham paśupatiḥ nāma mad bhaktāḥ ye ca mānavāḥ sarve pāśupatāḥ jñeyāḥ bhasma digdha tanūruhāḥ rakṣā artham maṅgala artham ca pavitra artham ca bhāmini liṅga artham ca eva bhaktānām bhasma dattam mayā purā tena saṃdigdha sarva aṅgāḥ bhasmanā brahmacāriṇaḥ jaṭilāḥ muṇḍitāḥ vā api nānākṛta śikhaṇḍinaḥ vikṛtāḥ piṅgala ābhāḥ ca nagnāḥ nānā prahāriṇaḥ bhaikṣam carantaḥ sarvatra niḥspṛhāḥ niṣparigrahāḥ mama pāśupatam divyam yoga śāstram anuttamam sūkṣmam sarveṣu lokeṣu vimṛśantaḥ caranti te evam nitya abhiyuktānām mad bhaktānām tapasvinām upāyam cintayāmi āśu yena mām upayānti te sthāpitam triṣu lokeṣu śiva liṅgam mayā priye iṣṭam dattam adhītam ca yajñāḥ ca saha dakṣiṇāḥ śiva liṅga praṇāmasya kalām na arhanti ṣoḍaśīm arcayā śiva liṅgasya parituṣyāmi aham priye śiva liṅga arcanāyām tu vidhānam api me śṛṇu go kṣīra navanītābhyām arcayet yaḥ śivam mama sa phalam prāpnuyāt martyaḥ brāhmaṇasya agnihotriṇaḥ sa ghṛtam guggulum samyak dhūpayet yaḥ śiva antike yaḥ tu deśa antaram gatvā śiva liṅgam samarcayet tasmāt sarva manuṣyeṣu na asti me priya kṛttamaḥ evam nānāvidhaiḥ dravyaiḥ śiva liṅgam samarcayet mad prasādāt manuṣyeṣu na punar jāyate naraḥ palāśa bilva patrāṇi rājavṛkṣa srajaḥ tathā arka puṣpāṇi medhyāni mad priyāṇi viśeṣataḥ phalam vā yadi vā śākam puṣpam vā yadi vā jalam mad bhaktāḥ na vinaśyanti mad bhaktāḥ vīta kalmaṣāḥ mad bhaktāḥ sarva lokeṣu pūjanīyāḥ viśeṣataḥ mad dveṣiṇaḥ ca ye martyāḥ mad bhakta dveṣiṇaḥ tathā yānti te narakam kṣipram iṣṭvā kratu śataiḥ api etat te sarvam ākhyātam yogam pāśupatam mama śṛṇuyāt yaḥ paṭhet vā api mama etam dharma niścayam nāradaḥ svargam kīrtim dhanam dhānyam labheta sa nara uttamaḥ evam uktvā mahādevaḥ śrotu kāmaḥ svayam prabhuḥ maheśvaraḥ anukūlām priyām bhāryām pārśva sthām samabhāṣata parāvara jñe dharmāṇām tapovana nivāsinām dīkṣā dama śama upete satatam vrata cāriṇi pṛcchāmi tvā varārohe pṛṣṭā vada mama īpsitam sāvitrī brahmaṇaḥ patnī kauśikasya śacī śubhā lakṣmīḥ viṣṇoḥ priyatamā dhṛtiḥ bhāryā yamasya tu mārkaṇḍeyasya dhūmorṇā ṛddhiḥ vaiśravaṇasya tu rohiṇī śaśinaḥ bhāryā svāhā vahneḥ aninditā kaśyapasya aditiḥ ca eva vasiṣṭhasya api arundhatī etāḥ ca anyāḥ ca devyaḥ tu sarvāḥ tāḥ pati devatāḥ śrūyante loka vikhyātāḥ tvayā ca eva saha uṣitāḥ tābhiḥ ca pūjitā sā tvam anuvṛttya anubhāṣaṇaiḥ tasmāt tvām paripṛcchāmi dharma jñe loka saṃmate sa dharma cāriṇī me tvam samāna vrata cāriṇī samāna sārā vīryeṇa mayā eva tvam śubha ānane mama eva ardhāt śarīrasya nirmitā asi purā śubhe sura kāryam karoṣi tvam loka saṃdhāraṇī tathā ayam hi strī gaṇaḥ tvām tu anuyāti na muñcati tvad prasādāt hitam śrotum strī vṛttam śubha lakṣaṇe tvayā ca uktam viśeṣeṇa guru bhūtam hi tiṣṭhati tvayā eva viditaḥ hi arthaḥ strīṇām dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ tasmāt tvā paripṛcchāmi pṛṣṭā vada mama īpsitam nāradaḥ evam uktā tadā devī mahādevena śobhanā punar punar tadā devaḥ devīm kim iti ca abravīt bahuśas coditā devī sa vrīḍā ca idam abravīt umā maheśvaraḥ tvad antike mayā vaktum strīṇām dharmam katham bhavet umā imāḥ nadyaḥ mahādeva sarva tīrtha udaka anvitāḥ ayuktam satsu tad jñeṣu tān atikramya bhāṣitum nāradaḥ mayā saṃmānitāḥ ca eva bhaviṣyanti sarit varāḥ iti matvā mahādevī nadīḥ devīḥ samāhvayat vipāśām ca vitastām ca candrabhāgām sarasvatīm śatadrum devikām sindhum gomatīm kauśikīm tathā yamunām narmadām ca eva kāverīm atha nimnagām tathā devanadīm gaṅgām prāptām tripathagām śubhām sarva tīrtha udaka vahām sarva pāpa vināśanīm umā etāḥ nadīḥ samāhūya samudvīkṣya idam abravīt he he puṇyāḥ sarit śreṣṭhāḥ sarva pāpa vinodakāḥ jñāna vijñāna saṃpannāḥ śṛṇudhvam vacanam mama ayam bhagavatā praśnaḥ uktaḥ strī dharmam āśritaḥ yuṣmābhiḥ tat vicārya eva vaktum icchāmi śobhanāḥ nāradaḥ iti pṛṣṭāḥ tayā devyā deva nadyaḥ cakampire tāsām śreṣṭhatamā gaṅgā vacanam ca idam abravīt dhanyāḥ ca anugṛhītāḥ sma anena vacanena te yā tvam sura asuraiḥ mānyāḥ nadīḥ mānayase anaghe tava eva arhanti kalyāṇi evam sāntva prasādanam aśakyam api ye mūrkhāḥ sva ātma saṃbhāvanā yutāḥ yaḥ śaktaḥ ca an ahaṃvādī su durlabhatamaḥ mataḥ tvam hi śaktā satī devī vaktum praśnam aśeṣatas vyāhartum nā icchasi strī tvāt saṃpūjayasi naḥ tathā tvam hi deva samā devi ūha apoha viśāradā tvam eva arhasi tat vaktum strīṇām dharmam śubha aśubham yācāmahe vayam śrotum amṛtam tvad mukha udgatam nāradaḥ evam prasāditā devī gaṅgayā loka pūjitā umā prāha sarvam aśeṣeṇa strī dharmam surasundarī tvad prasādāt suraśreṣṭha tava eva priya kāmyayā nāradaḥ evam bruvantyām strī dharmam devyām devasya śāsanāt ṛṣi gandharva yakṣāṇām yoṣitaḥ ca apsaraḥ gaṇāḥ nāga bhūta striyaḥ ca eva nadyaḥ ca eva samāgatāḥ śrotu kāmāḥ param vākyam sarvāḥ paryavatasthire umā devī mudā yuktā pūjyamānā aṅganā gaṇaiḥ ānṛśaṃsya parā devī satatam strī gaṇam prati strī gaṇasya hita arthāya bhava priya cikīrṣayā umā vaktum vacanam ārebhe strīṇām dharma āśritam hitam ṛtu prāptā su śuddhā yā kanyā sā iti abhidhīyate tām tu kanyām pitā mātā bhrātā mātulaḥ eva vā pitṛvyaḥ ca iti pañca ete dātum prabhava tām gatāḥ vivāhāḥ ca tathā pañca tāsām dharma artha kāraṇāt kāmataḥ ca mithas dānam bhayāt ca prasabhāt tathā dattā yasya bhavet bhāryā eteṣām yena kena cit dātāraḥ saṃvimṛśya eva dātum arhanti na anyathā uttamānām tu varṇānām mantravat pāṇi saṃgrahaḥ vivāha karaṇam ca āhuḥ śūdrāṇām saṃprayogataḥ tadā prabhṛti sā nārī daśa rātram vivarjya ca manasā karmaṇā vācā hi anukūlā ca sā bhavet iti bhartṛ vratam kuryāt patim uddiśya śobhanā tadā prabhṛti sā nārī tat tat kuryāt pati priyam pativratā tvam nārīṇām etat eva samāsatas tādṛśā sā bhavet nityam yādṛśaḥ tu bhavet patiḥ śubha aśubha samācāre etat vṛttam samāsatas daivatam satatam sādhvī bhartāram yā anupaśyati etasmin kāraṇe deva paurāṇī śrūyate śrutiḥ kathayāmi prasādāt te śṛṇu deva samāhitaḥ tayoḥ ekā dharma kāmā devān uddiśya bhaktitaḥ cakāra vipulam dharmam pūjana arcanayā anvitam aparā dharma kāmā ca patim uddiśya śobhanā devapriyāyām tiṣṭhantyām puṇya lokam jagāma sā devapriyā ca tiṣṭhantī vilalāpa su duḥkhitā tām yamaḥ lokapālaḥ tu babhāṣe puṣkalam vacaḥ mā śocīḥ tvam nivartasva na lokāḥ santi te śubhe daivatam hi patiḥ nāryāḥ sthāpitam sarva daivataiḥ avamatya śubhe tam tvam katham lokān gamiṣyasi tadā gantāsi lokān tān yān gacchanti pativratāḥ na anyathā śakyate prāptum patnīnām lokaḥ uttamaḥ umā patimatyāḥ divārātram vṛttāntaḥ śrūyatām śubhaḥ patyuḥ pūrvam samutthāya prātaḥkarma samādiśet tatas paurvāhṇikam kāryam svayam kuryāt yathāvidhi nivedya ca tathā āhāram yathā saṃpadyatām iti śuśrūṣā poṣaṇa vidhau pati priya cikīrṣayā bhartuḥ niṣkramaṇe kāryam saṃsmaret apramādataḥ āgatam tu patim dṛṣṭvā sahasā paricārakaiḥ svayam kurvīta saṃprītyā kāya śrama haram param pādya āsanābhyām śayanaiḥ vākyaiḥ ca hṛdaya priyaiḥ atithīnām āgamane prīti yuktā sadā bhavet karmaṇā vacanena api toṣayet atithīn sadā maṅgalyam gṛha śaucam ca sarva upakaraṇāni ca sā yatnam paramam kuryāt tasya sāhāyya kāraṇāt yugaṃdharā bhavet sādhvī patyuḥ dharma arthayoḥ sadā vihāra kāle vai bhartuḥ jñātvā bhāvam hṛdi sthitam alaṃkṛtya yathāyogam hāva bhāva samanvitā kaṭhorāṇi na vācyāni anyadā pramadā antare yasyām kāmī bhavet bhartā tasyāḥ prīti karā bhavet anantaram tathā anyeṣām bhojana avekṣaṇam caret dāsī dāsa balīvardān caṇḍālān ca śunaḥ tathā anāthān kṛpaṇān ca eva bhikṣukān ca tathā eva ca kupitam vā artha hīnam vā śrāntam vā upacaret patim yathā sa tuṣṭaḥ svasthaḥ ca bhavet saṃtoṣayet patim tathā kuṭumba cintāyām vivāde ca artha saṃśaye āhūtā ca sahāya artham tadā priya hitam vadet apriyam vā hitam brūyāt tasya kāma artha kāṅkṣayā bahis ālokanam ca eva moham vrīḍām ca paiśunam bahu āśi tvam divāsvapnam evamādīni varjayet rahasi eka āsanam sādhvī na kuryāt ātmajaiḥ api vismṛtam vā pati dravyam pratidadyāt sva śaucataḥ atiyācñām atīrṣyām ca dūratas parivarjayet bāla vat vṛddha vat nāryāḥ sadā eva ātura vat patiḥ spṛhā yasyāḥ yathā patyau sā nārīṇām pativratā patiḥ hi daivatam strīṇām patiḥ bandhuḥ patiḥ gatiḥ na anyām gatim aham paśye pramadānām yathā patiḥ jātiṣu api ca vai strī tvam viśiṣṭam iti me matiḥ kāya kleśena mahatā puruṣaḥ prāpnuyāt phalam yathāsukham patimatī sarvam pati anukūlataḥ īdṛśam dharma saukaryam paśyadhvam pramadāḥ prati tapaḥ caryā ca dānam ca patau tasyāḥ samarpitau rūpam kulam yaśaḥ svargaḥ sarvam tasmin pratiṣṭhitam evaṃvṛtta samācārā sva vṛttena eva śobhanā patinā ca samam gacchet puṇya lokān sva karmaṇā evaṃbhūtaḥ api bhartā vai strīṇām bhūṣaṇam uttamam āḍhyam vā rūpa yuktam vā virūpam dhana varjitam yā patim toṣayet sādhvī sā nārīṇām pativratā daridrān ca virūpān ca pramūḍhān kuṣṭha saṃyutān evaṃvidhān patīn deva toṣayitvā pativratā evam pravartamānāyāḥ patiḥ pūrvam mriyeta ced umā etat evam mayā proktam strīṇām dharma āśritam hitam saumya śīlāḥ śubha ācārāḥ sarvāḥ tāḥ saṃbhavanti hi atha aśubham pravakṣyāmi strīṇām vṛttam maheśvara āsuryaḥ ca eva paiśācyaḥ rākṣasyaḥ ca bhavanti yāḥ tāsām vṛttam aśeṣeṇa śrūyatām loka kāraṇāt nyāyataḥ vā anyathā proktāḥ bhāva doṣa samanvitāḥ bhartṝn apacaranti eva rāga dveṣa balātkṛtāḥ pravṛddha viṣayāḥ nityam pratikūlam vadanti ca sva buddhi niratāḥ bhūtvā jīvanti ca yathā tathā punar bhavanti pāpiṣṭhāḥ viṣamam vṛttam āśritāḥ anavasthita maryādāḥ bahu veṣāḥ vyaya priyāḥ asaṃtuṣṭāḥ ca lubdhāḥ ca īrṣyā krodha yutāḥ bhṛśam bhoga priyāḥ hita dveṣyāḥ kāma bhoga parāyaṇāḥ patīn paribhavanti eva pratikūla parāyaṇāḥ prāyaśas anṛta bhūyiṣṭhāḥ gurūṇām pratilomakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ āsuram bhāvam āśritāḥ aparāḥ pāpa kāriṇyaḥ satatam kalaha priyāḥ paruṣāḥ rūkṣa vacanāḥ nirghṛṇāḥ nirapatrapāḥ niḥsnehāḥ kopanāḥ ca eva bhartṛ putra sva bandhuṣu bandhubhiḥ bhartsitāḥ bhūtvā gṛha kāryāṇi kurvate atha vā bhartsitāḥ deva nīca vṛttāḥ sva bandhuṣu nirdayāḥ niranukrośāḥ kuṭumba artha vilopanāḥ dharma artha rahitāḥ ghorāḥ satatam kurvate kriyāḥ anartha nipuṇāḥ pāpāḥ para prāṇeṣu nirdayāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ striyaḥ paiśācyam āśritāḥ aparāḥ moha saṃyuktāḥ nirlajjāḥ rodana priyāḥ aśuddhāḥ mala digdha aṅgāḥ pāna māṃsa ratāḥ bhṛśam duṣprasādāḥ mahā krodhāḥ svapna śīlāḥ nirantaram tāmasyaḥ naṣṭa tattva arthāḥ manda śīlāḥ mahā udarāḥ bhuñjanti vividham siddham bhojanam tīvra saṃbhramāḥ hitvā anyān eva gacchanti sarvathā bhṛśa pāpikāḥ nirlajjāḥ dharma saṃdigdhāḥ pratikūlāḥ samantataḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ striyaḥ rākṣasyam āśritāḥ evaṃvidhānām sarvāsām na paratra iha vā sukham etāsām eva ku strīṇām mṛtānām ca maheśvara asaṃśayam ciram kālam narake vāsaḥ iṣyate narakāt hi vimuktānām katham cit kāla paryayāt iṣyate janma mānuṣyam kaṣṭam tatra api bhuñjate tāsām duṣkṛta saṃyogāt duḥkham janma antareṣu api daridrāḥ kleśa bhūyiṣṭhāḥ virūpāḥ kutsitāḥ paraiḥ vidhavāḥ durbhagāḥ vā api labhante duḥkham īdṛśam śata varṣa sahasra antam ātmānam vyabhicāriṇī abhisaṃyānti tāḥ lokān puṇyān parama śobhanān avamatya tu yāḥ pūrvam patim duṣṭena cetasā vartamānāḥ ca satatam bhartṝṇām pratikūla tām bhartrā anumaraṇam kāle yāḥ kurvanti tathāvidhāḥ kāmāt krodhāt bhayāt mohāt apahāsyāḥ bhavanti tāḥ ādi prabhṛti ku strīṇām tathā anumaraṇam vṛthā ādi prabhṛti yā sādhvī bhartuḥ priya parāyaṇā ūrdhvam gacchati sā tatra bhartrā anumaraṇam gatā evam mṛtāyāḥ vai lokān aham paśyāmi cakṣuṣā spṛhaṇīyān sura gaṇaiḥ yān gacchanti pativratāḥ mṛtam ca patim uddiśya kuryāt ca evaṃvidham tapaḥ evam gacchati sā nārī patīnām lokam uttamam ramaṇīyam anirdeśyam duṣprāpam deva mānuṣaiḥ prāpnuyāt tādṛśam lokam kevalam yā pativratā nāradaḥ evam vadantīm rudrāṇīm lajjā bhāva samanvitām toṣayām āsa deveśaḥ vācā saṃpūjayan priyām ṛṣayaḥ deva gandharvāḥ sa asurāḥ sa apsaraḥ gaṇāḥ daivata pramadāḥ ca api deva nadyaḥ ca saṃgatāḥ praṇamya śirasā devīm stutibhiḥ ca api tuṣṭuvuḥ pūjayām āsuḥ apare devadevam mudā yutāḥ saṃprahṛṣṭa manāḥ śarvaḥ saṃvādam cintayan muhur śṛṇu kalyāṇi mad vākyam yat idam tu tvayā mayā kṛtam saṃvādam abhavat tat trilokam gamiṣyati puṇyam pavitram ākhyānam bhavitā tat na saṃśayaḥ yaḥ idam śrāvayet vidvān saṃvādam tu āvayoḥ priye śuciḥ bhūtvā narān yuktān sa yajña phalam āpnuyāt ye tu enam śṛṇuyuḥ nityam dharmyam tad gata mānasāḥ śravaṇāt eva teṣām tu mahat dharma phalam bhavet kathayet śṛṇuyāt vā api yaḥ kaḥ cit nityam āhnikam yaḥ imam paṭhate nityam saṃvādam ca āvayoḥ śubham nāradaḥ devīm devam vayam tatra na apaśyāma prabhāvataḥ evam purā mayā āścaryam dṛṣṭam haimavate vane caratā tīrtha yātrā artham purā keśiniṣūdana bhīṣmaḥ tat adya kathitam sarvam tava keśava śṛṇvataḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ ṛṣayaḥ maheśvaraḥ māhātmyam vāsudevasya śrotum icchāma śaṃkara pitāmahāt api varaḥ śāśvataḥ puruṣaḥ hariḥ kṛṣṇaḥ jāmbūnada ābhāsaḥ vyabhre sūryaḥ iva uditaḥ śrīvatsa aṅkaḥ hṛṣīkeśaḥ sarva daivata pūjitaḥ brahmā tasya udara śayaḥ tathā aham ca śiraḥ bhavaḥ śiroruhebhyaḥ jyotīṃṣi romabhyaḥ ca sura asurāḥ ṛṣayaḥ deha saṃbhūtāḥ tasya lokāḥ ca śāśvatāḥ pitāmaha gṛham sākṣāt sarva deva gṛham ca saḥ saḥ asyāḥ pṛthivyāḥ kṛtsnāyāḥ sraṣṭā tribhuvana īśvaraḥ saṃhartā ca eva bhūtānām sthāvarasya carasya ca sa deva ripu jit sākṣāt deva nāthaḥ paraṃtapaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarva saṃśliṣṭaḥ sarva gaḥ sarvatomukhaḥ na tasmāt paramam bhūtam triṣu lokeṣu kim cana sura kārya artham utpannaḥ mānuṣam vapuḥ āsthitaḥ na hi deva gaṇāḥ śaktāḥ trivikrama vinākṛtāḥ bhuvane deva kāryāṇi kartum nāyaka varjitāḥ sarvāḥ sukha sthitāḥ ca eva śarīre tasya devatāḥ sa devaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ śrī garbhaḥ śrī saha uṣitaḥ śārṅga cakra āyudhaḥ khaḍgī sarva nāga ripu dhvajaḥ parākrameṇa vīryeṇa vapuṣā darśanena ca āroheṇa pramāṇena dhairyeṇa ārjava saṃpadā ānṛśaṃsyena rūpeṇa balena ca samanvitaḥ yoga māyaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ niravadyaḥ mahāmanāḥ kṣamāvān an ahaṃvādī brahmaṇyaḥ brahma nāyakaḥ bhaya hartā bhaya ārtānām mitrāṇām nandi vardhanaḥ śaraṇyaḥ sarva bhūtānām dīnānām pālane rataḥ śrutavān artha saṃpannaḥ sarva bhūta namaskṛtaḥ samāśritānām upakṛt śatrūṇām api dharma vid matimān nīti saṃpannaḥ brahmacārī jita indriyaḥ bhava artham iha devānām buddhyā paramayā yutaḥ prājāpatye śubhe mārge mānave dharma saṃhite aṅga nāmā manoḥ putraḥ tu antardhāmā tatas paraḥ tasya pracetaḥ pramukhāḥ bhaviṣyanti daśa ātmajāḥ dākṣāyiṇyāḥ tathā ādityaḥ manuḥ ādityataḥ tathā manoḥ ca vaṃśa jaḥ iḍā sudyumnaḥ ca bhaviṣyati budhāt purūravāḥ ca api tasmāt āyuḥ bhaviṣyati nahuṣaḥ bhavitā tasmāt yayātiḥ tasya ca ātmajaḥ yaduḥ tasmāt mahā sattvaḥ kroṣṭā tasmāt bhaviṣyati kroṣṭuḥ ca eva mahān putraḥ vṛjinīvān bhaviṣyati vṛjinīvataḥ hi bhavitā uṣadguḥ aparājitaḥ uṣadgoḥ bhavitā putraḥ śūraḥ citrarathaḥ tathā tasya tu avarajaḥ putraḥ śūraḥ nāma bhaviṣyati teṣām vikhyāta vīryāṇām cāritra guṇa śālinām yajvinām su viśuddhānām vaṃśe brāhmaṇa saṃmate sa śūraḥ kṣatriya śreṣṭhaḥ mahā vīryaḥ mahā yaśāḥ sva vaṃśa vistara karam janayiṣyati mānadaḥ rājñaḥ sarvān ca ruddhān sa mokṣayiṣyati mānadaḥ jarāsaṃdham ca rājānam nirjitya giri gahvare śūraseneṣu saṃbhūtaḥ dvārakāyām vasan prabhuḥ pālayiṣyati gām devīm vijitya naya vat sadā tam bhavantaḥ samāsādya vāc mālyaiḥ arhaṇaiḥ varaiḥ arcayantu yathānyāyam brahmāṇam iva śāśvatam yaḥ hi mām draṣṭum iccheta brahmāṇam vā pitāmaham draṣṭavyaḥ tena bhagavān vāsudevaḥ pratāpavān dṛṣṭe tasmin aham dṛṣṭaḥ na me atra asti vicāraṇā sa yasya puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ prīti yuktaḥ bhaviṣyati yaḥ ca tam mānavaḥ loke saṃśrayiṣyati keśavam dharmāṇām deśikaḥ sākṣāt sa bhaviṣyati dharma bhāj sa hi devaḥ mahā tejāḥ prajā hita cikīrṣayā dharma artham puruṣa vyāghraḥ ṛṣi koṭīḥ sasarja ha tāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ tena vibhunā parvate gandhamādane sanatkumāra pramukhāḥ tiṣṭhanti tapasā anvitāḥ tasmāt sa vāgmī dharma jñaḥ namasyaḥ dvija puṃgavāḥ arcitaḥ ca arcayet nityam pūjitaḥ pratipūjayet dṛṣṭaḥ paśyet ahar ahar saṃśritaḥ pratisaṃśrayet karmaṇā manasā vācā namasyaḥ sa dvijaiḥ sadā tam dṛṣṭvā sarvaśas devāḥ dṛṣṭāḥ syuḥ muni sattamāḥ mahāvarāham tam devam sarva loka pitāmaham tatra vaḥ tritayam dṛṣṭam bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ śiraḥ nāgaiḥ mahā bhogaiḥ parikīrṇam mahātmanaḥ cintitāni sameṣyanti śastrāṇi astrāṇi ca eva ha suparṇaḥ yasya vīryeṇa kaśyapasya ātmajaḥ balī antam na eva aśakat draṣṭum devasya paramātmanaḥ saḥ eṣa viṣṇuḥ saḥ anantaḥ bhagavān vasudhādharaḥ yaḥ rāmaḥ sa hṛṣīkeśaḥ yaḥ acyutaḥ sa dharādharaḥ draṣṭavyau mānanīyau ca cakra lāṅgala dhāriṇau eṣa yaḥ anugrahaḥ proktaḥ mayā puṇyaḥ tapodhanāḥ nāradaḥ yam bhavantaḥ dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ pūjayeyuḥ prayatnataḥ prāvṛṣi iva ca parjanyaḥ vavṛṣe nirmalam payaḥ nabhaḥ ca eva abhavat ghoram diśaḥ ca na cakāśire na śarvam bhūta saṃgham vā dadṛśuḥ munayaḥ tadā tīrtha yātrām tatas viprāḥ jagmuḥ ca anye yathāgatam śaṃkarasya umayā sārdham saṃvādam tad kathā āśrayam bhagavān puruṣa vyāghraḥ brahma bhūtaḥ sanātanaḥ yad artham anuśiṣṭāḥ smaḥ giri pṛṣṭhe mahātmanā dṛṣṭvā ati vismitāḥ kṛṣṇa sā ca naḥ smṛtiḥ āgatā iti uktaḥ sa tadā kṛṣṇaḥ tapaḥ vana nivāsibhiḥ mānayām āsa tān sarvān ṛṣīn devaki nandanaḥ atha ṛṣayaḥ saṃprahṛṣṭāḥ punar te kṛṣṇam abruvan punar saṃdarśayasva asmān sadā eva madhusūdana tādṛśam ca mahā bāho yathā āha bhagavān bhavaḥ tvam eva hi artha tattva jñaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ asmān pṛcchase sadā vayam tu baṭu cāpalyāt aśaktāḥ guhya dhāraṇe sādhayāma vayam deva vṛddhim puṣṭim avāpnuhi putraḥ te sadṛśaḥ tāta viśiṣṭaḥ vā bhaviṣyati bhīṣmaḥ tatas praṇamya deveśam yādavam puruṣottamam pradakṣiṇam upāvṛtya prajagmuḥ te mahā ṛṣayaḥ saḥ ayam nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān dīptyā paramayā yutaḥ vratam yathāvat tat cīrtvā dvārakām punar āgamat pūrṇe ca daśame māsi putraḥ asya parama adbhutaḥ rukmiṇyām saṃmataḥ jajñe śūraḥ vaṃśa dharaḥ prabho sa kāmaḥ sarva jantūnām sarva bhāgavataḥ nṛpa surāṇām asurāṇām ca carati antargataḥ sadā saḥ ayam puruṣa śārdūlaḥ megha varṇaḥ catur bhujaḥ saṃśritaḥ pāṇḍavān premṇā bhavantaḥ ca enam āśritāḥ sa indrāḥ devāḥ trayastriṃśat sthitāḥ na atra vicāraṇā ādidevaḥ mahādevaḥ bhūtānām ca pratiśrayaḥ svayaṃjātaḥ mahā tejāḥ surāṇām artha siddhaye tava iyam pṛthivī kṛtsnā nārāyaṇa samāśrayāt ayam nāthaḥ tava acintyaḥ yasya nārāyaṇaḥ hṛdi sa bhavān bhūmipa adhvaryuḥ raṇa agnau hutavān nṛpān kṛṣṇaḥ sruveṇa mahatā yuga anta agni samena vai kṛtavān yaḥ abudhaḥ kopāt hari gāṇḍīva vigraham daiteyāḥ dānava indrāḥ ca mahā kāyāḥ mahā balāḥ cakra agnau kṣayam āpannāḥ dāva agnau śalabhāḥ iva pratiyoddhum na śakyaḥ hi mānuṣaiḥ eṣa saṃyuge nihīnaiḥ puruṣa vyāghra sattva śakti bala ādibhiḥ jayaḥ yaḥ ayam yuga anta ābhaḥ savyasācī raṇa agra gaḥ yat tu vai vṛṣabhāṅkena munibhyaḥ samudāhṛtam purāṇam himavat pṛṣṭhe tat me nigadataḥ śṛṇu yāvat asya udbhavaḥ tuṣṭiḥ tejaḥ vīryam parākramaḥ yatra kṛṣṇaḥ hi bhagavān tatra tuṣṭiḥ anuttamā vayam hi bāla matayaḥ para netrāḥ su viklavāḥ jñāna pūrvam prapannāḥ smaḥ mṛtyoḥ panthānam uttamam bhavān ca api ārjava paraḥ sarvam kṛtvā pratiśrayam rāja vṛttāt na calate pratijñā pālane rataḥ na hi pratijñayā dattam tam prahātum ariṃdama kālena ayam janaḥ sarvaḥ nihataḥ raṇa mūrdhani vayam ca kālena hatāḥ kālaḥ hi parameśvaraḥ na hi kālena kāla jña spṛṣṭaḥ śocitum arhati tasmāt kuntī suta jñātīn na iha śocitum arhasi mādhavasya ca māhātmyam śrutam yat kathitam mayā vyāsasya vacanam śrutvā nāradasya ca dhīmataḥ svayam ca eva mahā rāja kṛṣṇasya arhattamasya vai prabhāvaḥ ca ṛṣi pūgasya kathitaḥ su mahān mayā maheśvarasya saṃvādaḥ śailaputryāḥ ca bhārata dhārayiṣyati yaḥ ca imam mahā puruṣa saṃbhavam śṛṇuyāt kathayet vā yaḥ sa vai śreyaḥ labhet param bhavitāraḥ ca tasya atha sarve kāmāḥ yathā īpsitāḥ pretya māheśvaram lokam labhate na atra saṃśayaḥ na ayam śreyaḥ abhikāmena pratipattum janārdane eṣa vai ca akṣayaḥ vipraiḥ smṛtaḥ rājan janārdanaḥ maheśvara mukha utsṛṣṭāḥ ye ca dharma guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ te tvayā manasā dhāryāḥ kuru rāja divāniśam dharmeṇa hi sadā rājā prajāḥ rakṣitum arhati śaṃkarasya umayā sārdham saṃvādaḥ dharma saṃhitaḥ śrutvā vā śrotu kāmaḥ vā api arcayet vṛṣabhadhvajam saṃdeśaḥ deva pūjā artham tam tathā kuru pāṇḍava daśa varṣa sahasrāṇi badaryām eṣa sātvataḥ tapaḥ cacāra vipulam saha gāṇḍīvadhanvanā tri yugau puṇḍarīka akṣau vāsudeva dhanaṃjayau bālaḥ eva mahā bāhuḥ cakāra kadanam mahat kaṃsasya puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ jñāti trāṇa artha kāraṇāt śāśvatasya purāṇasya puruṣasya yudhiṣṭhira yasya te puruṣa vyāghraḥ sahāyaḥ ayam janārdanaḥ yad kṛte pṛthivī sarvā vinaṣṭā sa haya dvipā vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ duḥśāsana caturthānām kuravaḥ nidhanam gatāḥ evam prabhāṣamāṇe tu gāṅgeye puruṣa ṛṣabhe tūṣṇīm babhūva kauravyaḥ madhye teṣām mahātmanām tat śrutvā vismayam jagmuḥ dhṛtarāṣṭra ādayaḥ nṛpāḥ saṃpūjya manasā kṛṣṇam sarve prāñjalayaḥ abhavan ṛṣayaḥ ca api te sarve nārada pramukhāḥ tathā iti etat akhilam sarvam pāṇḍavaḥ bhrātṛbhiḥ saha śrutavān su mahat citram puṇyam bhīṣma anuśāsanam yudhiṣṭhiraḥ tu gāṅgeyam viśrāntam bhūri dakṣiṇam yudhiṣṭhiraḥ kaḥ upāyaḥ vara prāptau sarveṣām pāpa karmaṇām bhīṣmaḥ upāyaḥ ayam vara prāptau paramaḥ parikīrtitaḥ nārāyaṇasya tu dhyānam arcanam yajanam stutiḥ śravaṇam tad kathānām ca vidvat saṃrakṣaṇam tathā vidvat śuśrūṣaṇa prītiḥ upadeśa anupālanam saṃdhyā anena japena āśu mucyate prākṛtaḥ api ca dvija śuśrūṣayā jñānam vidvas saṃrakṣaṇena vā na asādhyam jñāninām kim cit tasmāt rakṣyāḥ dvijāḥ tvayā āsīt āśvāsitā vidvas saṃrakṣaṇa phalāt kila vaiśyā tvam abhilāṣā iyam vipra kanyā iti sāṃpratam iti uktā āśvāsitā apṛcchat kena evam pāpa saṃyutā yamaḥ jātāḥ vipra kule samyak śreyaḥ ca api bravīhi me anya janmani vidvāṃsam prahāraiḥ abhipīḍitam cora śaṅkā vimokṣeṇa mokṣayitvā sujanmikā iti ukta aṣṭa akṣara dhyāna japa ādi śreyasā tu sā yamena anugṛhītā abhūt puṇya loka nivāsinī tat nityam viduṣām rakṣā tatparaḥ bhava bhūpate teṣām saṃrakṣaṇāt tuṣṭaḥ sarva pāpaiḥ pramucyate viṣṇoḥ nāma sahasrasya vedavyāsaḥ mahān ṛṣiḥ chandaḥ anuṣṭubh tathā devaḥ bhagavān viśva rūpa dhṛk ṛṣiḥ nāmnām sahasrasya vedavyāsaḥ mahā muniḥ chandaḥ anuṣṭubh tathā devaḥ bhagavān devakī sutaḥ tri sāmā hṛdayam tasya śānti arthe viniyujyate viṣṇum jiṣṇum mahāviṣṇum prabhaviṣṇum maheśvaram yudhiṣṭhiraḥ pitāmaha mahā prājña sarva śāstra viśārada prasthāne vā praveśe vā pravṛtte vā api karmaṇi daive vā śrāddha kāle vā kim japyam karma sādhanam śāntikam pauṣṭikam rakṣā śatru ghnam bhaya nāśanam bhīṣmaḥ vyāsa proktam imam mantram śṛṇuṣva ekamanāḥ nṛpa sāvitryā vihitam divyam sadyas pāpa vimocanam śṛṇu mantra vidhim kṛtsnam procyamānam mayā anagha yam śrutvā pāṇḍava śreṣṭha sarva pāpaiḥ pramucyate tat te aham saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva ekamanāḥ nṛpa āyuṣmān bhavate ca eva yam śrutvā pārthiva ātmaja sevitam satatam rājan purā rāja ṛṣi sattamaiḥ nṛpaiḥ bharata śārdūla prāpyate śrīḥ anuttamā namaḥ vasiṣṭhāya mahāvratāya parāśaram veda nidhim praṇamya namaḥ astu ṛṣibhyaḥ paramam pareṣām deveṣu devam vara dam varāṇām sahasra śīrṣāya namaḥ śivāya sahasra nāmāya janārdanāya ajaikapād ahirbudhnyaḥ pinākī ca aparājitaḥ ṛtaḥ ca pitṛrūpaḥ ca tryambakaḥ ca maheśvaraḥ vṛṣākapiḥ ca śaṃbhuḥ ca havanaḥ atha īśvaraḥ tathā ekādaśā ete prathitāḥ rudrāḥ tribhuvana īśvarāḥ śatam etat samākhyātam śatarudre mahātmanām aṃśaḥ bhagaḥ ca mitraḥ ca varuṇaḥ ca jaleśvaraḥ tvaṣṭā pūṣā tathā evā indraḥ dvādaśaḥ viṣṇuḥ ucyate iti ete dvādaśa ādityāḥ kāśyapeyāḥ iti śrutiḥ pratyūṣaḥ ca prabhāsaḥ ca vasavaḥ aṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ nāsatyaḥ ca api dasraḥ ca smṛtau dvau aśvinau api mārtaṇḍasya ātmajau etau saṃjñā nāsā vinirgatau atas param pravakṣyāmi lokānām karma sākṣiṇaḥ adṛśyāḥ sarva bhūteṣu paśyanti tridaśa īśvarāḥ śubha aśubhāni karmāṇi mṛtyuḥ kālaḥ ca sarvaśas viśvedevāḥ pitṛ gaṇāḥ mūrtimantaḥ tapodhanāḥ munayaḥ ca eva siddhāḥ ca tapaḥ mokṣa parāyaṇāḥ śuci smitāḥ kīrtayatām prayacchanti śubham nṛṇām prajāpati kṛtān etān lokān divyena tejasā prāṇānām īśvarān etān kīrtayan prayataḥ naraḥ lokān ca labhate puṇyān viśveśvara kṛtān śubhān ete devāḥ trayastriṃśat sarva bhūta gaṇa īśvarāḥ īśvarāḥ sarva lokānām gaṇeśvara vināyakāḥ saumyāḥ raudra gaṇāḥ ca eva yoga bhūta gaṇāḥ tathā jyotīṃṣi saritaḥ vyoma suparṇaḥ pataga īśvaraḥ pṛthivyām tapasā siddhāḥ sthāvarāḥ ca carāḥ ca ha himavān girayaḥ ca eva catvāraḥ ca mahā arṇavāḥ bhavasya anucarāḥ sarve hara tulya parākramāḥ viṣṇuḥ devaḥ atha jiṣṇuḥ ca skandaḥ ca ambikayā saha atas ūrdhvam pravakṣyāmi mānavān ṛṣi sattamān yavakrītaḥ ca raibhyaḥ ca arvāvasu parāvasū auśijaḥ ca eva kakṣīvān balaḥ ca āṅgirasaḥ sutaḥ ṛṣiḥ medhātitheḥ putraḥ kaṇvaḥ barhiṣadaḥ tathā brahma tejaḥ mayāḥ sarve kīrtitāḥ loka bhāvanāḥ mahendra guravaḥ sapta prācīm vai diśam āśritāḥ prayataḥ kīrtayan etān śakra loke mahīyate dṛḍhavyaḥ ca ūrdhvabāhuḥ ca tṛṇasoma aṅgirāḥ tathā mitrāvaruṇayoḥ putraḥ tathā agastyaḥ pratāpavān dharmarāja ṛtvijaḥ sapta dakṣiṇām diśam āśritāḥ dṛḍheyuḥ ca ṛteyuḥ ca parivyādhaḥ ca kīrtimān ekataḥ ca dvitaḥ ca eva tritaḥ ca āditya saṃnibhāḥ atreḥ putraḥ ca dharma ātmā ṛṣiḥ sārasvataḥ tathā varuṇasya ṛtvijaḥ sapta paścimām diśam āśritāḥ atriḥ vasiṣṭhaḥ bhagavān kaśyapaḥ ca mahān ṛṣiḥ ṛcīka tanayaḥ ca ugraḥ jamadagniḥ pratāpavān dhaneśvarasya guravaḥ sapta ete uttarām śritāḥ apare munayaḥ sapta dikṣu sarvāsu dhiṣṭhitāḥ kīrti svasti karāḥ nṝṇām kīrtitāḥ loka bhāvanāḥ dharmaḥ kāmaḥ ca kālaḥ ca vasuḥ vāsukiḥ eva ca anantaḥ kapilaḥ ca eva saptā ete dharaṇīdharāḥ rāmaḥ vyāsaḥ tathā drauṇiḥ aśvatthāmā ca lomaśaḥ śānti svasti karāḥ loke diśām pālāḥ prakīrtitāḥ yasyām yasyām diśi hi ete tad mukhaḥ śaraṇam vrajet sraṣṭāraḥ sarva bhūtānām kīrtitāḥ loka pāvanāḥ saṃvartaḥ merusāvarṇaḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ ca dhārmikaḥ sāṃkhya yogau nāradaḥ ca durvāsāḥ ca mahān ṛṣiḥ atyanta tapasaḥ dāntāḥ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutāḥ apare rudra saṃkāśāḥ kīrtitāḥ brahma laukikāḥ pṛthum vainyam nṛpa varam pṛthvī yasya abhavat sutā gavāmayena yajñena yena iṣṭam vai kṛte yuge viśva jit tapasā upetam lakṣaṇyam kāma lakṣaṇam tathā śvetam ca rājarṣim kīrtayet parama dyutim mahādeva prasādena yena gaṅgā avatāritā sagarasya ātmajāḥ yena plāvitāḥ tāritāḥ tathā hutāśana samān etān mahā rūpān mahā ojasaḥ devān ṛṣi gaṇān ca eva nṛpān ca jagatī īśvarān sāṃkhyam yogam ca paramam havyam kavyam tathā eva ca kīrtitam paramam brahma sarva śruti parāyaṇam maṅgalyam sarva bhūtānām pavitram bahu kīrtitam vyādhi praśamanam śreṣṭham pauṣṭikam sarva karmaṇām prayataḥ kīrtayet ca etān kalyam sāyam ca bhārata ete vināyakāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ dakṣāḥ kṣāntāḥ jita indriyāḥ narāṇām aśubham sarve vyapohanti prakīrtitāḥ sākṣi bhūtāḥ mahātmānaḥ pāpasya sukṛtasya ca etān vai kalyam utthāya kīrtayan śubham aśnute etān kīrtayatām nityam duḥsvapnaḥ naśyate nṛṇām mucyate sarva pāpebhyaḥ svastimān ca gṛhān vrajet dīkṣā kāleṣu sarveṣu yaḥ paṭhet niyataḥ dvijaḥ nyāyavān ātma nirataḥ kṣāntaḥ dāntaḥ anasūyakaḥ roga ārtaḥ vā abhiyuktaḥ vā paṭhan pāpāt pramucyate gacchataḥ kṣemam adhvānam grāma antara gataḥ paṭhan ātmanaḥ ca sutānām ca dārāṇām ca dhanasya ca bījānām oṣadhīnām ca rakṣām etām prayojayet etān saṃgrāma kāle tu paṭhataḥ kṣatriyasya tu kaśmalam laghu tām yāti pāpmanā ca pramucyate yānapātre ca yāne ca pravāse rāja veśmani parām siddhim avāpnoti sāvitrīm hi uttamām paṭhan na ca rāja bhayam teṣām na piśācāt na rākṣasāt caturṇām api varṇānām āśramasya viśeṣataḥ karoti satatam śāntim sāvitrīm uttamām paṭhan na agniḥ dahati kāṣṭhāni sāvitrī yatra paṭhyate na teṣām vidyate duḥkham gacchanti paramām gatim ye śṛṇvanti mahat brahma sāvitrī guṇa kīrtanam gavām madhye tu paṭhataḥ gāvaḥ asya bahu vatsalāḥ prasthāne vā praveśe vā sarva avasthā gataḥ paṭhet japatām juhvatām ca eva nityam ca prayata ātmanām ṛṣīṇām paramam japyam guhyam etat narādhipa yāthātathyena saṃgamya itihāsam purātanam parāśara matam divyam śakrāya kathitam purā hṛdayam sarva bhūtānām śrutiḥ eṣā purātanī soma āditya anvayāḥ sarve rāghavāḥ kuravaḥ tathā paṭhanti śucayaḥ nityam sāvitrīm prāṇinām gatim abhyāśe nityam devānām saptarṣīṇām dhruvasya ca mokṣaṇam sarva kṛcchrāṇām mocayati aśubhāt sadā vṛddhaiḥ kāśyapa gautama prabhṛtibhiḥ bhṛgu aṅgiraḥ tri ādibhiḥ bhāradvāja matam ṛcīka tanayaiḥ prāptam vasiṣṭhāt punar sāvitrīm adhigamya śakra vasubhiḥ kṛtsnāḥ jitāḥ dānavāḥ yaḥ go śatam kanaka śṛṅga mayam dadāti divyām ca bhārata kathām kathayet ca nityam eṣā te kathitā rājan sāvitrī brahma śāśvatī yaḥ paṭhet śṛṇuyāt vā api śreyaḥ prāpnoti anuttamam śrī bhagavān uvāca bhaktānām anuraktānām trātā bhava janārdana yaḥ mām nāma sahasreṇa stotum icchati pāṇḍava sahasra pāda akṣi śira ūru bāhave sahasranāmne puruṣāya śāśvate sahasra koṭī yuga dhāriṇe namaḥ yena tvayā bhārata taila pūrṇaḥ prajvālitaḥ jñāna mayaḥ pradīpaḥ eṣa niṣkaṇṭakaḥ panthāḥ yatra saṃpūjyate hariḥ kupatham tam vijānīyāt govinda rahita āgamam śrī sahasranāma tad tulyam rāma nāma varānane iti stutvā hṛṣīkeśam pārthaḥ tūṣṇīm sthitaḥ tatas jagat hitāya kṛṣṇāya govindāya namaḥ namaḥ ākāśāt patitam toyam yathā gacchati sāgaram jitam te puṇḍarīkākṣa namaḥ te viśvabhāvana namaḥ te astu hṛṣīkeśa mahāpuruṣa pūrvaja mahātmanām jñāna vidām tapaḥ eva dhanam param teṣām vṛddhāḥ ca bālāḥ ca sarve sat mārga darśinaḥ utathya arthe tu cāru aṅgī param niyamam āsthitā kasmāt tvam hatavān asi aspṛśyaḥ apāna yogyaḥ ca kṣipram eva bhaviṣyati muktaḥ tvam āyuḥ śeṣeṇa mā bhaiḥ gaccha jaleśvara mā avamaṃsthāḥ punar mohāt mā tvām ninyuḥ yama kṣayam vaikhānasa vidhānena gaṅgā tīram samāśritāḥ cyavanasya mahā rāja śṛṇu māhātmyam uttamam iṣṭvā yajñam mahābhāga cyavanaḥ su mahā tapāḥ kriyatām mad vacaḥ devāḥ yathā vai samudāhṛtam aśvibhyām saha somam vai na pāsyāmi dvijottama garbhiṇī vatsaka ādiṣu pūrvāhṇeṣu na dīvyanti hatvā vai dānavān devāḥ sarve saṃbhūya saṃyuge te na abhyajānan hi tadā brāhmaṇaiḥ nihatān kapān yathā hatā mahābhāgaiḥ tejasā brāhmaṇaiḥ kapāḥ nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā prītāḥ sarve divaukasaḥ kṛṣṇasya dehāt medinī saṃbabhūva kṛtsnam etat sthāvaram jaṃgamam ca sa viśvasṛj pārtha sa viśva rūpaḥ tam ghoṣa arthe gīrbhiḥ indrāḥ stuvanti sa ca api īśaḥ bhārata ekaḥ paśūnām hiraṇmayaḥ sapta gūḍhaḥ sa saṃvid caturbāhuḥ padmagaḥ padmanābhaḥ tam ṛk sahasraiḥ puruṣam purāṇam ṛtūn māsān vividham kārya jātam vidyut saṃghaiḥ āpatantaḥ ca meghāḥ sṛṣṭvā viśvam viśvayoniḥ mahātmā tatra atiṣṭhat viśvarūpaḥ hi yogāt tādṛśaḥ keśavaḥ devaḥ bhūyas nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ ādiḥ antaḥ ca madhyam ca deśataḥ kālataḥ hariḥ jagatām tasthuṣām ca eva bhūtānām prabhava apyayaḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ pitāmaha vacaḥ śrutvā rājan te prapitāmahaḥ praṇamya puṇḍarīkākṣam idam vacanam abravīt dvāravatyām samāsīnam purā mām kuru nandana trivarge ca apavarge ca yaśaḥ śrī roga śāntiṣu devatā pitṛ pūjāsu saṃtoṣyāḥ eva naḥ dvijāḥ roṣaṇam sarva bhūtānām sūkṣme api apakṛte kṛte śivaḥ sarva gataḥ rudraḥ sraṣṭā yaḥ tam śṛṇuṣva me tad kṣaṇāt eva kuryāt sa bhasma vahni śikhā yathā yudhiṣṭhiraḥ tripurāṇi mahā bāho jitavān sa maheśvaraḥ vāsudevaḥ ācakṣva me yathātattvam sarvajña madhusūdana śṛṇu me gadataḥ tāta yathā dagdham puratrayam rudram tam raudra karmāṇi sarva astra kuśalam prabhum bhakta anugrahaṇa arthāya gūḍhaliṅgaḥ tatas smṛtaḥ prahṛṣṭaḥ ca eva śaṃkaraḥ mūla ācāraḥ tu sādhūnām yajña svādhyāya vṛttataḥ satya ārjava parāyaṇāḥ eteṣām dharma śīlānām sāmānyam bhojanam kuryāt svayam na agrya aśanam vrajet muni śāpāt dvijaḥ kaḥ cit vyāghra tām samupāgataḥ bahulām bhakṣaṇa rucim āsvādya śapathena tu vimucya pīta vatsām tām dṛṣṭvā smṛtvā purātanam jagāma lokān amalān sā sva rāṣṭram tathā punar tasmāt satya ārjava rataḥ rājā rāṣṭram samānavam dāna āgāre gavām ca eva brāhmaṇānām ca saṃnidhau yudhiṣṭhiraḥ mānasāni iha tīrthāni praśaṃsanti mahā ṛṣayaḥ bhīṣmaḥ śṛṇu tīrthāni gadataḥ mānasāni iha mānada yeṣu samyak naraḥ snātvā prayāti paramām gatim satyam tīrtham kṣamā tīrtham satyam indriya nigrahaḥ sarva bhūta dayā tīrtham tīrtham ārjavam eva ca dānam tīrtham damaḥ tīrtham saṃtoṣaḥ tīrtham uttamam brahmacaryam param tīrtham tīrtham ca priya vādi tā jñānam tīrtham tapaḥ tīrtham dhṛtiḥ tīrtham udāhṛtam tīrthānām api yat tīrtham viśuddhiḥ manasaḥ parā yaḥ lubdhaḥ piśunaḥ krūraḥ dāmbhikaḥ viṣaya ātmakaḥ sarva tīrtheṣu api snātaḥ pāpaḥ malinaḥ eva saḥ cittam antargatam duṣṭam tīrtha snānaiḥ na śudhyati śataśas api jale dhautam surā bhāṇḍam iva aśuci tatra tasya kurukṣetram naimiṣam puṣkaram gayā yudhiṣṭhiraḥ yaḥ snātaḥ mānase tīrthe sa yāti paramām gatim pāpasya kim adhiṣṭhānam kā prasūtiḥ ca bhārata bhīṣmaḥ kim ca asya kāraṇam proktam tat me brūhi pitāmaha lobhaḥ pratiṣṭhā pāpasya prasūtiḥ lobhaḥ eva ca atra te saṃśayaḥ mā bhūt lobhaḥ pāpasya kāraṇam lobhāt mohaḥ ca māyā ca mānāt mātsaryam eva ca nityam gambhīra toyābhiḥ āpagābhiḥ iva arṇavaḥ sarva bhūteṣu aviśvāsaḥ sarva bhūteṣu anirdayaḥ su mahānti api śāstrāṇi dhārayantaḥ bahuśrutāḥ chettāraḥ saṃśayānām ca lobha grastāḥ pratyānti adhas lobha kṣaye kṣayam yānti sarva pāpāni dehinām yadi icchasi mahā rāja śāśvatīm gatim ātmanaḥ ayatnena edhamānāḥ ca dṛśyante bahavaḥ janāḥ bhāva śuddhiḥ tu tapasā devatānām ca pūjayā yudhiṣṭhiraḥ pitāmaha mahā bāho sarva dharma bhṛtām vara bhīṣmaḥ nānā janapada ākīrṇe yajña vāṭa vibhūṣite tatra sā nagarī ramyā yamunā tīram āśritā indra yaṣṭi dhvaja ākīrṇā gopībhiḥ upaśobhitā bahu dvija samākīrṇā nānā paṇya upaśobhitā su pramāṇā su ramyā ca svāti nakṣatra nirmitā prākāra aṭṭa pratolībhiḥ durjayā parikhādibhiḥ āmrātaka kapitthaiḥ ca rājavṛkṣaiḥ ca śobhitā devatāyatanaiḥ divyaiḥ kadalī khaṇḍa maṇḍitā haṃsa mālā pratīkāśaiḥ dīpyate dhavalaiḥ gṛhaiḥ karavīraiḥ karṇikāraiḥ pāṭalā aśoka campakaiḥ mallikā kunda jātībhiḥ kubja āhlāda kuraṇṭakaiḥ suvarṇa śveta yūthībhiḥ kiṃkirāta utpala ādibhiḥ puṃnāga vṛkṣa bakulaiḥ udyānaiḥ upaśobhitā saṃpūrṇā dhana dhānyaiḥ ca go dhanaiḥ upaśobhitā veda adhyayana ghoṣaiḥ ca pavitrīkṛta maṅgalā vedī śṛṅgāṭakaiḥ ramyaiḥ trika catvara śobhitā rāvaṇasya iva ratna āḍhyā laṅkā ca eva mahā purī kṣatra dharme rataḥ śrīmān prajāpati samaḥ kṣitau tena sā nagarī ramyā rāja siṃhena pālitā nitya utsava pramuditā śaṅkha vāditra nāditā su saṃgīta vidagdhaiḥ ca prekṣaṇīyaiḥ samākulā antarāpaṇa vīthībhiḥ suvibhaktaiḥ catuṣpathaiḥ dīrghikā kūpa vāpībhiḥ taḍāgaiḥ upaśobhitā sabhā prapā samākīrṇā vivāha makha saṃkulā purī candravatī nāma dṛṣṭā te yadi vā śrutā tasyām puryām purā vṛttam tat tvam śṛṇu yudhiṣṭhira hṛṣṭa puṣṭā su saṃtuṣṭā bahulā nāma viśrutā go dhanasya ca sā mukhyā haṃsa varṇā ghaṭa sravā dīrgha ghoṇā vibhakta aṅgī śreṣṭha loma tanu tvacā vistīrṇa jaghanā divyā pīna śroṇi payodharā sarva lakṣaṇa saṃpannā sarva avayava sundarī sāndram su puṣpitam surabhi acchinnam carate tṛṇam mṛga siṃha samākīrṇe bahu śvāpada sevite vallī vṛkṣa ādi gahane śivā śata ninādite dvīpī śoṇita māṃsa āśī mahā daṃṣṭraḥ mahā balaḥ tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭraḥ nakha āyudhaḥ tasya yūtha paribhraṣṭā bahulā tṛṇa tṛṣṇayā abhidravan ca tām vyāghraḥ tiṣṭha tiṣṭha iti ca abravīt vyāghrasya vacanam śrutvā niṣṭhuram loma harṣaṇam sutam rūpa anvitam bālam candra rūpa sama prabham rudantī karuṇam sā tu nirāśā putra darśane dṛṣṭvā tu bahulām vyāghraḥ krandamānām su duḥkhitām na rudantyāḥ vā hasantyāḥ vā tathā te jīvitam bhavet kautukam me adya saṃjātam aśeṣam kathayasva naḥ vyāghrasya vacanam śrutvā bahulā vākyam abravīt tvām samālokya lokasya paritrāṇam na vidyate jīvita artham na śocāmi prāptavyam maraṇam mayā tasmāt tat na aham eva ekam vyāghra śocāmi jīvitam kim tu sneha vaśāt vyāghra duḥkhena ruditam mayā asti me hṛdi saṃtāpaḥ tam ca tvam śrotum arhasi agre vayasi vartantī prasūtā aham mṛgādhipa iṣṭaḥ prathama jātaḥ tu sutaḥ ca mama bālakaḥ kṣīram pibati me vatsaḥ tṛṇam na adya api jighrati sutam tam anuśocāmi katham jīviṣyate sutaḥ tasya icchāmi stanam dātum putra sneha vaśa anugā sakhīnām arpayitvā ca saṃdiśya ca hita ahitam punar pratyāgamiṣyāmi yatheṣṭam bhakṣayiṣyasi śīghrā aham āgamiṣyāmi mahā vyāghra tava antikam bahulāyāḥ vacaḥ śrutvā mṛgendraḥ punar abravīt kim te putreṇa kartavyam maraṇam kim na paśyasi tvam punar kṛpayā āviṣṭā putra putra iti bhāṣase na mantrāḥ na tapaḥ dānam na mātā na pitā sutaḥ śaknuvanti paritrātum āgatām mad samīpataḥ vṛṣabhaiḥ nāditam ramyam bāla vatsa vibhūṣitam bhūṣaṇam deva lokasya svarga tulyam na saṃśayaḥ pavitram tat pavitrāṇām maṅgalānām ca maṅgalam yat tīrtham sarva tīrthānām ramyāṇām ramyam uttamam samasta guṇa saṃkīrṇam īśvara āyatanam mahat yatra vatsāḥ ca huṃkāram karuṇam mātṛ kāṅkṣayā yat gopaiḥ pālitam śūraiḥ bāhu yuddha viśāradaiḥ pragīta nṛtya saṃlāpam valgu tā āsphoṭa nāditam saraḥ vat bhrājate goṣṭham śrīmadbhiḥ iva paṅkajaiḥ tat śrīniketanam ramyam mātaram bhrātaram sutam dṛṣṭvā sakhī janam bhūyas katham pratyāgamiṣyasi bahulā na nirāśāni bhūtāni vāc mātreṇa karomi aham prathamam dṛṣṭavat sā aham mṛgendra śṛṇu me vacaḥ dṛṣṭvā sakhī janam bālam gopān ca paricārakān gopī janam samāgamya jananīm ca viśeṣataḥ vyāghraḥ śapathaiḥ āgamiṣyāmi manyase yadi muñca mām śapathāḥ kīdṛśāḥ bhadre ye tvayā parinoditāḥ bahulā śapathaiḥ āgamiṣyāmi satyam brūyām na saṃśayaḥ mā dadasva iti yaḥ brūyāt guru agni brāhmaṇeṣu ca madhu māṃse ca yat pāpam lākṣā vikrayaṇe tathā a saṃtoṣī ca yaḥ vipraḥ nityam bhavati yācakaḥ matsarī gotra bhāvena bhūtānām guṇa nāśakaḥ kaumāram ca api bhartāram yā nārī na anumanyate ye saktāḥ guru dāreṣu svāmi dāreṣu ye narāḥ brāhmaṇaḥ vṛṣalīm gatvā na tu tīrthāni sevate brāhmaṇaḥ kṛta saṃskāraḥ śūdra prekṣaṇa kārakaḥ anṛtam dhana saṃbaddhe kūṭasākṣyam vadanti ye yat pāpam brahmahatyāyām mātā pitṛ vadhena ca yat pāpam lubdhakānām ca mlecchānām gara dāyinām pādena spṛśate gāḥ ca śirobhiḥ na namanti ye goṣu avajñām prakurvanti śapante tāḍayanti ca sakṛt dattvā tu yaḥ kanyām dvitīyāya prayacchati kathāyām kriyamāṇāyām antarāyam karoti yaḥ dvi bhāryaḥ puruṣaḥ yaḥ tu ekām snehena paśyati ekākī mṛṣṭam aśnāti yaḥ kaḥ cit puruṣa adhamaḥ yaḥ trīn hale balīvardān viṣamam vāhayeta yaḥ tena pāpena lipye aham yadi na āyām tava antikam iti etaiḥ pātakaiḥ ghoraiḥ āgamiṣyāmi aham punar loke asmin nāstikāḥ ke cid mūrkhāḥ paṇḍita māninaḥ bhrāmayanti ca te buddhim cakra ārūḍhaḥ iva īkṣaṇam atathyāni api tathyāni darśayanti api peśalāḥ prāyas kṛtārthaḥ lokaḥ ayam manyate na upakāriṇam vatsaḥ kṣīra kṣayam dṛṣṭvā parityajati mātaram ṛṣi deva asura gaṇaiḥ śapathāḥ kārya siddhaye kṛtāḥ parasparam pūrvam tat na manyāmahe katham tena evam darśitam pūrvam yathā iṣṭam kuru sāṃpratam mātaram bhrātaram dṛṣṭvā sakhīḥ sva jana bāndhavān bhīṣmaḥ satyam eva agratas kṛtvā śīghram āgamanam kuru evam sā pratyayam kṛtvā bahulā satya vādinī anujñātā mṛgendreṇa prayātā putra vatsalā aśru pūrṇa mukhī dīnā vepamānā su duḥkhitā kurarī niḥśvasantī iva patitā śoka sāgare kariṇī iva hi nāgena gṛhītā salilāśaye aśakta ātma paritrāṇe vilapantī muhur muhur sā tu gopa kulam gatvā bahulā hṛta vikramā śrutvā ātma vatsam krośantam paryadhāvata tad mukhī upāsarpata tam vatsam bāṣpa paryākula īkṣaṇā sa prāptām mātaram vatsaḥ śaṅkitaḥ paripṛcchati bahulā udvignā ca api te dṛṣṭiḥ bhīta bhītā ca dṛśyate piba putra stanam mahyam kāraṇam yadi pṛcchasi āgatā ca tava snehāt kuru tṛptim yathā īpsitām apaścimam idam vatsa durlabham mātṛ darśanam eka aham atha mām pītvā pratyūṣe kasya pāsyasi kāmarūpasya vyāghrasya dātavyam jīvitam mayā bahulāyāḥ vacaḥ śrutvā vatsaḥ vacanam abravīt ślāghyam mama api maraṇam tvayā saha na saṃśayaḥ ekākinā api martavyam mayā avaśyam tvayā vinā yadi mām sahitam mātar vane vyāghraḥ haniṣyati yā gatiḥ mātṛ bhaktānām dhruvam sā me bhaviṣyati tasmāt avaśyam yāsyāmi tvayā saha na saṃśayaḥ atha mātar ca tiṣṭha tvam śapathāḥ santu te mama na asti mātṛ samaḥ bandhuḥ bālānām kṣīra pāyinām na asti mātṛ samaḥ nāthaḥ na asti mātṛ samā gatiḥ na asti mātṛ samaḥ snehaḥ na asti mātṛ samam sukham na ca ayam anya jīvānām mṛtyuḥ syāt anya mṛtyunā apaścimam imam putra mātuḥ saṃdeśam uttamam anutiṣṭhasva mad vākyam tatra śuśrūṣaṇam param pramādāt sarva bhūtāni vinaśyanti na saṃśayaḥ lobhāt vināśaḥ sarveṣām iha loke paratra ca samudram aṭavīm yuddham viśantaḥ lobha mohitāḥ ātmā ca satatam putra rakṣitavyaḥ prayatnataḥ sarpebhyaḥ śvāpadebhyaḥ ca mleccha cora ādi saṃkaṭe tiraścām pāpa jātīnām ekatra vasatām api nadīnām nakhinām ca eva śṛṅgiṇām śastra pāṇinām viśvāsaḥ nā eva kartavyaḥ strīṇām veśyā janasya ca viśvāsāt bhayam utpannam mūlāni api nikṛntati gāvaḥ paśyanti gandhena vedaiḥ paśyanti brāhmaṇāḥ cāraiḥ paśyanti rājānaḥ cakṣurbhyām itare janāḥ yathā hi pathikaḥ kaḥ cit chāyām āśritya viśramet vatsa antar śokam utsṛjya mad vākyam anupālaya śokena mahatā āviṣṭā bāṣpa vyākula locanā viniḥśvasantī mārgī iva vyāghrāṇām vaśam āgatā mahā paṅka vinirmagnā tiṣṭhantī eva atra sīdati na asti putra samā prītiḥ na asti putra samā gatiḥ anṛtam vadate lokaḥ candanam kila śītalam putra gātra pariṣvaṅgaḥ candanāt api śītalaḥ digambaram gata vrīḍam jaṭilam dhūli dhūsaram kim candanena pīyūṣa bindunā kim kim indunā putra gātra pariṣvaṅga pātram gātram bhavet yadi iti putra guṇān uktvā nirīkṣya ca muhur muhur sva mātaram sakhīḥ gopān kramāt tān anupṛcchati yūthasya agre carantīm mām āsasāda mṛgādhipaḥ muktā aham tena śapathaiḥ kulam dṛṣṭvā āgamiṣyasi sā aham bhavantam saṃdraṣṭum mātaram sva kulam sutam mātar kṣamasva tat sarvam bāla tve yat kṛtam mayā ajñānāt jñānataḥ vā api yat uktam kva cit apriyam tat kṣamadhvam mahābhāgāḥ paścāt yat ca kṛtam mayā sarvāḥ sarva guṇa upetāḥ sarva lokasya mātaraḥ sarvāḥ sarva pradāḥ nityam rakṣadhvam mama bālakam anātham abalam dīnam sīdantam mama putrakam mātṛ śoka abhisaṃtaptam bhaginyaḥ pālayiṣyatha bhaginīnām ayam putraḥ sāṃpratam ca viśeṣataḥ bālaḥ abalābhiḥ sarvābhiḥ poṣyaḥ pālyaḥ ca putra vat para putra ātma putreṣu yāsām cittam samam bhavet tāḥ dhanyāḥ tāḥ kṛtārthāḥ ca tāḥ striyaḥ tāḥ ca mātaraḥ modante deva lokeṣu tāḥ gāvaḥ tāḥ ca yoṣitaḥ yāḥ pālayanti anāthān ca para putrān ātma putra vat rakṣadhvam ca mahābhāgāḥ yāsyāmi satya saṃśrayāt śrutvā etat bahulā vākyam mātā sakhyaḥ ca vihvalāḥ viṣādam paramam jagmuḥ idam ūcuḥ su vismitāḥ praveṣṭum udyatā bhīmam bahulā satya vākyataḥ atra gāthāḥ purā gītāḥ ṛṣibhiḥ tattva darśibhiḥ uktvā anṛtam bhavet yatra prāṇinām prāṇa rakṣaṇam anṛtam tatra vai satyam satyam ca eva anṛtam bhavet kāminīṣu vivāheṣu gavām kārye tathā eva ca bahulā pareṣām prāṇa rakṣā artham vadāmi eva anṛtam vacaḥ bhuṅkte ca ekaḥ sukham duḥkham mātar satyam vadāmi aham udadhiḥ satya vākyena maryādām na vilaṅghati viṣṇave pṛthivīm dattvā baliḥ pātālam āśritaḥ svarga apavarga narakāḥ sarve vāci pratiṣṭhitāḥ yaḥ tām lopayate vācam aśeṣam tena lopitam yaḥ anyathā santam ātmānam anyathā satsu bhāṣate kim tena na kṛtam pāpam coreṇa ātma apahāriṇā yāsyāmi narakam ghoram vilopya ātmānam ātmanā snātvā pāpa vinirmuktaḥ prayāti paramām gatim satyam sādhu tapaḥ śrutam ca paramam kleśa ādibhiḥ varjitam svādhīnam ca su durlabham ca jagataḥ sādhāraṇam bhūṣaṇam sādhūnām nikaṣam satām kula dhanam sarva āśramāṇām balam mā tvam parama satyena prāṇān tyajasi dustyajān brūmaḥ kim atra kalyāṇi yā tvam dharma dhuraṃdharā adhiyogam ca paśyāmaḥ tyāgāt asmāt sutena te na hi kalyāṇa cittānām āpadaḥ santi kutra cit dṛṣṭvā gopāñjanam sarvam parikramya ca gokulam bahulā prasthitā devān vṛkṣān ca āpṛcchya tān punar kṣitim ca jalam agnim ca vāyum kha arka niśākaram daśa diś devatām adrīn nakṣatrāṇi grahān tathā karuṇā hita cittānām na syāt para janaḥ yatas tasmāt karuṇayā bālam anātham pālayiṣyatha ye saṃśritāḥ vane siddhāḥ sarvāḥ ca vana devatāḥ campaka aśoka puṃnāga sarala arjuna kiṃśukāḥ śṛṇvantu pādapāḥ sarve saṃdeśam mama viklavam bālam ekākinam dīnam carantam viṣame vane mātā pitṛ vinirmuktam anātham dīnam āturam vicarantam imām bhūmim krandamānam su duḥkhitam mātṛ śoka abhibhūtasya kṣudh pipāsā āturasya ca nyastasya ekākinaḥ śūnyam jagat sarvam prapaśyataḥ caramāṇasya kartavyam sa anukrośasya rakṣaṇam evam saṃdiśya bahulā putra sneha vaśam gatā śoka agninā ati saṃtaptā chinna āśā putra darśane gatvā tu sā punar tatra yatra asau piśita aśanaḥ āste visphūrjita mukhaḥ tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭraḥ bhayānakaḥ vyāghram dṛṣṭvā tu sā dhenuḥ idam vacanam abravīt āgatā aham mahābhāga satya dharma vyavasthayā kuru tṛptim yathākāmam mama māṃsena sāṃpratam tarpayasva sva bhūtāni pañca asya śoṇitena me vyāghraḥ tvayā uktam bahule pūrvam satyāt pratyāgatā punar tena naḥ kautukam jātam prāptā āgacchet katham punar anyathā mām samāsādya jīvantī yāsyase katham tat ca naḥ kautukam pūrṇam dṛṣṭam satyam gavām mayā satye pratiṣṭhitāḥ lokāḥ dharmaḥ satye pratiṣṭhitaḥ satye pratiṣṭhitam jñānam sarvam satye pratiṣṭhitam satyena ṛṣayaḥ siddhāḥ sarvam satye pratiṣṭhitam dhanyaḥ sa rāṣṭraḥ sa tṛṇaḥ prāṇi vṛkṣa samanvitaḥ cacāra bahulāḥ yatra diṣṭyā pāpa vināśinī te dhanyāḥ te kṛtārthāḥ ca taiḥ eva sukṛtam kṛtam taiḥ prāptam janmanaḥ sāram ye pibanti gavām payaḥ mṛgendraḥ pratyayam gatvā vismayam paramam gataḥ pratyādeśaḥ ayam asmākam sarvaiḥ devaiḥ pradarśitaḥ tat kariṣyāmi aham karma yena mokṣyāmi kilbiṣāt mayā jīva sahasrāṇi bhakṣitāni hatāni ca gatim kām nu gamiṣyāmi dṛṣṭvā go satyam īdṛśam aham pāpī durācāraḥ nṛśaṃsaḥ jīva ghātakaḥ kān lokān hi gamiṣyāmi kṛtvā karma su dāruṇam gamiṣye puṇya tīrthāni kariṣye kāya śodhanam patiṣye girim āsādya viśe vā api hutāśanam bahule yat mayā kāryam tapaḥ pāpa viśuddhaye bahulā tat ādeśaya saṃkṣepāt na kālaḥ vistarasya me tapaḥ kṛte praśaṃsanti tretāyām yajña karma ca abhayam sarva bhūtānām na asti dānam atas param cara acarāṇām bhūtānām abhayam yaḥ prayacchati na asti ahiṃsā param dānam na asti ahiṃsā paraḥ damaḥ yogavṛkṣasya yā chāyā tāpa traya vināśinī duḥkha traya arka saṃtaptaḥ chāyām yogataroḥ śritaḥ iti evam paramam śreyaḥ kīrtitam te samāsatas vyāghraḥ jñātam ca etat tvayā sarvam kevalam mām hi pṛcchasi aham devaiḥ purā sarvaiḥ vyāghra rūpeṇa pātitaḥ tatra prāṇi vadhe ghore aśeṣam mama vismṛtam tvad saṃparka upadeśena saṃjātam smaraṇam punar mukta śāpaḥ ca saṃjātaḥ prasādāt tava dhenuke prāṇāyāmaiḥ svakam deham parityajya divam gataḥ vimāne haṃsa saṃyukte mahatā apsaraḥ gaṇaiḥ vimāna saṃsthitam dṛṣṭvā vismayam paramam gataḥ bahulā hṛṣṭa tuṣṭa aṅgī gokulam punar āgatā āgatya ca yathāvṛttam vyāghra saṃvādam uttamam sakhībhyaḥ kathayām āsa putrasya ca viśeṣataḥ bahulā putra saṃyuktā su prītā su nirākulā vicacāra aṭavīm sarvām aśeṣa bhaya varjitā yatheṣṭa ceṣṭam abhavat gokulam tad prabhāvataḥ sa rāṣṭra bhṛtya rājānam sa antaḥpura paricchadam sarvān gopān ca tān dṛṣṭvā gopī go maṇḍalāni ca iti saṃgṛhya tān sarvān bahulā prasthitā divam mahā vimānaiḥ śrīmadbhiḥ asaṃkhyaiḥ sarvatas vṛtam prāpya svargam kramāt ūrdhvam aśeṣaiḥ sahitā punar upari upari lokānām gatvā golokam āsthitā tatra saṃbhoga saṃkīrṇe asaṃkhyātaiḥ pura uttamaiḥ bhīṣmaḥ krīḍate sahitā sarvaiḥ yāvat ābhūtasaṃplavam iti etat satya vākyasya māhābhāgyam udāhṛtam yaḥ paṭhet śṛṇuyāt vā api svarga lokam sa gacchati bahulā vyāghra saṃvādam yaḥ paṭhet satatam dvijaḥ śṛṇuyāt ekacittaḥ ca sa vyāghraiḥ na abhibhūyate viyuktā yā sutaiḥ nārī naraḥ vā api paṭhet sadā yaḥ paṭhet prātar utthāya caturdaśī aṣṭamīṣu ca satām puṇya kṛtām lokān gacchate na atra saṃśayaḥ gavām goṣṭhe ca paṭhatām go vṛddhiḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ kṣetra madhye ca paṭhatām sasya vṛddhiḥ prajāyate gṛha madhye ca paṭhatām bālānām śānti vardhanam bhavet graha abhibhūtānām rakṣām etām ca yaḥ paṭhet durgeṣu paṭhatām nityam aṭavī āyataneṣu ca duḥsvapna ariṣṭa durmitram śrutvā idam saṃpraṇaśyati kumārī ca sadā śṛṇvan vīram patim avāpnuyāt nārī vā puruṣaḥ vā api durbhagaḥ subhagaḥ bhavet gavām parvasu bhaktyā ca māhātmyam puratas paṭhet śūdraḥ aham na adhikāraḥ me cāturāśramya sevane viśeṣeṇa ca vakṣyāmi cāturvarṇyasya liṅgataḥ pañcabhūta śarīrāṇām sarveṣām sadṛśa ātmanām sarveṣām tulya dehānām sarveṣām sadṛśa ātmanām kālaḥ dharmeṇa saṃyuktaḥ śeṣaḥ eva su saṃgrahaḥ evam sati na doṣaḥ asti bhūtānām dharma sevane pālaya tvam prajāḥ sarvāḥ śānta ātmā ca anuśāsitaḥ dvaipāyanaḥ tu ayam cakṣuḥ kṛṣṇaḥ te astu parāyaṇam iti uktvā upāsana arthāya virarāma mahāmatiḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca śara talpa gatam bhīṣmam pāṇḍavaḥ atha kuru udvahaḥ tasmai śuśrūṣamāṇāya bhūyas śāṃtanavaḥ tadā deva vaṃśam yathānyāyam ācaṣṭa puruṣa ṛṣabha yudhiṣṭhiraḥ sarva śānti karam japyam dāridrya ātura duḥkhinām tuṣṭi puṣṭi pradam nityam pitāmaha vadasva me bhīṣmaḥ ayam śastaḥ mahā japyaḥ deva rāja ṛṣi saṃyutaḥ buddhi pūrvam abuddhiḥ vā rātrau yaḥ ca api saṃdhyayoḥ viṣṇoḥ nābhi saraḥ jātaḥ brahmā bhuvana pāvanaḥ anantaḥ nāga rājaḥ ca vāsukiḥ takṣakaḥ tathā carmaṇvatī nadī puṇyā kauśikī yamunā tathā etāḥ pāpa harāḥ sarvāḥ devanadyaḥ prakīrtitāḥ rājā rathaṃtaraḥ ca eva bhārgavaḥ cyavanaḥ tathā udarkaḥ ca eva māndhātā tathā evā uḍaḥ mahodayaḥ kuruḥ saṃvaraṇaḥ ca eva māndhātā satya vikramaḥ ādi rājaḥ pṛthuḥ vainyo pṛthuḥ tathā uparicaraḥ kekayaḥ ca mahā yaśāḥ pṛṣadhruḥ ca pratīpaḥ ca śaṃtanuḥ ca tathā nṛpaḥ havidhraḥ ca ajaḥ ca eva rantidevaḥ purūravāḥ ajaḥ prācīnabarhiḥ ca tathā ikṣvākuḥ mahā yaśāḥ rambhodbhavaḥ ca rājarṣiḥ māndhātā rāja sattamaḥ devāḥ devarṣayaḥ ca eva stutāḥ rājarṣayaḥ tathā puṣṭim āyuḥ yaśaḥ svargam vidhāsyanti mama īśvarāḥ avikārāya śuddhāya nityāya paramātmane sadā eka rūpa rūpāya viṣṇave prabhaviṣṇave śara talpa gate bhīṣme kauravāṇām dhuraṃdhare śayāne vīra śayane pāṇḍavaiḥ samupasthite yudhiṣṭhiraḥ mahā prājñaḥ mama pūrva pitāmahaḥ dharmāṇām āgamam śrutvā viditvā dharma saṃśayān dānānām ca vidhim śrutvā chinna dharma artha saṃśayaḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca abhūt muhūrtam stimitam sarvam tat rāja maṇḍalam tatas ca puṇḍarīkākṣam āha dharma bhṛtām varaḥ bhagavan yoga yuktaḥ asi tūṣṇīm dhyāyasi śatru han brūhi mām puruṣa śreṣṭhaḥ ājñāpyaḥ bhavatā hi aham dhyāyasva puṇḍarīkākṣa śreyaḥ me paramam hitam śrī bhagavān śara talpa gataḥ bhīṣmaḥ śāmyan iva hutāśanaḥ mām dhyāti puruṣa vyāghraḥ tatas me tad gatam manaḥ bhrātṝn ca eva puraskṛtya mātaram ca pṛthām api dhyāyantam paramātmānam kṛṣṇa dharmam sanātanam mādhavena mahā rājaḥ kṛṣṇena akliṣṭa karmaṇā vāsudevaḥ hiraṇya ātmā puruṣaḥ savitā virāj jīva bhūtaḥ anurūpaḥ tvam parama ātmā sanātanaḥ trāyasva puṇḍarīkākṣa puruṣottama nityaśas duryodhanam durācāram kṣatriya anta karam raṇe suhṛdbhiḥ sahitaḥ rājan bhuṅkṣva rājyam akaṇṭakam sa mām tvam anujānīhi kṛṣṇa mokṣye kalevaram tvayā aham samanujñātaḥ gaccheyam paramām gatim dhanam bahuvidham rājan dattvā nityam dvijātiṣu deva duṃdubhi nādaḥ ca puṣpa varṣaiḥ saha abhavat siddhāḥ brahmarṣayaḥ ca eva sādhu sādhu iti harṣitāḥ candanānām ca khaṇḍāni mahārghāṇi bahūni ca yudhiṣṭhireṇa sahitau dhṛtarāṣṭraḥ tu pādataḥ sarve te dīna manasaḥ śocamānāḥ punar punar hasti aśva ratha yānaiḥ ca saṃprāptāḥ hastināpuram iti etat bahu vistāram ānuśāsanikam śubham yatra uktāḥ sarva dharmāḥ ca parvaṇi asmin trayodaśe śrutvā yān svastha tām āpa dharmaputraḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ bhīṣmāt bhāgīrathī putrāt sarva saṃdeha hāriṇaḥ adhyāyānām śatam ca atra ṣaṭcatvāriṃśat eva tu nava śloka sahasrāṇi śatāni aṣṭau tathā eva ca vyāsena udāra matinā dṛṣṭaḥ granthaḥ mahātmanā ānuśāsanikam parva śrutvā bhakti samanvitaḥ nārāyaṇam pūjayitvā bhaktyā paramayā tatas bhīṣma uddeśena kartavyam brāhmaṇānām ca tarpaṇam vyāsa rūpī hariḥ sākṣāt paśyatām iti kīrtayet dadyāt dhenu hiraṇya ādi vācakāya viśeṣataḥ sarva kāmān avāpnoti dīrgham āyuḥ ca vindati
